### `D-REFAV1-SEED-GOAL-MISMATCH` — ⛔ **BLOCKING, and prior to both fixes R28 proposed**

ℹ️ **Attribution: escalated qualitatively by the steer-conversion agent in commit `4139203`** (*"a 2 s plan is judged against a 6 s goal, so the planner's own seed cannot reproduce its own goal"*). This row is the MEASUREMENT of that residual, and it shows the same commit's `cost_time_grid` repair does not close it.

**MEASURED at source, 0 GPU, exact arithmetic on the shipped code.** `plan()` rolls the imagined
goal from the FULL 30-step canonical control subsampled onto the tactical grid — operative indices
`[0,3,6,9,12,15,18,21,24,27]` (`refa_v1.py:1761`) — but seeds the search with that control
TRUNCATED to `cfg.plan_steps = 10` (`refa_v1.py:1932`) ⇒ `[0..9]` under the DEFAULT
`cost_time_grid="dense"` — **the behaviour every BANKED refav1 number was produced under; the
parameter did not exist before `4139203`** — or `[0,3,6,9,9,…]` under that commit's new
`"tactical"` regrid. **62 of 64 (lat, lon) v7.0 token pairs hand the goal and the seed DIFFERENT
tactical action sequences UNDER BOTH GRIDS** (48/64 differ in curvature alone, under both), because
the cause is the TRUNCATION, not the regrid — ⚠️ **so `4139203`'s time-grid repair does NOT close
this residual.** The only two pairs that agree, `(LANE_KEEP, CRUISE)` and `(ABORT_LC, CRUISE)`,
are the all-zero control — *the one manoeuvre the planner can faithfully chase is doing nothing*,
which is the behaviour observed on 140/140 windows. At v0 = 10 m/s under the DEFAULT grid:
`TURN_L`/`TURN_R` over-rotate their own goal by **±82.506°** (ψ 3.360 → 4.800 rad); `LANE_CHANGE_R`
loses its return arc's sign and reads **−27.072°**; `NUDGE_R`'s S-curve is stretched **2.5× in
time** so **8 of 10** tactical steps carry the wrong curvature (its NET heading still matches, which
is why heading excess alone is not a sufficient gate); `LANE_KEEP × BRAKE_TO` differs on **9 of
10** accel steps and ends at **6.66×** the goal's final deceleration (−0.5811 vs −0.0872 m/s²).
⇒ The cost is not measuring the right pair imprecisely — **it is measuring the wrong pair.**

### `D-REFAV1-TAC-DECODER` — the lateral head IS the constant predictor, to the digit

**MEASURED, T0, 140 windows / 20 episodes, in-band n = 40.** The incumbent (fp32) step-1,000
checkpoint emits `LANE_KEEP` on **140/140** windows; its in-band accuracy **0.650** equals the
constant-only base rate **EXACTLY** and its macro-recall **0.200** equals **1/K = 1/5 EXACTLY**;
the clustered label-permutation null gives `p(macro ≥ observed) = 1.0000`. Per class: `LANE_KEEP`
1.000 (n 26), `NUDGE_L` 0.000 (6), `NUDGE_R` 0.000 (2), `TURN_L` 0.000 (2), `TURN_R` 0.000 (4).
The EMA+bf16 arm (ep2) emits `TURN_R` on 25/140, reaching accuracy 0.675 / macro 0.377
(clustered p = 0.0168) with `TURN_R` recall 1.000 — **but that is a nav echo**: under nav-shuffle
its macro falls to **0.177, BELOW the constant control**, and under nav-zero it returns to
`LANE_KEEP` 140/140. On the geometric turn stratum (`gt_turn_deg ≥ 5°`, n = 27/140, 10 episodes)
the incumbent asks for a turn on **0/27** (0.000 [0.000, 0.000]) and ep2 on **9/27**
(0.333 [0.000, 0.667]). ⚠️ Only 8 of those 27 are in-band and only 3 carry a `TURN_*` label — the
geometric stratum is not the v7.2 token. **Longitudinally the head is NOT collapsed** (accuracy
0.550 vs 0.450, macro 0.378 vs 0.200, clustered p = 0.0098) and its predictions are row-for-row
identical on both checkpoints. Evidence: `raw/decode_audit.json`, `RESULT.md` §6.

### `D-REFAV1-TAC-LABEL-SHARE` — ⚠️ **CORRECTS the R28 brief and inverts its lever ordering**

**MEASURED.** The R28 brief read the decoder's supervision as 0.1–0.8 % of the loss from
`loss_feat_tac`. That is the tactical **FIELD PREDICTOR's** MSE — a different term. The decoder's
own term is `w_tac_label · mean(CE_lat, CE_lon)` and on the 140 eval windows it is **0.11313
(incumbent) / 0.11695 (ep2)** against feature terms summing to **0.71918 / 0.45324** — i.e.
**13.59 % / 20.51 %** of the label+feature sum. `w_tac_label = 0.1` in both recorded configs and the
live ep2 run passed real `--labels`. ⇒ **"the head is unsupervised / the term is too weak" is
REFUTED, and raising `w_tac_label` is NOT the lever.** Two further reads settle the mechanism:
`CE_lat = 0.8665 nats` sits **BELOW** the in-band label entropy `H = 1.0944` (the head carries
0.228 nats beyond the marginal *while its argmax is constant*), and the head's own
`P(TURN_L)+P(TURN_R)` ranks the label at **AUC 0.873 [0.686, 1.000]** (ep2 0.922 [0.737, 1.000])
against a no-information 0.500. **The head ranks; it does not decide.** ⇒ the lever is the DECISION
RULE (class-balanced / focal CE), not the weight. Evidence: `raw/intent_probe_*.json`, `RESULT.md` §2.

### `D-REFAV1-TAC-NAV-ECHO` — every scrap of the tactical decision is oracle nav, not scene

**MEASURED, and it discharges the standing nav-shuffle obligation (`refa_v1.py:395`).** With
`nav_zero`, the incumbent's `CE_lat` rises 0.8665 → **1.3390** (ep2 0.9420 → 1.3684) — **above** the
marginal entropy 1.0944 — and its turn-ranking AUC collapses **0.873 → 0.520**. The longitudinal
head collapses to `CRUISE` 140/140 (accuracy 0.450 = the base rate, macro 0.200 = 1/K). A predictor
reading **nothing but `nav_cmd`** (leave-one-episode-out) scores **0.684** on the same 38 scorable
in-band rows — **above the model's 0.650 and above the constant control's 0.650.** ⚠️ `nav` is an
**ORACLE (ego-future)** derivation, training-input only. ⇒ The one signal the tactical head has
learned to read is the one that will not exist at deployment. ep2 alone retains a vision-borne
component (`nav_zero` label-TURN AUC **0.858 [0.667, 1.000]**, CI excluding 0.500).
Evidence: `raw/decode_audit.json`, `raw/intent_probe_*.json`, `RESULT.md` §6.

### `D-REFAV1-TAC-LABEL-SPARSITY` — the head sees ~2 labelled rows per batch-8 step

**MEASURED (this slice) / INFERRED (train corpus).** `s2_labels_v7.2_train.jsonl.gz` carries
**4,572 records**, one per clip, with `bands.tactical_s = [2.0, 6.0]` and **`t0_s ≡ 8.0` on
4,572/4,572** — so the label attaches only to windows whose NOW lies in `[6.0 s, 10.0 s]`
(`refav1_loader.py:443`). In the TRAINING loader shape (`str_ext_steps = 2`, reach 60) that is
**199 / 739 = 26.93 %** of windows (eval shape: 420 / 1,339 = 31.37 %, confirmed independently by
the loader's own banner). **8.13 %** of batch-8 steps carry NO labelled row and the term is
`continue`-skipped entirely. ⚠️ `F.cross_entropy` uses `reduction='mean'` over non-ignored rows, so
the term's magnitude does not shrink — its **sample size** does, to ~2.15 rows per step. **The
gradient is noisy, not weak; reading "27 % of rows" as "27 % less loss" picks the wrong lever.**
Label distribution: `LANE_KEEP` 2,958 (64.70 %), `NUDGE_R` 592, `NUDGE_L` 488, `TURN_L` 275,
`TURN_R` 259; **`LANE_CHANGE_L`, `LANE_CHANGE_R`, `ABORT_LC`, `YIELD_MERGE` = 0** — 5 of the head's
16 output units have zero support. Evidence: `raw/window_band_census.json`,
`raw/decode_audit.json` `label_census_train`, `RESULT.md` §§3–4.

### `D-REFAV1-CHORD-INSUFFICIENT` — ⚠️ **CORRECTS BACKLOG R29's framing**

**MEASURED, 0 GPU, on ep2's 25 already-curved-goal windows.** `chord = √(2(1−cos))` is strictly
monotone in `1−cos`, so it cannot change the goal term's own ordering; what it changes is that
term's **leverage inside the cost SUM**, by a measured **median 5,792.6× (conv A) / 4,096.0×
(conv B)**. That flips **1 of 25** windows under convention A and **0 of 25** under B. As shipped
the turn wins **0/25** under both. The deeper reason: on **17/25** (A) and **21/25** (B) windows the
canonical turn's goal term is **no better than constant velocity** — the direction is wrong, not
only the magnitude (see `D-REFAV1-SEED-GOAL-MISMATCH` for why). The goal term's κ-range is
**1.63e-10 (f64)** against the `0.05·κ²` penalty's **2.00e-03** — a factor **1.2 × 10⁷**, of which
the chord's √ recovers ~2 × 10³. ⚠️ And because the cost is a SUM, swapping the metric while holding
`0.05` fixed is **not a one-variable arm**: it is a metric change *and* an implicit 5,793×
reweighting. ⚠️ **SCOPE: the banked panel predates the steer-conversion repair** (`4139203`, same
day), whose tiny-model evidence puts the units-FIXED turn advantage at **+5.364e-07** (vs
**−2.384e-07** half-applied). Chord-transformed, that value would clear the 3.200e-04 charge by
**2.33×** — but on a TINY MODEL, not these checkpoints. The two readings disagree and only the
0-GPU E0 arm on the real checkpoints settles it. ⚠️ The κ² penalty is also not the only charge — the `0.02·jerk²` term from the
canonical control's own longitudinal step has mean **1.695e-04** and max **3.218e-03**, exceeding
the curvature charge on 2/25 windows. ⇒ **R29 is NECESSARY but far from SUFFICIENT and must be
declared jointly with the goal:penalty balance.** Evidence: `raw/turn_decomposition_{A,B}.json`,
`RESULT.md` §9.

### `D-REFAV1-TAC-LOSS-UNINSTRUMENTED` — the mandated term has no instrument

**MEASURED by CONTENT.** `refa_v1_train.py:699-734` writes `loss`, `loss_feat_op`, `loss_feat_tac`,
`loss_feat_str`, `grad_norm`, `adapter_std`, `participation`, `loss_sigreg`, `loss_varfloor`,
`tgt_std_{op,tac,str}`, `ema_decay`, `clip`, `skipped_steps`, `tac_target_s`, `str_target_s`,
`elapsed_s` — and **no `loss_lat_label`, `loss_lon_label`, `loss_route_label` or
`loss_feat_str_ext`**. Every row of all **nine** banked refav1 `train_log.jsonl` artifacts was
parsed: **0 rows** carry any of them. Consequence: the residual
`loss − (1.0·op + 0.5·tac + 0.25·str)` in any banked refav1 log conflates three terms and cannot be
attributed, which is why `D-REFAV1-TAC-LABEL-SHARE` had to be recomputed from checkpoints. ⇒ The one
term the PI made mandatory (2026-08-31, *"It must be trained with this data"*) is the one term with
no telemetry. **Four lines; a preflight condition for every arm in `PREREG_TACTICAL_DECODER.md`.**
Evidence: `RESULT.md` §7.

| **`H-ARCH-EGOZERO-1`** | refcv3 masks the ego speed to 0.0 on 50 % of training samples (`ego_dropout`) while `ego_valid_channel = False` withholds the companion bit, so a WITHHELD speed and a GENUINE 0.0 m/s standstill are byte-identical at the measurement encoder — MEASURED: 4.4531 % of the 781,635 train windows are exactly 0.0, so 52.227 % of samples present a zero of which only 4.263 % are real (22.5 : 1), and the token means something 23.5× different at eval where dropout is off. Feeding the bit that the model ALREADY computes and ALREADY passes to the S2 band (`ego_keep`) will improve the LONGITUDINAL family on the low-speed stratum with a 95 % paired episode-cluster CI excluding zero, and will NOT move the lateral family. | **OPEN** — pre-registered 2026-09-03, no arm spent; SPEC + measurements in `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-03-ego-zero-collision/` |

A second `as_command` call would have left the same shape of defect one edit away; the register records that reasoning in the function's own docstring. `_imagine_tactical_goal(units=)` and `imagined_goal(model_action_units=)` are keyword-only with legacy defaults, so `cost_surface_probe.py:243`'s 3-positional call is untouched. **The trap reproduced in miniature** (tiny model, decode pinned to `TURN_R`, `raw/evidence.json`): the turn's advantage over the straight line reads **0.0** (κ on both sides) → **−2.38e-07** (the shipped half-conversion) → **+5.36e-07** (fixed) — the same sign pattern as the 0 → −5.96e-08 measured on real windows. ⚠️ **This buys the planner NOTHING measurable and is not sold as a win**: `D-REFAV1-BOUNDARY-NULL`'s `share = +0.0000 [0, 0]` stands; a correctness trap was removed. **TIME GRID (R27):** `plan(cost_time_grid=)` with `"dense"` the byte-identical default and `"tactical"` = `[0,3,6,9,9,9,9,9,9,9]` against today's `[0..9]` and the goal's `[0,3,…,27]`. ⭐ A source fact stronger than the original finding: **`forward()` builds `tac_a = acts_in[:, ::stride]` (`refa_v1.py:1270`), so the dense feed is OUT OF THE TRAINING DISTRIBUTION**, not merely off-clock. ⭐ **And the banked-panel question is answerable MODEL-FREE, exactly**: the banked config has `speed_channel: false`, so the regrid is the IDENTITY on any candidate that is constant over the plan horizon — and across all 20 episodes / 140 windows / 3 arms of `t1_dump/decisions/` **the winner is constant on 140/140 in every arm** (`cl` all-zero on 140/140) ⇒ every banked winner's cost is bit-identical under the corrected grid, so `D-REFAV1-COST-SURFACE` needs no re-read. Every statement in these rows is a DIRECTION. The fix is forward-looking only: the in-training eval must dump per-window values with episode ids, or its numbers can never carry an interval. **(6) A conflict reported rather than reconciled**: `C-REFCV3-EVAL-PRIOR-LEAK` says 34 leaked evals; this file measures 40 — and since it begins at step 550, neither count covers the whole run. **(7) Enabling check passed**: `eval_windows` 160 and four other stamps each take exactly ONE distinct value across all 55 evals and all 10 launches, so the window set really is fixed and the two controls read their known value in every era. Engineering: ≈ 4.3 h lost to six deaths, three switches and nine restarts; uint8 measured at **−13.4 %** s/step over 143 rows, cross-checked by two independent clocks agreeing to < 1 %.

Status stays **PROPOSED** until the Master Mind/PI reads them. Apply verbatim or amend.

### `D-HF-COMPARABILITY`

> ⛔⛔ **D-HF-COMPARABILITY — refav1 AND refcv3 SHARE A CORPUS AND SHARE ALMOST NOTHING ELSE: THE
> DOMINANCE CLAIM'S BLOCKER IS A DEFINITION, NOT COMPUTE (2026-09-03, Benchmarks & Evals FlyWheel;
> `Benchmarks & Evals/Research/2026-09-03-refcv3-epoch-conclusions/RESULT.md` §5).** Same 4,572-clip
> train split and same 141-clip eval split — and that similarity has been carrying far more
> rhetorical weight than it can. **refav1 is a latent WM + iCEM planner whose predictor is
> action-conditioned; refcv3 is a SUPERVISED ONE-SHOT ANCHOR TRAJECTORY MODEL (128 FPS anchors × 8
> slots) with NO action input, NO rollout and NO per-step decode.** ⇒ `t1_eval.roll_closed`
> (`t1_eval.py:753–786`) — which DOES handle a supervised model, the flagship, by decoding
> `(a_j, yaw_j)` from a `UnicycleStepReadout` head at each step and feeding
> `(steer = atan(L·κ), a_j)` back to the predictor with perception frozen at t0 — **cannot be ported
> to refcv3, because there is no action to feed back.** The flagship is supervised *and*
> autoregressive; refcv3 is supervised and one-shot, and that is the difference that matters.
> **WHAT IS COMMON:** `ha0` (constant velocity at the measured `v0`) is **identically defined and
> bit-comparable** on the shared windows — the only such arm — and `ha` is common as a control that
> can be worse than trivial. **WHAT IS NOT:** `ol` does not exist for refcv3 (it consumes no recorded
> actions); `cl` is not the same procedure on both sides and must not share a name.
> **INADMISSIBLE if any of these holds:** (1) different window grids, unasserted; (2) refcv3's
> **oracle-anchor-selected** `traj` quoted against refav1's `cl` (the C6-confound family: the eval's
> own target selects the thing being scored); (3) a cross-tier comparison, or a `cl`-to-`cl`
> comparison of two different procedures; (4) either arm degenerate on the trivial profile — then the
> read is **VOID, not negative** (`D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID`); (5) any refcv3 arm run through the
> rescued adapter's `recorded_controls` without the steer→curvature fix (`C-REFCV3-ARM-SAME-DEFECT`;
> the identical defect cost refav1 **0.716 m** lateral against a **0.053 m** floor); (6) no shared
> floor — both arms must report against `ha0` **on the same windows**. **THE ADMISSIBLE CLAIM is
> therefore not "F beats H" but the DIFFERENCE OF EACH ARM'S MARGIN OVER THE SAME TRIVIAL FLOOR,
> PER FAMILY, with a paired episode-cluster bootstrap over the shared `eid` set** — `cl − ha0` for
> refav1 against `os − ha0` for refcv3, never `cl` against `os` as levels. Two metrics are **not
> defined for both** and must be recomputed or quarantined: refcv3's `traj` is a mean **L1 per
> coordinate**, not an L2 ADE (recompute from the dumped path; never convert), and `law` consumes a
> **future frame's** latent (T0 WM diagnostic only, no refav1 analogue). `route` is admissible for
> neither arm without the `cl_navshuf` control beside it — nav is an INPUT, and the flagship v1 route
> head scored **1.0000** by echoing it. ⚠️ **THE OPEN DECISION (PI / Master Mind, not a FlyWheel):
> does the doctrine admit as T1 a model that consumes NO actions at all?** Its T1 text says *"the
> predictor consumes the decoder/planner's own actions"*, which does not literally cover refcv3.
> Benchmarks' RECOMMENDATION, flagged as such: **admit it** — the doctrine's purpose is to keep
> future information out of inference, and a correctly-gated refcv3 forward pass (frames ≤ t0, nav,
> measured `v0`, anchor chosen by `sel_score_v3` and NOT by `a_star`) admits none — but give it a
> **distinct arm name (`os`, never `cl`)** so no reader believes the two `cl` columns describe the
> same procedure. **This ruling, not GPU, is what register decision 9 is waiting on.**

⛔⛔ **D-REFAV1-COST-SURFACE — THE MASTER MIND'S THREE-FACTOR ORDERING IS REFUTED BY THE PROBE IT COMMISSIONED: THE ×2.9 CONVERSION CHANGES **NOTHING**, THE COST IS 99.5 % ITS OWN κ² PENALTY, AND THE REAL LEVERS ARE A DEGENERATE GOAL AND A float32-SATURATED COSINE (2026-09-03, ArchInf FlyWheel, BACKLOG R10; 33 tests re-run green by the Master Mind; the rows below are the FlyWheel's PROPOSED text, applied VERBATIM).** I wrote that the flat plan was three compounding factors — intrinsic lateral potency, the ×2.9 boundary under-actuation, and the `0.05·κ²` penalty charged at full κ — and commissioned this probe to apportion them. **The apportionment came back and the ordering is wrong.** ⭐ **The decisive read**: on the clean epoch's 25 windows where the tactical decoder actually asks for `TURN_R` — the only population in this corpus where the cost has a lateral question at all — the canonical turn that `plan()` itself injects as a seed scores `1−cos` **5.96e-08 (ONE ULP)** against the straight line's **0.0**, and pays **3.2000e-04** in the penalty: total 3.2006e-04, beating `cv` on **0 of 25**. Rolled 10 steps under the very turn profile that BUILT the goal, the tactical predictor produces a terminal field float32 cannot distinguish from the zero-action one. **What the correct turn buys in the model is exactly 0; what it pays is 3.2e-04.** ⛔ **Factor (ii) is ZERO, with a zero-width interval**: `share(ii) = +0.0000 [0.0000, 0.0000]` on every stratum of BOTH checkpoints (`p_delta_gt0 = 0.0000`, 10,000 paired episode-cluster resamples). The conversion DOES multiply the imagined lateral displacement by **2.935×**, exactly as predicted — "a factor of 9 on a quantity 2×10⁶ times too small". **So the steer/curvature repair, which I championed and which the PI's ruling correctly sited, buys the planner nothing.** It remains right as a CONTRACT (the geometry was genuinely over-rotating by 2.9×, D-STEER-INTERFACE-RESOLVED) and it is now measured to be irrelevant to the flat plan. Factor (iii) is real but is nearly the WHOLE cost rather than a third of it: the penalty is **99.5 %** of the total variation over the candidate box (2.000e-03 of 2.010e-03), while the world model contributes 1.00e-05 along `a` and **1.63e-10** along κ; the shipped argmin is (a = 0, κ = 0) on 139/140 and the origin is the GLOBAL minimum on 140/140. ⭐ **The two factors that dominate were not in my list at all:** **(0) THE GOAL IS DEGENERATE** — it IS the constant-velocity rollout on 129/140 (incumbent) / 110/140 (ep2), the incumbent decodes `LANE_KEEP` on **140/140** (zero curvature everywhere) and asks for a turn on **0 of the 27** windows where the human turns (recall 0.000; ep2 reaches 0.333) — so the flat plan is the EXACT global minimum by construction, confirmed by a second model-free probe; and **(iv) float32 SATURATION** — `1−cos` spans a median of **2.00 ULPs along κ** against 167.5 / 19,804 along `a`, and read off the banked dumps with no model the shipped winning cost takes **six** distinct values over 140 windows, some of them negative. ⇒ **The lever is the TACTICAL DECODER and it is a TRAINING lever, not a planner tweak** (repair R4): a planner cannot search for a turn it is never asked to make, and cannot rank one it cannot represent. ⛔ **URGENT, and it is a trap I shipped: the κ→steer repair is INCOMPLETE.** `as_command` is called at EXACTLY ONE site (`refa_v1.py:1832`, inside `_cost_chunk` — verified by the Master Mind, one grep hit), so `model_action_units="steer"` converts the CANDIDATE while `_imagine_tactical_goal` (`:1680`) still rolls the GOAL in raw κ; measured, the converted turn lands ONE ULP FURTHER from its own goal (advantage 0 → −5.96e-08). Default-OFF so nothing banked is affected — **but the flag must convert both sides or neither** (BACKLOG R26, and it blocks R21's cut-over). Two further defects found in passing: **a units error in TIME** — `_cost_chunk` consumes the 2.0 s plan on the 0.6 s tactical clock, imagining 6.0 s (BACKLOG R27) — and **T4 is DEAD CODE** (`target_speed` is never passed), so the cost has THREE live terms, not the four my brief asserted from recollection; the brief flagged that as recollection and the agent checked it. ⚠️ `PANEL_VOID = True` is stamped honestly: control C3 failed its pre-registered EXACTLY-0.0 form (2.00 / 4.00 ULPs) and that failure IS the noise-floor measurement; its two κ-axis sub-assertions passed and the decisive reading above uses no argmin at all. Tier T0 throughout — no driving claim without the T1 adapter.

## Consequences for rows already in the register

* **`D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID` now has a mechanism, and it is ARITHMETIC, not
  representational.** The flat plan is not evidence about the world model; it is the exact
  minimum of a cost that is 99.5 % curvature penalty and whose model term is at float32's
  resolution laterally. ⭐ **And its point (5) is independently corroborated on a
  cost-side instrument: the two checkpoints whose deployed trajectories are byte-identical
  differ 117× in the cost's own dynamic range** (goal-term span 171 → 19,961 float32 ULPs;
  36 → 164 distinct values over 189 candidates).
* **`D-ACTDIV-ANCHORED-REFAV1` stands.** ⚠️ The 0.42 % (incumbent) / 0.70 % (clean epoch)
  lateral/longitudinal terminal-field displacement ratio measured here is **NOT a
  replication** of that row's 0.329 % / 2.514 % and must not be quoted as one: different
  horizon (tactical h = 10 vs h = 1), different normalisation (the planner's
  ±`kappa_max` box vs matched ≈2σ corpus levels), different population.
* **`C-STEER-CURVATURE-INTERFACE`'s mechanism is confirmed at ×2.935 and its repair is
  measured to be insufficient in the planner — and incomplete as implemented.** Its "the
  cost surface goes flat in κ" is correct; the reason is not only the under-actuation but
  that the κ direction of the cost was never above the arithmetic's floor to begin with.
* **The Master Mind's "the next probe is the COST SURFACE ITSELF (`_cost_chunk`'s four
  terms)" is executed — and the count was wrong: THREE terms are live.** `target_speed` is
  never passed by the T1 adapter, so T4 (`0.10·(v_end − target_speed)²`) is dead code in
  every banked read, and the tactical brain's longitudinal decision reaches the cost only
  through T1.

✅✅ **D-V7-DINO-SEED — THE PI's "DINO AS INIT" IS WIRED END TO END AND REFUSES A PARTIAL SEED; AND THE PRE-REGISTRATION'S OWN LAUNCH LINE WOULD HAVE FROZEN THE TRUNK (2026-09-03, ArchInf FlyWheel, 0 GPU; 40 new CPU tests, selection **5 F / 1,265 P / 24 S** against a **5 F / 1,225 P / 24 S** baseline — the same five pre-existing failures by name, 0 new; BACKLOG R15 closed).** Three artifacts close the gap D-PREREG-V7F measured: `stack/scripts/dinov3_seed_checkpoint.py` converts a LOCAL DINOv3 snapshot (never downloads) into an encoder-only seed carrying a provenance stamp (source path + sha256, model id, the full name-mapping table, mapped / skipped / left-at-init counts, target geometry); `--init-encoder-from` (alias `--enc-init-from`) loads it while the whole-stack `--init-from` path stays byte-identical — a SECOND DOOR rather than a looser first one, because `load_stage_init` exists precisely to refuse a partial checkpoint; and `--trunk-lr-scale` / `--trunk-lr-warmup-steps` split the flat AdamW into a trunk group (`group_of == "encoder"`) and the rest. **Verified against REAL weights** (the on-box ViT-L/16, sha256 `dcb2e451…`, 415 tensors): 292/293 targets mapped, 99.78 % of parameters seeded, loaded into a real `V6Stack` at 256×640 with a finite forward and ZERO non-encoder tensors touched. The mapping is exact — qkv row-concat, a zero fill for DINOv3's absent key bias, and LayerScale folded into `attn.out_proj` and `mlp.2` (algebraically exact: each branch ends in a linear map) — and it is pinned by a **from-scratch DINOv3 reference forward with a negative control that must fail**, not by a key count. ⛔ **THE DEFECT THAT MATTERS: `PREREG_V7F.md` §9 said `--stage S-S`, which trains ONLY `layer_str`** (`v6.py` `STAGE_GROUPS["S-S"] = ("layer_str",)`; executed by the Master Mind: S-W → encoder TRUE, S-T / S-S → FALSE, S-J → TRUE) — **a DINOv3-seeded run at S-S would have been a FROZEN-TRUNK run wearing a trainable-trunk launch line, the exact opposite of the PI's directive**, and `--stage S-S` appears nowhere else in the repo (every proven v7-family line uses `S-W`). Corrected in place, with the reason, plus two more: `--vit5-encoder --n-registers 4` contradicted the prereg's OWN D1 (RMSNorm has no bias and qkv/proj are bias-free, so DINOv3's LayerNorm and q/v biases would be silently dropped — the converter now refuses `--target vit5` and the loader refuses the class), and `--param-budget` takes RAW PARAMS so D5's shorthand `300` refuses instantly. A startup guard turns the stage defect into a refusal instead of a silent frozen trunk. ⛔ **`--w-trunk-anchor` IS DECLARED BUT NOT WIRED and refuses loudly at any non-zero value** — O7's teacher CANNOT be reused, for three independent reasons each sufficient: it is ViT-L/16 not the seed's own ViT-B/16 (anchoring to a different network is not an anchor), it returns pooled READOUT CELLS not per-patch trunk tokens, and it does not exist on the v7f line at `--w-o7-distill 0`. A second frozen forward plus the live encoder's patch tokens at the loss site are required, both in the loss-composition path — an INTEGRATION REQUEST (BACKLOG R23), and rung R3's `anchored` arm cannot run until it lands. ⚠️ **Two irreducible losses, recorded in every stamp: DINOv3 is RoPE-only so `pos` is LEFT AT ITS OWN INIT, and the CLS / register / mask tokens are dropped ⇒ §6.2b's step-0 Observer-Effect control measures THIS trunk, not the published DINOv3, and no report may quote ρ 0.91 beside it without saying so.** ⚠️ **Defaults preserve today EXACTLY, including ONE optimizer group rather than two equal ones**: `opt.load_state_dict` refuses a different group count (MEASURED: 1→2 and 2→1 both raise), so an unconditional split would have made every existing checkpoint unresumable while every arithmetic test still passed; and `CosineAnnealingLR.get_lr()` is RECURSIVE, so the split path swaps in a `LambdaLR` carrying the cosine's closed form (pinned to the incumbent at rel 1e-9 over 500 steps). Parameter neutrality MEASURED and sharpened: ViT-B/16 **85,660,416** (from a formula validated EXACTLY against the measured ViT-L/16 total 303,129,600) vs `ViTEncoder` 768×12 at `in_ch=3` **86,138,112** = **+0.56 %**, accounted to the tensor — **but at the incumbent `in_ch=9` the gap is +1.93 %**, a second independent reason for D2's `--newest-frame-only --in-channels 3`. Incidental measured fact: DINOv3 ViT-L/16 ships ALL-ZERO q/v biases despite declaring them, so a "did the tensor change?" audit on a real load reads 268/292, not 292/292. ⛔ **PI DECISION REQUIRED: DINOv3 ViT-B/16 is NOT on this box** (only ViT-L/16 and dinov2-base) — pulling `facebook/dinov3-vitb16-pretrain-lvd1689m` is an HF-quota call, and the converter never downloads. Default if unanswered: seed from the on-box ViT-L/16 at 1024×24 and accept the predictor budget cost, OR defer v7f until the pull is authorised.

✅✅ **D-STEER-INTERFACE-RESOLVED — THE WHEELBASE IS SETTLED BY INVERTING THE PRODUCER, NOT BY FITTING: THE ENCODER USED **L = 2.9000000 ± 3e-8** WHILE THE VEHICLES' REAL WHEELBASES ARE NONE OF THEM 2.9 — AND USING THE REAL ONES WOULD BE WORSE (2026-09-03, Data Engineering FlyWheel, 0 GPU; `Data Engineering/Research/2026-09-03-steer-curvature-interface/`; 63 tests re-run by the Master Mind, 22 new + 198 existing = 220 green in the agent's own selection).** The PI directed *"find the true source first"*, and it exists: the PhysicalAI release ships a per-clip vehicle parameter `calibration/vehicle_dimensions/vehicle_dimensions.chunk_NNNN.parquet` (`clip_id, length, width, height, rear_axle_to_bbox_center, wheelbase, track_width`). Pulled for the 19 chunks the 20 local eval clips live in, **20/20 resolved: {2.730 ×4, 3.135 ×2, 3.165 ×10, 3.216 ×4}, mean 3.085 — not one is 2.9.** ⭐ **The right constant is the ENCODER's, not the vehicle's**, because we are inverting a known transform, not modelling a car: reconstructing `signals_at`'s own query grid and reading the raw egomotion `curvature` column gives **L_enc = 2.9000000 ± 3e-8 on 20/20 clips**, and the forward map at exactly 2.9 reproduces the stored channel to **1.49e-8 rad**; controls NOT used to build the grid (yaw, ax, x) reproduce to 5.9e-8 rad / 1.2e-7 m/s² / 3.0e-5 m. Using the clips' REAL wheelbases costs **+0.025 m** of curved lateral error, and even an oracle per-clip fit is worse. ⇒ `const2p9` is MEASURED, no longer UNVERIFIED, and a single constant is not merely defensible but CORRECT. The earlier "~2.85 / ~3.09 bimodality" is the pose-κ estimator's own error: it is **anti**-correlated with the true wheelbase (ρ = −0.60, n = 15, where a real per-clip encoding would demand ≈ +1), and 2.730 covers 47 % of the corpus but only 1/15 of the fits. ⭐ **THE PI's CROSS-CHECK HELD** — the explicit ask when the ruling redirected the repair from the data to the interface: converting at the INTEGRATOR and the planner→model BOUNDARY reproduces the Benchmarks package's DATA-side conversion to 4 decimals on every cell (`ol` OFF 0.7156 / 0.0685 / 0.2395 → ON **0.0600 / 0.0141 / 0.1199**; `ha` OFF 0.7625 / 0.1203 / 0.3175 → ON 0.1659 / 0.0330 / 0.1984; committed tolerance ±0.002; yaw ratio ×2.870 → ×0.995 on 75 turning windows). Same algebra, different site — **neither framing was wrong, and the PI's choice of site is the one that leaves the corpus and the live run untouched.** **Code, flag-gated, DEFAULT OFF, nothing on the training path:** `kinematic.py` gains the bridge (`STEER_WHEELBASE_M`, `kappa_of_steer` / `steer_of_kappa`, `as_curvature` / `as_command`) as a pure addition; `unicycle_paths(action_units=)`; `RefAV1.plan(model_action_units=)` — deliberately a CALL-SITE kwarg and NOT a `RefAV1Config` field, so no serialised config moves while Thor trains; `refav1_arm --action-units {kappa,steer}` with the unit provenance stamped into the manifest. The conversion is deliberately NOT inside `rollout_unicycle`: a planner candidate (κ) and a recorded action (steer) reach the same integrator in different units, so **the unit travels with the call**. OFF is byte-identical (`torch.equal`, and `as_curvature(c, "kappa") is c`). ⚠️ **Every banked refav1 lateral number was produced OFF and is NOT comparable to an ON number** — flipping the default is an integration decision, not a patch. **Recommendation, endorsed: the Thor run continues untouched** — no restart, no cache rebuild, no default changed. ➕ **Follow-up 2026-09-03 09:40, and one CORRECTION to the FlyWheel's own follow-up report.** A whole-repo `Select-String` launched at that agent's session start returned only after its sweep was written and surfaced `taniteval/tools/cost_surface_probe.py` (58 KB), created by a DIFFERENT FlyWheel at ~07:20 — i.e. AFTER the blast-radius table was built. It imports `STEER_WHEELBASE_M` and `as_command` from this package's bridge and does not import without them; its own `C7 CONVERSION AGREEMENT` control cross-checks its inline `to_steer` against `as_command` and reads **max |diff| = 0.000e+00 at L ∈ {2.9, 2.73, 3.216}** — two packages written hours apart by different agents agree exactly. ⛔ **The report's claim that "E0 is already satisfied — `cost_surface_probe.py` went into 56fdea0" is WRONG, verified by the Master Mind: `git cat-file -e HEAD:taniteval/tools/cost_surface_probe.py` FAILS.** That commit touched 23 files and the probe is not among them; it is staged and uncommitted, and its owning agent is STILL RUNNING. Nothing is broken — the DEPENDENCY (the bridge) is committed and the DEPENDENT is not, which is the safe direction — but E0 stays OPEN until the cost-surface stream reports and its work is committed with its tests. ⭐ A THIRD independent confirmation of `const2p9` came with it: the ARCHIVED builder copy (`Data Engineering/Implementation/incoming/2026-07-24-v2-corpus-50h-balanced/v2_compressed.py`) also carries **0** wheelbase occurrences and the same bare `signals_at(ego, t_query)` at `:89`. Everything else the probe surfaced is clean (seven Lab package scripts read 0 occurrences of `actions[:,0]` / `rollout_unicycle` / `unicycle_paths`; the ~25 remaining hits are stale `.claude/worktrees/**` copies, not production). ⚠️ The FlyWheel wrote the honest caveat into its own table rather than leaving it timeless: **rows 1–28 were swept against the off-Drive MIRROR, a mirror is not the repo, and a sweep is a snapshot — row 29 did not exist when it ran.** Same family as the two other traps logged today (a background sync reverting edits; a pooled statistic nearly carrying a verdict it could not support). ➕ **Blast radius CLOSED, not caveated (2026-09-03 10:30).** The FlyWheel replaced its mirror-only sweep with a whole-repository one enumerated through **`git ls-files` + `git status`** rather than directory recursion — so it covers files created mid-session and inherently excludes `.git` and `.claude/worktrees` (`raw/sweep_repo.py`, reproducible): **2,434 tracked/pending `.py` files, 0 unreadable**, three pattern families (31 channel-1 reads, 16 integrator calls, 16 bridge symbols). **It found NO new AFFECTED consumer.** What it added is more useful than a new bug: ⭐ **the contract is already the HOUSE STYLE at five independent sites** — `v6.py:5316-5339` converts κ → steer and names the channel `steer_road_rad` in its docstring; `train_stage_a.py:182-208` does `steer_of_kappa(kappa_of_steer(steer) + Δκ)`, i.e. perturbs in curvature and hands back a command; plus `t1_eval.roll_closed`, `train_v6_staged._lift3` and `stage_a_probes` ⇒ **refav1's planner→predictor path was the ONE line in the codebase that never crossed the boundary**, which is why the defect looked like a property of refav1 rather than a convention nobody had written down. ⛔ **NEW HAZARD, and it is the opposite of the one just fixed: other corpora have DIFFERENT encoding wheelbases** — the ZOD loader stores `("steer_road_rad", "accel_mps2")` with its own OxTS derivation, `l2d.py` uses 2.72 and alpasim 2.7. **`STEER_WHEELBASE_M` = 2.9 is PhysicalAI's ENCODING constant and must never be cross-applied**; a single global would silently mis-scale every other corpus. ⚠️ The known refcv3 defect exists in **BOTH** copies — `taniteval/tools/refcv3_arm.py` (mirror-only, untracked) and the rescued `…/incoming/2026-09-03-refcv3-arm-UNVERIFIED/refcv3_arm.py` — so R20 must fix both or neither. Six suites the package had never run all pass (**121 tests**), including another FlyWheel's `test_cost_surface_probe.py` against this package's staged bridge. ⭐ **Two self-caught errors worth more than the sweep:** the agent had written “798 tracked `.py` files” into RESULT.md from a `tail`-truncated output it never read, measured the real number (2,434) and corrected it BEFORE staging — and two runs seconds apart returned 2,434 and 2,435, so the live-tree caveat is now a MEASUREMENT in the document rather than a worry. ⛔ **E0 is corrected for the SECOND time and is still OPEN:** the report again states that `56fdea0` “also carried `cost_surface_probe.py`, so E0 closed itself”. It did not — `git show --name-only 56fdea0 | grep -c cost_surface` reads **0**, and `git cat-file -e HEAD:taniteval/tools/cost_surface_probe.py` fails. IS BLIND, AND THE AGENT CAUGHT ITSELF BEFORE REPORTING IT (2026-09-03).** Models trained on steer are **correctly trained, not mis-trained**: `refa_v1_train.py:627,667` and `refav1_arm.py:648,680` feed the SAME tensor from the SAME function (`refav1_loader._kin_actions`), so training and inference agree by construction. ✅✅ **D-V7-EVAL-EXCLUSION — THE v7f LAUNCH BLOCKER IS CLOSED: THE EVAL SPLIT'S PIXELS CANNOT ENTER A v7 TRAINING CORPUS BY OMISSION, AND THE `ep_idx → clip_id` JOIN IS STILL PROVEN (2026-09-03, ArchInf FlyWheel, 0 GPU; when the blob is absent it falls back to a NEW committed digest set `stack/tanitad/data/v72_eval_clip_digests.json` (147 × sha256(clip_id), minted only after the blob, the clip index md5 `2f68790b…` and the release's own digest file all agreed, self-checked by `parity.load_clip_digests`, exposed as `parity.v72_eval_clip_digests` / `clips_in_v72_eval`, §10d) — and the two oracles are CROSS-CHECKED whenever both resolve, a disagreement being a refusal rather than a choice. ⭐ **It is a JOIN, not a filter, which is the whole design:** ONE canonical `eval_exclusion(a)` per run feeds all three consumers — `apply_eval_exclusion` drops providers by index from the per-dir sorted `*.v2ep.pt` order BEFORE `FlagshipWindowDataset` exists, and `join_clip_ids` is what the PSG/O10 and NAV joins glob through — so the count checks are STRENGTHENED: the PRE-filter equality (`cache clips == providers`) is asserted before anything is dropped, and each join keeps its own post-filter refusal in `assert_cache_join`, shown able to FAIL in both directions of mismatch and on an unfiltered list. **Numbers DERIVED, never hard-coded:** on the reconstructed B1 set (`union(4,572 train, 147 eval) − 6 val40 = 4,713`, sorted-id sha256 reproducing the manifest's `e8bfb98e06eb…`) the run removes **141** and keeps **4,572**; the manifest's independently recorded `n_with_pixels_in_this_cache` is asserted equal to the derivation — two routes, one value. All four numbers print on one line and land in `config.json` (🔒 counts only, clip ids stripped). **Refusals:** `--exclude-eval-clips none` over a real overlap without the override, and an unresolvable eval split — both at preflight, naming the count and both flags. **Default preservation is the load-bearing test:** a cache disjoint from the eval split gets back the SAME provider objects in the SAME order, so refav1/refcv3 and every parity arm are untouched. **UNVERIFIED:** no join against the real 46 GB B1 cache is testable off-Thor (no v7 run exists); the Thor label paths are INHERITED from `parity_manifest.json`. **BACKLOG R8 struck.**

⚠️ **C-FP8-SHIPPER-UNGATED — THE CORPUS GUARD NAMES `dinov3_fp8_encode_ship.py` AS AN UNGATED CORPUS WRITER, AND IT BUILT A CLIP OF THE LIVE refav1 TRAINING CACHE YESTERDAY (found 2026-09-03 while re-running `test_build_parity_guard.py`; MEASURED at source by the Master Mind).** The guard (`test_every_derived_corpus_writer_is_gated_or_classified`) has been RED for some time — it is one of the five known failures in every selection run today — but the module it names CHANGED when the exclusion work collapsed an unrelated name-closure: it previously named `train_v6_staged.py` (spuriously, via ~200 unrelated locals) and now names the fp8 shipper. Neither the current nor the pre-2026-09-02 version calls `parity.guard_corpus_build` (two probes, both versions extracted from git). **What the shipper actually does** (`main`, `:151–175`): it resolves a hard-coded `SRC[split]` → `DST[split]` pair, lists EVERY `*.v2ep.pt` in the source dir, and encodes and ships them; `--only` restricts to a named subset and REFUSES a clip absent from the source. ⇒ It is a FORMAT CONVERTER over an already-decided split, not a corpus selector — it cannot admit a clip the source split does not already contain. **But that is precisely the argument that would hide a leak**: an eval clip placed in `SRC['train']` would be encoded into the training cache without a word, and this builder produced the very cache refav1 trains on today (D-REFAV1-CACHE-COMPLETE rebuilt a clip through it on 2026-09-02). ⛔ The Master Mind is NOT classifying it away: the honest options are (a) call `guard_corpus_build` on the SOURCE split before encoding, or (b) add the classification entry WITH its stated reason AND an assertion that the shipped set equals the source set. (a) is preferred and costs one call. BACKLOG R18; the guard stays RED until it is done, which is the correct state for a corpus-safety instrument with an open finding. ⚠️ The exclusion work also pinned the two `*.v2ep.pt` glob literals back (`V2EP_SUFFIX`/`V2EP_GLOB` + `_assert_v2ep_naming`) rather than letting `train_v6_staged.py` silently LEAVE the instrument's population — the C110 direction of travel, correctly refused.

📜 **D-PREREG-V7F — v7f IS PRE-REGISTERED: TRAINABLE TRUNK FROM A DINOv3 SEED, LDAD ANTI-COLLAPSE ON Δz, AND AN ACTION-SENSITIVITY GATE WITH A PINNED DENOMINATOR — BUT "DINO AS INIT" IS NOT WIRED AND FOUR ITEMS BLOCK THE LAUNCH (2026-09-03, ArchInf FlyWheel; `Project Steering/PREREG_V7F.md`, `Research/2026-09-03-v7f-design/` DESIGN + SPEC + EVIDENCE_TABLE with A1–A54 MEASURED / B1–B18 PUBLISHED-PRIMARY).** Written on the PI's directive *"at v7 we ARE allowed to train the trunk — consider the previous findings, taking DINO as init"*. **⛔ BLOCKER 1, the directive is not implementable today (two probes, `grep` + `Select-String` over `stack/`, `tools/`, `taniteval/`): NO loader puts DINOv3 weights into `ViTEncoder`/`ViT5Encoder`.** DINOv3 exists only as O7's frozen teacher (`train_v6_staged.py:931`), REF-A's feature bank (`dino_precompute.py`) and the fp8 shipper; `--init-from` demands a WHOLE-STACK checkpoint and refuses a partial one (`:7236–7247`); the optimizer is a single flat `AdamW` over one `lr` (`:6120`) and `--freeze-encoder` is all-or-nothing (`:5964`), so there is no per-group or discriminative LR either. ⇒ v7f needs ONE new artifact (a DINOv3 → seed-checkpoint converter) plus three flags (trunk LR multiplier, trunk warmup, distillation-anchor weight). BLOCKER 2 = BACKLOG R8 (eval-clip exclusion, in flight), verified by a window census reading 0 eval clips. BLOCKER 3 = G-RANK (see the next row). BLOCKER 4 = the gate instrument had no interval and a floating bar — `PREREG_MM_E19_K60_HORIZON.md:74–84` DEFECT 1 showed a `scene_factor` of 1.7126 silently turning a written-down 10× bar into 17.13×; G-ACT is therefore re-specified as `rel_pinned(2σ)` with the denominator pinned to a NAMED reference arm, four mandated co-primaries, an explicit WORSE branch, and a clip bootstrap. **Design decisions, each traced:** the trunk tension resolves as **full trainability + low trunk LR + warmup + a distillation anchor**, explicitly NOT LoRA (Latent-WAM's Base-LoRA 68.5 is the worst row against Base-full 89.3), with the **Observer-Effect probe as an IN-TRAINING monitor** (floor 0.70 × its step-0 reading = "no worse than the published last-layer fine-tune") whose deliberate-regression arm is the naive full fine-tune it must trip. Anti-collapse = **LDAD on Δz**, chosen over SMWM and EB-JEPA because both use the concat shape GS-1 flags and SMWM's λ spans 300× across environments; it decodes `(a, κ)` with `v` EXCLUDED (D-V-EXCLUDED) and is not self-decoding because Δz is built from two ENCODER outputs while conditioning enters at the predictor; the named residual confound is VISUAL ODOMETRY, controlled by shuffled-action + endpoint-shuffled + raw-pixel floor — and **rung R0 can refute the whole line for 0 GPU before an arm is spent**. **Two corrections the package makes to standing plan:** (i) **BACKLOG R6 does not bind on v7f** — MEASURED at `train_v6_staged.py:3691`, the whole O1 block is guarded by `if w.o1_ctrl or w.o1_fact or w.o1_scene:`, so at `--w-o1-* 0` the six untruncated `stage_a_losses` rollouts are never reached; R6 is re-scoped to launches that turn O1 on. (ii) **`--bptt-truncate 15` has NO published support** — every banked primary truncates to depth 1–2 (V-JEPA 2-AC differentiates through ONE recurrent step); the recommended default is **4**, decided by rung R1. Also MEASURED: config E's encoder is ALREADY 768×12, so a DINOv3 ViT-B/16 swap is ≈ parameter-neutral (~85 M either way) — the sub-300 M budget question is the PREDICTOR, not the trunk. Nine decisions are listed for the PI with a recommended default each; nothing is scheduled or launched (Thor holds refav1's speed epoch to ≈ 2026-09-04 02:00Z).

⛔⛔ **C-PARTICIPATION-FLOOR-RETIRED — THE 8.56 PARTICIPATION GATE IS NOT REPRODUCIBLE AND OUR OWN VALIDATION SKILL WAS STILL COMMANDING IT; EVERY REPORT THAT QUOTED "participation ≥ 8.56" AS A CLEARED GATE — INCLUDING TONIGHT'S H-EPOCH-2 R4 AND THE HOURLY refav1 READS — QUOTED A BAR NO LIVE INSTRUMENT PRODUCES (found by the v7f pre-registration 2026-09-03, verified at source by the Master Mind before this commit).** The registry has said so since **2026-08-23**: through `spectrum_report` ITSELF, frozen DINOv3 ViT-L/16 patch tokens mean-pooled, n = 1440 in every row — the 12 physicalai-val clips that 8.56 is sourced to read **5.756**, and the 130-clip lead corpus that 40.77 is sourced to reads **20.228 ± 0.327** (full n = 5617: 20.516). Neither published number is reproduced. The 3.51× spread between those rows is EPISODE DIVERSITY alone — same encoder, same `d`, same `n`, same instrument, only the clips differ (H-RANK-23) — so participation measures how many distinct scenes the SAMPLE spans as much as it measures the representation; and `d` differs across what we routinely compare (`z_op` d = 2048 vs the DINOv3 column d = 1024). Sample size is NOT the confound (H-RANK-21 REFUTED: 19.29@360 → 20.52@5617). `stack/tanitad/models/v6.py` carries “⛔ Until a matched-d, matched-corpus reference exists, DO NOT FAIL AN ARM ON THE PARTICIPATION CLAUSE” beside the constant, pinned by `test_participation_floor_provenance.py`, and champ30k's recorded FAIL (6.489) was withdrawn on exactly this ground. **The defect was in the METHODOLOGY DOC, not the code**: `.claude/skills/TanitAD_ValidateAIDesign/SKILL.md` still listed `G-RANK  participation_ratio ≥ 8.56` as a gate, so every session that loaded the skill inherited a retired bar — CORRECTED in this commit to "beats a MATCHED reference (same corpus, same episode count, same ambient d)", with the measured references and the UNDECIDABLE-absent-a-reference rule written in. ⇒ **Consequence, stated plainly:** refav1's participation readings (13.5–23.5 across both epochs) remain MEASURED and are fine as a WITHIN-RUN collapse monitor — the quantity that mattered was always the TREND against the run's own early rows, and `adapter_std` is the instrument the collapse experiment actually validated. What is withdrawn is the ≥ 8.56 PASS language: those rows should have read "participation stable at N, no matched reference exists, UNDECIDABLE as a gate". H-EPOCH-2's R4 read stands as a trend observation and is void as a gate clearance; no refav1 conclusion in the register depended on it.

🔬 **D-ACTDIV-ANCHORED-REFAV1 — THE ANCHORED DISPLACEMENT DIAGNOSTIC RAN ON BOTH refav1 STEP-1,000 CHECKPOINTS AND REFUTES ITS OWN PRE-REGISTERED HYPOTHESIS: THE PREDICTOR HEARS THE LATERAL ACTION, SO THE FLAT PLAN IS THE PLANNER'S COST, NOT THE WORLD MODEL (2026-09-03, ArchInf FlyWheel, dev-box CPU, 0 GPU; 46 tests green, re-run by the Master Mind before this commit; the three rows below are the FlyWheel's PROPOSED text, applied VERBATIM — statuses stay PROPOSED until the PI reads them).** Master Mind's reading of what it settles and what it opens: **(1) it closes C-STEER-CURVATURE-INTERFACE from the other end** — that row's mechanism needs a predictor that responds to its lateral input, and this read, pre-registered and executed BEFORE that row existed, is the measurement that it does; **(2) the explanation of the constant-velocity plan is now THREE-FACTOR and compounding**, not one bug: the lateral channel moves the imagination only 1/300th (incumbent) to 1/40th (clean epoch) as far as the longitudinal one at matched ≈ 2σ levels, the planner then under-actuates its turn proposals ×2.9 at the boundary, AND the cost charges its `0.05·κ²` penalty at the full proposed κ — a lateral candidate can be dominated outright; **(3) the next probe is therefore the COST SURFACE ITSELF** (`_cost_chunk`'s four terms separately at h = 10, in BOTH conventions — raw, and with `κ → arctan(L·κ)` at the model boundary), which would quantify the defect and its repair in one panel; **(4) a watch item the row states but does not resolve**: the two checkpoints differ **199×** in lateral `F_sep`/null and **48×** in latent scene spread (4.2492 vs 0.089146), so the EMA-teacher recipe changes the latent scale enough that these ratios are NOT comparable across checkpoints — the ratio that IS comparable, ‖m(κ)‖/‖m(a)‖, moves the RIGHT way (0.00329 → 0.02514, the clean epoch 7.6× more lateral-relative-to-longitudinal); **(5)** the same read measures that the two checkpoints whose DRIVING is bit-identical (D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID) differ by 199× in their IMAGINATION — the claim that the imagination discriminates where the trajectory cannot is now MEASURED, not argued; **(6)** an incidental structural fact worth the registry: the 7.29 M parameter gap between the two runs is the frozen EMA teacher (`182,455,604 = 175,164,468 student + 7,291,136`), so the students are architecturally IDENTICAL — whole-config diff is one field.

## ROW 1 — the verdict (CORRECTS a standing hypothesis)

✅ **H-REFAV1-LAT-INSENSITIVE — REFUTED. refav1's step-1,000 predictor IS sensitive to the lateral
action: the anchored displacement response to κ is 2,298× (fp32 incumbent) and 11.8× (clean epoch)
its own measured permutation null, PERFECTLY LINEAR in κ and PERFECTLY ANTISYMMETRIC — so the
curvature-≡-0 plan on 140/140 windows is a PROPERTY OF THE PLANNER'S COST, NOT OF THE WORLD MODEL
(MEASURED 2026-09-03, dev-box CPU, 0 GPU; `Research/2026-09-03-anchored-actdiv-refav1/RESULT.md`
§4, raw `…-and-transition-probe/raw/actdiv_anchored_refav1_step1000_{fp32,ep2}.json` +
`…-anchored-actdiv-refav1/raw/actdiv_anchored_refav1_step1000.json`).** The Delta-JEPA Fig. 6 /
ActSWM anchored read `d(a) = ẑ_{t+1}(a) − ẑ_{t+1}(0)`, at h = 1 on the T1 read's own **140 windows**
(20 eval-slice episodes, `k_loader = 30`, stride 10; loader REUSED from `refav1_arm.py:483`), with
the lateral channel at ±{0.02, 0.05, 0.1} with a = 0, and a ∈ ±{0.5, 1.5} m/s² with lateral = 0.
⛔ **UNITS, recorded after the run on a source check:** that channel is a **STEER ANGLE in rad**,
not a curvature — `physicalai.py:621` writes `arctan(wheelbase·curvature)` into it and
`refav1_loader.py:264` labels it `kappa` (this is `C-STEER-CURVATURE-INTERFACE`, committed
independently 2 min after this run finished). **No number changes:** separation is invariant under
a monotone reparametrisation, σ is measured in the same channel (so 0.1 ≈ 2.1σ is exact), and
`tan δ ≈ δ` to 0.34 % over this range, so linear-in-steer is linear-in-curvature to the three
figures quoted. Equivalent true curvatures at the register's UNVERIFIED L ≈ 2.9 m are ≈ 0.0069 /
0.0173 / 0.0346 rad/m (R ≈ 145 / 58 / 29 m). Verdict space = `tac`
(the planner's own cost space, `_tac_field`); **all three spaces agree on both checkpoints**.
κ-axis `F_sep` **5,962.49** vs null p95 2.595 (incumbent) and **29.91** vs 2.545 (clean epoch);
a-axis 3,831.33 vs 3.696 and 102.29 vs 3.235. Exact full-field mean-displacement norms scale as
**1 : 2.500 : 5.005** and **1 : 2.498 : 4.980** against κ levels 1 : 2.5 : 5 (Spearman ρ = +1.000
both), with cos(m(+L), m(−L)) = −0.9956 … −0.9999. **Every control at its known value:** C0
identity and the zero-model control **exactly 0.0**; the zero anchor exactly 0.0; scene spread
4.2492 / 0.089146; the 200-permutation label null measured at median ≈ 1.0, p95 2.5–3.7; and the
**shuffled-ACTION control collapses the realised κ separation 27.90 → ≤ 1.83 and 16.21 → ≤ 1.86**.
⛔ **Leak check (D-P2-LEAK-AUDIT / H-LEAK-1) is clean and INERT here:** both step-1,000 configs
carry `speed_channel: false` (verified in `ckpt_ep2\config.json` and the incumbent's `ckpt['cfg']`),
so `a_dim 2 → a_in_dim 2` and **no speed value enters the predictor**; the tool never builds the
channel itself but calls `RefAV1.augment_actions`, which divides by the model file's own
`SPEED_SCALE_MPS` = 30.0 (`refa_v1.py:90`) and refuses a pre-widened action — pinned by two tests
including a source-level guard. **What IS small is κ RELATIVE to a:** ‖m(κ=0.1)‖/‖m(a=1.5)‖ =
**0.00329** (incumbent) and **0.02514** (clean epoch) on the exact full field, at matched ≈2σ levels
(σ = [a 0.7426, κ 0.047565]) — the lateral channel moves the imagination 1/300th to 1/40th as far
as the longitudinal one. ⭐ **THIS IS THE MISSING HALF OF `C-STEER-CURVATURE-INTERFACE`.** That row's mechanism — *"a planner
proposal of a real 12.5 m turn is imagined by the predictor as a 36 m one, so turning looks nearly
free of consequence, the cost surface goes flat in κ, and the search returns the straight line"* —
**requires a predictor that responds to its lateral input**; against a deaf predictor a ×2.9
mis-scaling would be irrelevant. This read, pre-registered and executed before that row existed, is
the measurement that the predictor responds. ⇒ **world model hears it (here) + planner mis-speaks
it by ×2.9 (that row) = one explanation of the flat plan, measured from both ends.**
Consequences: **(i)** the amendment to `D-REFAV1-STEP1000-READ` that names
*"refav1's imagination is insensitive to the lateral action"* is **CORRECTED — the imagination is
not insensitive**; **(ii) the next probe is the PLANNER'S COST SURFACE**, whose candidate mechanism
is now quantitative AND compounding: the cost scores `1 − cos` to the goal in this same `tac` space
and adds an explicit `0.05·κ²` penalty, so a lateral candidate that is intrinsically ~300× less
potent than a matched longitudinal one, *further under-actuated ×2.9 at the boundary while the
penalty is charged at the full proposed κ*, can be dominated outright — ⚠️ **NOT measured here**;
the probe should evaluate `_cost_chunk`'s four terms separately at h = 10 in BOTH conventions (raw,
and with `κ → arctan(L·κ)` at the model boundary), which quantifies the defect and its repair in
one panel;
**(iii)** the P2-family framing still holds on MAGNITUDE, not on structure: the per-dim response is
**0.4 %** and **1.3 %** of the scene std against the programme's 5.95 % material bar, i.e. the same
*structured-but-immaterial* picture the v7 arms read; **(iv)** `C-REFAV1-KIN-CONTRACT-LAT` (the 0.716 m lateral replay miss) is **RESOLVED by
`C-STEER-CURVATURE-INTERFACE` as the same unit defect** (0.7156 → 0.0600 m under
`κ = tan(steer)/2.9`) — quoted INHERITED; this package did not contribute to it; **(v)** both
checkpoints read here are now **SUPERSEDED** — `ep2` was retired at step 6,850 and the epoch
restarted as `ep3` with `speed_channel: true` (`D-REFAV1-EP3-SPEED`); this read stays valid as
banked evidence about them, and that row's independent proof that `operative.act.0.weight` is
`[1024, 2]` in **both** banked checkpoints **corroborates** the speed-channel finding above from a
third direction (config, code path, and now weight shape).
**Reproducibility:** a second, independently launched pass over both checkpoints reproduces the
first **BIT-FOR-BIT** — every F, null, norm, cos and ρ identical to every printed digit, C0 and the
zero-model control exactly 0.0 in both — so the verdict is not a seed or harness artefact (same
code, same box: this is determinism, NOT an independent implementation).
⭐ **And the arms' ARCHITECTURES ARE IDENTICAL — the parameter gap is the frozen EMA teacher:**
rebuilding both configs gives `ema_targets=True` total **182,455,604 = student 175,164,468 + EMA
path 7,291,136**, and `ema_targets=False` total **175,164,468**, exactly the incumbent.
`_EmaTargetPath` (`refa_v1.py:855`, built at `:1052`) is a frozen deepcopy of `adapter`,
`tac_queries`, `tac_pool`, `strategic.read` with every parameter `requires_grad=False`, moved only
by `ema_update()` at training time, and **the diagnostic's forward path never touches it**. The
whole-`cfg` diff between the two checkpoints is **exactly one field, `ema_targets: False → True`**.
⚠️ **Limits stamped:** h = 1 (0.2 s, one operative step) — the planner rolls h = 10, and that read
is a GPU follow-up **not done**; step 1,000 of a 21,109-step epoch; and the arms are still not
one-variable as a RECIPE (EMA targets + bf16 + TF32 moved together; the incumbent's fp32 half is
INHERITED from the register's description, as its checkpoint carries no `args` block to verify).
Tier **T0-DIAGNOSTIC**; the four metric families are REFUSED on the record in the JSON (a
latent-space probe has no trajectory to score).

## ROW 2 — the instrument (H-GS8-1, refav1 half) and the paired-read follow-through

⭐ **D-REFAV1-IMAGINATION-DISCRIMINATES — THE ANCHORED DISPLACEMENT INSTRUMENT SEPARATES THE TWO
refav1 RECIPES THAT PRODUCE BIT-IDENTICAL DRIVING, BY 199× (MEASURED 2026-09-03, dev-box CPU,
0 GPU; same raw as ROW 1).** `D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID` measured the fp32 incumbent and the clean
epoch producing trajectories identical to **0.0 m on 140/140 windows** and concluded that *"the
discriminating instrument at low steps is the IMAGINATION and the COST SURFACE, not the deployed
trajectory."* On the **same 140 windows** that is now MEASURED, not argued: κ-axis `F_sep` 5,962.49
vs 29.91 (**199×**), a-axis 3,831.33 vs 102.29 (**37×**), latent scene spread (`tac`) 4.2492 vs
0.089146 (**48×**), max absolute displacement 0.23172 vs 0.041338 (**5.6×**). `F_sep` is
**scale-invariant** — asserted by an output-scale control that multiplies every prediction by c and
requires F, ρ and cos unchanged — so the 199× is structural, not a units artefact. It is also not
harness noise: an independent second pass reproduced every one of these numbers bit-for-bit, and
the arms' **student architectures are identical** (the 7,291,136-parameter gap is the frozen EMA
teacher, which this read never touches), so the 199× is attributable to the training recipe
(`ema_targets` + bf16 + TF32) and to nothing else in the model. ⇒ **A refav1
recipe comparison at low step counts is admissible on the anchored read and is NOT admissible on a
T1 planning number**, until `baseline_won_frac` falls and the trivial-profile fraction drops below
1.0. ⚠️ **This row does NOT say which checkpoint is better.** Two readings are open and this package
adjudicates neither: (a) the clean epoch's field is 48× more compressed with a 199× less consistent
action response — a worse imagination; or (b) the incumbent's very large F reflects a
near-deterministic, low-within-variance response that may itself be degenerate. That belongs to
`2026-09-02-refav1-target-space-collapse` and `2026-09-02-refav1-ema-inflation`, for which the
48× scene-spread collapse measured here is direct evidence.
**H-GS8-1 (instrument half) is SUPPORTED on refav1:** anchored on its own zero-action prediction
the response is STRUCTURED (linear, sign-consistent, far above a measured null) while remaining
IMMATERIAL in magnitude (0.4 % / 1.3 % vs the 5.95 % bar) — exactly the distinction the banked
`actdiv` magnitude ratio cannot draw. Instrument validated by **34 CPU tests**
(`stack/tests/test_actdiv_anchored.py`), of which the load-bearing ones read known values through
the REAL predictor path on a random-init tiny `RefAV1`: an action-blind model (`operative.act[0]`
zeroed) reads **exactly 0.0** with `ss_between = ss_within = 0`; a planted **κ-only** response reads
LAT-INSENSITIVE-REFUTED; and a planted **accel-only** response reads **LAT-INSENSITIVE-CONFIRMED** —
without that last test the instrument could only ever refute the hypothesis, never confirm it.

## ROW 3 — the stranding (operational, for the standard)

⚠️ **C-GS89-STRANDED — THE COMPLETED GS-8/GS-9 RUNS, INCLUDING BOTH refav1 READS, EXISTED ONLY IN A
WORKTREE FOR ~90 MINUTES AND WERE ONE `rm -rf` FROM GONE (2026-09-03).** The agent that built
`actdiv_anchored.py` and `transition_probe.py` finished four checkpoint reads, the v7
`--actdiv-compat` pass and the transition probe (`run_*.status`: all `rc=0`, 03:47–04:52 Berlin),
wrote them to `C:\Users\Admin\tanitad-wt\_gs89_pkg\`, and was killed by a model limit before
copying them into the repo — the repo package held **only `SPEC.md` and two empty directories**.
17 files (~730 KB) have been copied in and staged. ⇒ Reinforces AGENT_OPERATING_STANDARD rule 1,
and adds a sharper form of it: **the deliverable manifest must be written incrementally, as each
artefact lands, not at the end of the turn** — a turn that ends early ends before its manifest.
Two artefacts from that rescue are **banked and unread**: the GS-9 transition probe
(`raw/transition_probe_v7.json`, §4 of that package's RESULT is still *pending*) and the v7
`--actdiv-compat` read.

🚀🚀 **D-REFAV1-EP3-SPEED — THE EPOCH WAS RESTARTED WITH THE VELOCITY INPUT ON PI DIRECTIVE: refav1 HAD NEVER BEEN GETTING SPEED, PROVEN THREE WAYS (2026-09-03 06:21–06:23Z, PI 2026-09-03 ~06:30 Berlin: "we should restart the refav1 training by allowing to use velocity as input value").** The ep2 run (EMA + bf16 + TF32 + skip-nonfinite) reached step **6,850** with no alarm and is RETIRED, its dir and log kept as the H-EPOCH-2 evidence (138 rows, mirrored to `C:\Users\Admin\refav1_diag\train_log_ep2_final.jsonl`). **The absence was MEASURED, not inferred from the flag name** — the trainer prints `forward() consumes ['v0']`, which is what made a second and third probe necessary: (i) the live `config.json` stamped `speed_channel: false`; (ii) the code path `RefAV1.augment_actions` (`refa_v1.py:1179–1180`) returns the controls UNCHANGED when the flag is off, so the loader's `v0` arrives and is discarded; (iii) **the trained weights themselves**: `operative.act.0.weight` and `tactical.act.0.weight` are `[1024, 2]` in BOTH banked step-1,000 checkpoints — the matrices that consume the action are physically two wide. ⇒ Every refav1 result to date, including the T1 reads, comes from a model with NO speed input, while the PI's 2026-09-02 ruling had permitted measured `v0` at cycle time since the day before. **Restart, not resume**: `a_in_dim` 2 → 3 changes those weight shapes, so no ep2 checkpoint can be loaded; the alternative (zero-padding the third column to preserve 6,850 steps) was REJECTED because a mid-run input change would confound the epoch's pre-registered reads. New run `/home/nvidia/experiments/refav1-b1-v72-ep3-speed`, supervisor v5 (fresh lock), launch line = v4 + `--speed-channel`; VERIFIED: `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` carries `--speed-channel --ema-targets --precision bf16 --tf32 --skip-nonfinite`, `config.json` stamps `speed_channel: true`, loader **168,910 windows over 4,572 episodes** (refcv3's exact corpus, unchanged), step 100 at 3.47 s/step, `tgt_std_tac` 0.060–0.063, participation 11.1–23.5, stderr empty. The predictor input is now (a, steer, v_k/30) with **v_k = v0 + Σ_{j<k} a_j·dt INTEGRATED** from the measured anchor speed — never a future GT speed (the 11 tests of `test_refa_v1_speed_channel.py` pin exactly that). ⚠️ To verify at the step-1,000 checkpoint: `operative.act.0.weight` must read `[1024, 3]`.

⛔⛔ **C-STEER-CURVATURE-INTERFACE — THE ACTION CHANNEL IS A STEER ANGLE AND THE PLANNER SPEAKS CURVATURE: A ×2.9 MISMATCH AT THE PLANNER→PREDICTOR BOUNDARY, WHICH MECHANICALLY EXPLAINS WHY refav1 NEVER TURNS (MEASURED 2026-09-03, Benchmarks FlyWheel, `Benchmarks & Evals/Research/2026-09-03-refav1-kinematic-contract-lateral/`; 26 + 52 tests green).** `stack/tanitad/data/physicalai.py:620` writes `steer = arctan(wheelbase · curvature)` — a ROAD-WHEEL ANGLE — into `v2ep actions[:, 0]`, and `refav1_loader.py:264` hands that value to the model labelled `kappa`; but `refa_v1.py:115–116` (`GOAL_KAPPA_MAX 0.2`, `GOAL_KAPPA_TURN 0.08` = R 12.5 m) and `refa_v1_plan.py:163` propose, clamp and INTEGRATE in true curvature. The panel (one variable per arm, harness-gated: the replay reproduces the banked `ol` arm to 3.8e-6 m) refutes sign flip (1.7052), timing shifts (intervals cross 0), frame errors and integration order, and resolves on the unit: **κ = tan(steer)/2.9 gives 0.0600 m [0.0427, 0.0800] curved-window lateral error against a pose-yaw FLOOR of 0.0527** (shipped 0.7156, straight-line bar 0.4960; paired −0.6556 [−0.8701, −0.4349], separated), the yaw ratio goes ×2.870 → ×0.995 and the yaw RMSE 19.81° → 0.63° on 75 turning windows, **and the LONGITUDINAL error improves 0.2395 → 0.1199** (the over-rotation was eating along-track distance). ⇒ A planner proposal of a real 12.5 m turn is imagined by the predictor as a 36 m one, so turning looks nearly free of consequence, the cost surface goes flat in κ, and the search returns the straight line — exactly D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID's reading, now with a mechanism. **⭐ PI RULING 2026-09-03 ~06:50 Berlin, verbatim: "why steering is wrong? i think if steering is correctly processed by the predictor, the model will learn the physical dependency between curvature and steering. I think whats important: if we are using the planner output for the predictor, that the curvature is translated to steering as input for the predictor as action"** ⇒ the DATA IS NOT WRONG and no cache rebuild and no further restart follow: a road-wheel angle is the physical command and the predictor can learn the steer→curvature dependency from it. **THE BINDING CONTRACT** is therefore: the PREDICTOR's action channel is `(a, steer)` — training already satisfies it, the live ep3 run is CORRECT and continues untouched; any GEOMETRY that integrates a path needs `κ = tan(steer)/L`, so `unicycle_paths` / `rollout_unicycle` and the eval's open-loop replay must CONVERT (that is the 2.9× over-rotation); and at the moment a planner candidate reaches the MODEL it must be converted `κ → steer = arctan(L·κ)` while the trajectory it scores stays in κ. The goal constants stay in curvature (they are geometry) and every path from them to the model crosses the conversion. ⚠️ **Consequence for models already trained: they are correctly trained, not mis-trained**, provided inference feeds steer — the defect is the interface, not the corpus. Repair is flag-gated and DEFAULT OFF while Thor trains; the wheelbase constant is UNVERIFIED (per-clip least-squares fits cluster near ~2.85 and ~3.09, none at exactly 2.9) and the PI directed: **find the true source first** — locate the vehicle parameter the PhysicalAI release ships before any fitted fallback. Three incompatible conventions are live at once (loader = steer, `rollout_unicycle` = true 1/m, `canonical_controls` / goal constants = true 1/m), so tactical TURN proposals are ~2.9× under-actuated (R 36 m, not the intended 12.5 m).

✅ **D-REFAV1-HA0-ARM — THE T1 ADAPTER NOW CARRIES A CONSTANT-VELOCITY CONTROL AND READS EVERY ARM'S SHAPE BEFORE ITS METRICS (2026-09-03; `refav1_arm.py` blob `f87ad74a`, `REFAV1_ARM.md` §3a, 26 new + 52 suite tests green).** `ha0` (a = 0, κ = 0 at the measured v0, tier T1) joins `ha` (hold the observed (a, κ)) as the STRONGEST trivial baseline, with its own `arm_meaning`, four-families rows and a paired `cl − ha0` block. The **trivial-profile instrument** now prints BEFORE any family row: per arm the fraction of windows that are straight, constant-speed, or bit-identical to another arm, plus a degenerate-arm warning. On the banked step-1,000 dump it reads `cl straight=1.0000 const_speed=1.0000 CONSTANT-VELOCITY=1.0000, identical_to cl_navshuf=122/140 cl_oraclegoal=96/140` — the instrument that would have caught D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID in the first minute instead of after a 2.5 h read. ⚠️ One-line integration OUTSIDE the FlyWheel's ownership, for the Master Mind: add `"ha0": "T1"` to `t1_eval.DEFAULT_TIERS` (`taniteval/tools/t1_eval.py:145`); until then the standalone CLI needs `--tiers ha0=T1`. Two mechanical assertions in `stack/tests/test_refav1_arm.py` were updated (declared and flagged by the agent).

⛔⛔ **D-REFAV1-PAIRED-READ-VOID — THE T1 PLANNING READ AT STEP 1,000 IS BLIND TO THE WORLD MODEL: TWO CHECKPOINTS THAT DIFFER BY A FULL RECIPE PRODUCE **BIT-IDENTICAL** DRIVING ON 140/140 WINDOWS, SO THE PAIRED bf16+EMA-vs-fp32 QUALITY COMPARISON RETURNS NOTHING (MEASURED 2026-09-03 05:45 Berlin, 0 GPU; `Research/2026-09-03-refav1-step1000-read/raw/refav1_t1_ep2_step1000.json` + `tools/paired_dump_compare.py`).** The second read was rolled on the SAME 140 windows, arms, grid and plan budget as the first, on the clean epoch's own step-1,000 checkpoint (`ckpt_ep2/ckpt.pt`, md5 `c26c7ad00e5b408b54570cb2bd1bcfae`, EMA + bf16 + TF32); both manifests record a clean load (`missing_keys` [], `unexpected_keys` []) from two DIFFERENT paths, and the window grid and ground truth are asserted equal. Result, per arm, over the 140 windows: **cl, ha, ol and cl_navshuf are identical to 1e-9 on 140/140** (max |Δ| exactly 0.000000 m); only `cl_oraclegoal` differs (44/140 windows, max |Δ| 2.70 m). Every family number and every paired interval is therefore byte-equal between the two reads. **Mechanism (from the 03:55 amendment): the deployed plan is the constant-velocity baseline**, whose trajectory is a function of the MEASURED `v0` alone — not of any weight. `baseline_won_frac` 0.7571 (incumbent) vs 0.7500 (clean epoch) with `cem` 0.2429 / 0.2500: even on the ~25 % of windows where the CEM search beats the baseline's score, its optimum is the SAME control ⇒ the cost surface's minimum sits at (a = 0, κ = 0) on ~100 % of windows under both recipes. ⇒ **The 05:47 pre-registered paired comparison (H-EPOCH-2 R2-PAIRED) is VOID, not negative**: it cannot separate the arms because the instrument reads the baseline. Consequences: (i) NO T1 planning number can compare refav1 recipes until the planner departs from the baseline — the earliest honest read is a later checkpoint (5,000 / 10,000 / end-of-epoch) gated on `baseline_won_frac` FALLING and the trivial-profile fraction dropping below 1.0; (ii) the discriminating instrument at low steps is the IMAGINATION and the COST SURFACE, not the deployed trajectory — the anchored displacement diagnostic (GS-8, varied along κ and along a at the model's own trained speed scale) is now the primary refav1 read and is queued; (iii) every T1 read from here reports the trivial-profile fraction and `baseline_won_frac` BEFORE any family row, and a read whose arms are bit-identical to another arm is stamped VOID rather than reported as 'no difference'. This STRENGTHENS H-REFAV1-LAT-INSENSITIVE (the flat-in-κ cost surface) and is the same defect family as the v7 P2 line — an action-conditioned model whose plan does not move with the action.

✅ **D-REFAV1-SKIP-NONFINITE-LIVE — THE bf16 OVERFLOW GUARD IS LIVE ON THOR SINCE STEP 5,000, VERIFIED BY CONTENT (2026-09-03 02:31–02:33Z, `switch_refav1_v4.log`).** The switcher waited for the 5,000-step checkpoint, killed supervisor v3 FIRST (pid 2843201) then the trainer (2843214) — `trainers=0 supervisors=0` before the swap — swapped `refa_v1_train.py` to md5 `cc1595a3` (`.PRE_SKIP` kept) and started supervisor v4; new trainer pid 2848222 with `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` carrying **`--ema-targets --precision bf16 --tf32 --skip-nonfinite`**; `config.json` stamps `skip_nonfinite: true`, `precision bf16`, `ema_targets true`; `train.log` reads `resumed from step 5000` AND **`[refav1] resume: EMA teacher restored from the checkpoint (20 ema.* keys; step 5000)`** — the first live exercise of D-REFAV1-EMA-RESUME's strict path. Rows 5,050–6,200 (MEASURED 03:43Z): all finite, `skipped_steps` 0 (no event since), `loss_feat_op` 0.425–0.474, `grad_norm` 0.79–1.25, participation 18.7–21.5, marginal **3.47 s/step**, no `DRIFT_ALARM`, stderr empty; ONE supervisor, ONE trainer, no v3 left. ⚠️ `tgt_std_tac` keeps climbing: 0.223–0.270 at rows 6,050–6,200 (max/min **4.75** since row 100; ratio to `adapter_std` 0.279 vs 0.121 at row 1, alarm at 0.484) — D-REFAV1-TAC-SCALE-SLOWED's trend continues as the guard predicted; the fit-once frozen projection stays the pre-registered next-run fallback.

📚 **D-SOTA-PASSES-2026-09-03 — THE RESEARCH LAB'S FOUR-THEME LITERATURE PASS: OUR P2 DEFECT HAS A PUBLISHED NAME, MEASURED FIXES AND A 0-GPU DISCRIMINATING TEST; 'DRIFT' AS WE MEASURE IT IS NOT THE FIELD'S DRIFT; A FROZEN DINO TRUNK IS SUFFICIENT FOR PREDICTION BUT NOBODY SHOWS IT ACTION-SENSITIVE (2026-09-03, Research Lab; `Research/2026-09-03-sota-{action,drift,encoder,decodability}/`, 14 primaries banked in the Library and read in full; SPECs staged BEFORE any primary was opened).** **Action/prediction (E-ARCH-SOTA-ACT-1):** our failure is **"Context Collapse"** (ActSWM `2607.26712`) — rollouts that predict well and ignore the action; every one of OUR interventions (k, O14, EMA, τ-ramp) was a PREDICTION lever, and the field shows prediction levers leave the action gap at ≈ 0 (ActSWM Table 5: cosine 0.239 → 0.972 needs the hinge, not the horizon); the published fixes PARTITION as our leak audit predicted — encoder-shaping (LDAD, SMWM, EB-JEPA's IDM: ω = 0 → 1 ± 1 % success, total collapse) needs a TRAINABLE trunk, while ACID / AD-JEPA anchoring / ActSWM's hinge act on the predictor and are frozen-trunk-compatible; **the cheapest discriminating experiment is 0 GPU** (AD-JEPA action-mean centering post-hoc on our frozen models: raw action probe −0.002–0.052 → centred 0.19–0.52); and checkpoint selection by validation loss picks action-collapsed checkpoints **17/36** times (Dueling WM `2608.06706`) — our nrmse-selected arms are exposed to the same error. **Drift (E-ARCH-SOTA-DRIFT-1):** no primary measures drift as `r(Δz | z_t)` — the field measures rollout-vs-encoded-future divergence; EMA/teacher targets are NOWHERE an anti-drift lever (four primaries use them for stability, none ablates them against drift); every published multi-step rollout loss is TRUNCATED and SHORT (V-JEPA 2-AC differentiates through ONE recurrent step; What-Drives-Success finds K = 2 optimal) — independent support for `--bptt-truncate`; and drift is NOT the capability-limiting quantity (the best proxy for planning success is the ONE-step embedding loss). One admissible lever with a measured effect: JEPA-x `2608.24044` cross-prediction against a privileged physical state, TRAINING-ONLY (inference stays vision-only), drift 0.361 → 0.104. **Encoder (E-ARCH-SOTA-ENC-1):** the sign of freeze-vs-fine-tune is decided by the trunk's strength; frozen DINOv2/v3 **PATCH** tokens suffice for planning-quality prediction (DINO-WM Table 2: 0.90 patch vs 0.44 CLS / 0.20 ResNet) — our refav1 uses patch tokens; fine-tuning CORRUPTS what the trunk carries (Observer Effect `2602.12218`: linear probe on frozen ρ **0.91** vs full fine-tune **0.05**) — a direct argument for refav1's frozen design and against unfreezing v7's trunk casually; NO frozen-trunk world model reports a numeric action-sensitivity metric (the gap we are in); frozen-COMPATIBLE shaping exists with numbers (a bisimulation adapter on frozen DINOv2 patch tokens). **Decodability:** SPEC staged, RESULT not reached before the model limit — the one incomplete theme, queued. All `LAB-*` hypotheses stay PROPOSED in each package's `PROPOSED_HYPOTHESES.md` until the PI reads them; this row records the passes, not their adoption.

⚠️ **C-REFAV1-BF16-OVERFLOW — bf16 AUTOCAST OVERFLOWS THE GRADIENT ON RARE BATCHES: TWO EVENTS IN 3,750 STEPS OF THE CLEAN EPOCH, EACH COSTING ~150 STEPS OF RECOVERY; A FLAG-GATED SKIP GUARD IS TESTED AND SHIPS AT THE NEXT CHECKPOINT (MEASURED 2026-09-03 03:25 Berlin, `refav1-b1-v72-ep2-ema-bf16/train_log.jsonl` rows 900 and 2,750; `drift_check.log` 00:30Z).** Row 900: pre-clip `grad_norm` **2.5e18** (op loss 0.740 on a 0.49–0.65 band), recovered by 950. Row 2,750: `grad_norm` **inf**, batch loss 0.517 → 0.651 → **1.276** → **2.261** (row 2,800, gnorm 24.6) → 0.772 → 0.652; the 10-row operative mean reached 0.696 vs the best window 0.441 (the v3.1 rise guard fired, then cleared once the window passed). The fp32 incumbent's 900 rows peaked at 4.3 (not a controlled comparison: different batches). Mechanism as far as the log shows: an outlier batch (loss 1.07–1.28 before the update) whose backward overflows under bf16; `clip_grad_norm_` on an `inf` total norm scales every gradient by 0 (a finite element → 0, an `inf` element → NaN) and `opt.step()` runs anyway — a momentum-only step at best, a NaN model at worst (the trainer's own non-finite refusal would then end the run and the supervisor relaunch from the last 1,000-step checkpoint). **Guard (D-REFAV1-SKIP-NONFINITE, tested on the mirror):** `--skip-nonfinite` (default OFF, byte-identical old path) drops the batch and counts it (`skipped_steps` in every row) when the pre-clip norm is non-finite — GradScaler semantics for a precision that has no scaler; tests pin: the poisoned step leaves every tensor EXACTLY unchanged, the run continues finite, without the flag the same step NaNs the model and the trainer refuses (SystemExit), and without an event the flag is inert. Ships to Thor as supervisor v4 at the next checkpoint (pod-side switcher, supervisor v3 killed FIRST, `.PRE_SKIP` kept). **Pre-registered fallback:** if non-finite + > 1e3 events exceed 5 per 5,000 steps (drift check v3.2 counts them STICKILY over the run) or a NaN loss appears, restart from the last checkpoint under `--precision fp32 --tf32` at the 6× time cost. The step-1,000 checkpoint read at T1 (paired, 05:30 Berlin) was saved 100 steps AFTER the row-900 event.
