Tests 321 passed / 18 skipped (`-k "refc or anchor"`). ⛔ **STILL GATED (not mine to open):** compute placement (B1's 178 GB lives only on Thor; Thor busy), the PREREG_REFC_V3 §10 amendment (its §8 pins the OLD corpus; §7 cost must be RE-RUN on the launch device — ESTIMATED 2-2.5× from conv area, wide bars), B1 parity registration (NO `physicalai-b1-*` key in `parity_manifest.json` — `--require-parity` cannot enforce four-arms-same-corpus until it lands), and a taniteval RefCV3 adapter for the HIER arm (two-probe absence; the FLAT arm evaluates today via the `core.` strip). Full audit + exact launch lines: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-01-refcv3-training-readiness/READINESS.md`.

✅ **D-REFAV1-STRAT-EXT — PI DECISION 2026-08-31: THE LADDER IS 3.0 s × 2, AND THE STRATEGIC PREDICTOR EXTENDS TO 12 s IN LATENT SPACE.** Verbatim: *"I think you can use 3.0 x2. The strategic layer must have its long horizon predictor in the abstract latent space not only 6 seconds."* ⇒ resolves the D-REFAV1-LADDER provisional (`str_dt` 1.2×5 retired same day) and the open §4b question: **6 s binds the CONTROL OUTPUT; the strategic SUBSPACE predictor does not stop there.** IMPLEMENTED same turn: in-window ladder **0.2/0.6/3.0 = 1:3:15** (MM-E15's corpus ratio, stride 15, targets observed at 3.0/6.0 s); `str_horizon_s = 12.0` adds 2 ticks at **9.0/12.0 s** — ONE continued autoregressive rollout (pinned bit-identical in-window, `test_the_extension_CONTINUES_the_inwindow_rollout_not_a_second_head`), supervised from cached features at 6.0+k·3.0 via the same std→adapter→subspace pipeline, paired inputs refused if unpaired/mis-counted/loss-less. Reaches INSIDE the strategic band [8,30) at the 12.5 s median manoeuvre start. Cache unchanged at 295.9 GiB (ext ticks = cache steps 45/60 on the 0.2 s grid). ⚠️ Bonus defect found by a test going NaN: `future_feats` shorter than one strategic stride sliced to an EMPTY target → **NaN total loss**; now refused by name. Suite: **151 passed**. Evidence: `stack/tests/test_refa_v1_str_ext.py`, `Project Steering/V7_LAUNCH_GATE.md`.

⛔⛔ **D-REFAV1-LADDER — refav1's TEMPORAL LADDER WAS NEVER IMPLEMENTED; FOUND AND REPAIRED 2026-08-31.** MEASURED through the real forward path, not read off the source: the abstracted levels SUBSAMPLE the operative target grid and were sliced `tgt[:, ::stride]` where the correct phase is `[stride-1::stride]`, because `rollout()[:,0]` is the state after ONE step. ⇒ **10 of 10 tactical and 4 of 4 strategic targets were wrong**, each shifted early by a full stride; realised horizons **5.60 s** and **5.00 s** against a config claiming 6.00; the strategic rung's first step was regressed onto the frame **0.2 s** ahead while claiming **1.5 s**, off by 7.5x. Both abstracted levels were therefore trained as **0.2 s predictors** — change #9, the PI's three-planner directive and the structure MM-E15 says the programme most needs, was **not in the model**. ⛔ **A SECOND DEFECT IN THE SAME LINE:** `str_dt 1.5 / op_dt 0.2 = 7.5`, `round -> 8`, so the rung silently ran at **1.6 s** with only **3 of 4** steps inside a 30-step rollout — a rate that is not an integer multiple of `op_dt` is INEXPRESSIBLE, the same root cause as the stage-1 cache grid finding reached from the opposite direction. ⚠️ **WHY 39/39 TESTS WERE GREEN:** `test_change_9_all_three_rates_reach_exactly_six_seconds` asserts `dt * steps == 6.0` **on the CONFIG** — it never built a model, never called `forward`, never asked which frame a prediction was regressed onto. **The advertisement was tested, not the code** — *advertised-but-inert*, one level out — and a second test hardcoding `str_pred.shape[1] == 4` FAILED the repair. ✅ **REPAIRED WITH A NEGATIVE CONTROL:** phase fixed via a single `_stride()` helper (actions keep `[::stride]`: a level consumes the action opening its window and predicts the state closing it); `sanity()` refuses non-integer rate ratios and is now CALLED BY THE TRAINER before the spend (it never was); ⭐ **the model reports its own alignment** (`tac_target_idx`/`str_target_idx`, and `tac_target_s`/`str_target_s` in every log row) and the tests OBSERVE those indices rather than recomputing the slice, because a test that re-derives what it audits passes against the bug as happily as against the fix. Re-introducing `[::stride]` fails **5** tests incl. both load-bearing; restoring passes **12**; refav1 suite **76 passed**, other `refa_v1` importers **64 passed**. ⚠️ **`str_dt` forced to 1.2 x 5** (exact: 1.2/0.2 = 6, 5x6 = 30 = `op_steps`) — 1.5 could not stay, and choosing among the equally exact **1.0x6 / 2.0x3 / 3.0x2** is a PI design call (3.0x2 matches MM-E15's **1:3:15**). ⭐ **RETRACTS my 2026-08-31 claim** that refav1 already implements MM-E15's temporal abstraction: true of the design document, **false of the code**. No refav1 arm has ever run, so nothing published is affected. Evidence: `stack/tests/test_refa_v1_ladder_alignment.py`, `Project Steering/V7_LAUNCH_GATE.md`.

⛔⛔⛔ **D-V7-GATE — v7 TRAINING IS HELD UNTIL THE REMAINING PROBLEMS ARE SOLVED (PI 2026-08-31, BINDING).** Verbatim: *"we should not start training of v7 until the remaining problems are solved."* ⚠️ Recorded as a CHECKABLE list, not a feeling — `Project Steering/V7_LAUNCH_GATE.md`, each item with its evidence, state, and closing criterion. ⛔ **And I may not close any of them**: the 2026-08-26 *"CLOSED, negatively"* on action-conditioning was withdrawn by the PI — *"That was not my decision to make — closing a research direction is a programme call"* — so I report criteria met, the PI closes. **THE FOUR:** **P1 ⭐⭐ no v7 arm has ever beaten its own hold-action control** (the only T1 read: all three arms `LOSES_TO_HOLDV0` on every distance metric, heading ~95° = chance) — this is THE problem; P2–P4 are hypotheses about why. ⭐ the one positive: `copy_detector CLEAN`, echo 0.0000 — v1.x's fake-skill-by-echo traded for honest absence of skill. **P2 the model does not use its actions** — three instruments agree; **eliminated**: the missing objective (MM-E11, ratio FELL 0.40×), zero-init FiLM (it trained), a starved gain (MM-E18, converged); **remaining causes**: (a) horizon UNDER TEST (MM-E19, ~19 h), (b) ⭐ **our "action" is realised motion, not a command (E-DEC-57, r 0.9988) ⇒ a genuine command channel has NEVER been tested**, (c) the representation. **P3 drift** — ⛔ *do NOT* say initialisation is the lever: RETRACTED (E-DEC-60/C164), two arms sharing the same distilled init read 0.669/0.679 while distilled `splitp30k` reads 0.199, so the distilled/scratch split was a CONFOUND. ⭐ What survives is stronger: splitp30k's 3.3× is real and **seed-stable** — the effect is genuine, the attribution was not; the defined-and-unrun experiment is the ablation with init held FIXED. **P4 the horizon ladder** — labels describe events a median 12.5 s ahead; k=60 reaches tactical, **strategic (8–30 s) still unreached** and needs temporal abstraction (1 : ~3 : ~15), not a longer flat rollout. ✅ **Closed today and no longer gating:** the nav join, dead-horizon measurement corruption, path-identified v7.2 artifacts, and the unknown 6 s cost.

✅ **D-NAV-WIRED — THE NAV JOIN IS IMPLEMENTED; PI RESOLVED THE PROVENANCE QUESTION (2026-08-31).** ⭐ **PI, directly:** *"no nav command was generated by a combination of alpamayo cot and ego data. it was reviewed by myself, so use it."* **The data corroborates:** every record carries `cot_source` (chain_of_causation, conflict analysis) and `cot_tokens` (evade_obj, evidence, lane_change, oncoming, traffic_light…) — the Alpamayo CoT is present and rich. ⚠️ What I keyed on was the `nav_command` block's OWN stamp (`provenance: "ego-future"`, `oracle: true`, `_provenance.nav_command = "ORACLE (ego-future) — training input only"`), which records the ego half and not the CoT half — so the field understates its own derivation. **The PI has direct knowledge of the build and reviewed it; that settles it.** ⇒ **IMPLEMENTED:** `--nav-labels` (+ `--nav-semantics`) loads the v7 blob with `allow_oracle_nav=True` and constructs `NavEmitter`; the batch whitelist now emits `nav_token`/`nav_args`. ⭐ **The flag is not an override — it is the RECORD:** `NavEmitter` can reach an arg only through `oracle_nav()`, which checks the MANIFEST, so the stamp lands in `config.json` and no eval can quote a nav arm without it being visible. **Join safety, on the O10/PSG precedent:** clip ids from the cache's sorted `*.v2ep.pt` order, **count-verified against `len(ds_train.episodes)`** and refused on mismatch (*"a silent length mismatch would shift every label by one clip, which no loss curve would show"*), plus an upfront refusal listing any cache clip with no label. `NavEmitter` RAISES on an unmapped episode rather than defaulting — a default would feed one clip's route to another's windows, training a plausible wrong signal. **New preflight:** `--nav-cond` without `--nav-labels` is refused by name (was: a loud `NavTokenMissing` at step 0); the reverse stays legal so the join can be checked before a GPU is spent. ⚠️ **`--nav-semantics` defaults to `t0_constant`**; `decremented` is what a real nav system does but makes the args a FUNCTION OF EGO STATE and must clear information-disjointness first. **197 tests green**, including a contract test updated rather than relaxed: `--nav-cond` alone used to be the only compliant form and is now refused, because the capability it lacked has landed.

⚠️ **SUPERSEDED — D-NAV-ORACLE (kept for the reasoning, not the verdict):** ⛔  — THE NAV JOIN IS BLOCKED ON A PI DECISION, NOT ON IMPLEMENTATION (2026-08-31).** Went to implement the join (the last v7f blocker) and found why it has not landed. **OUR ONLY nav_command IS AN ORACLE, on 100 % of records:** `provenance: "ego-future"` — computed from the ego's **own future path**. `v7_labels.oracle_nav` refuses it by default and names the consequence: *"Feeding it at inference reproduces the flagship-v1 route echo (a bijection of its own input that scored 1.0000)."* ⇒ **the PI's binding directive (nav token mandatory on all three layers) and the corpus are in DIRECT CONFLICT** — the only nav we have is the answer. ⚠️ And it compounds: the ACTION channel is already near-oracle (realised motion, r 0.9988), so an ego-future nav on top adds a second copy of the answer. ⭐ **THREE WAYS OUT, all PI calls:** **(1) ORACLE-CEILING ARM** — `allow_oracle_nav=True`, which stamps the manifest so no eval can quote the arm without it being visible; the result is a CEILING, never a capability claim. **(2) USE `tac_anchor` INSTEAD** — a PREDICTED geometric goal point, admissible, and the code calls it *"the lever the literature shows actually works"*; but it is not the nav-command token the directive names. **(3) SOURCE REAL NAV** — a route over the map rather than over the ego's future (`map.xodr` exists in the NuRec scenes). ⛔ **I did NOT implement the join**: wiring an oracle into the model input is the v1.x echo failure reproduced deliberately, and choosing among the three is a design decision, not a coding task. ⭐ The JOIN MECHANICS are solved and cheap when a source is chosen — the O10/PSG block (`train_v6_staged.py:5443`) is the working precedent: derive `clip_ids` from the cache, **verify the count against `len(ds_train.episodes)` and refuse on mismatch** (*"a silent length mismatch would shift every label by one clip, which no loss curve would show"*), build a per-episode bank, index by `ep_idx`. ⚠️ Related trap already documented: **the `oracle` BOOLEAN is absent on 529/4,719 records (11.2 %)** which are still `ego-future` — a guard keyed on the flag rather than on `provenance` lets 11 % through as non-oracle.

✅ **D-NAV-JOIN — NAV IS COMPLETE AND CORRECTLY GUARDED ON THE MODEL SIDE; ONLY THE DATA JOIN IS MISSING, AND v7f CANNOT LAUNCH WITHOUT IT (verified 2026-08-31).** Traced end-to-end because v7f is gated on it. **PRESENT:** `--nav-cond` flag (`train_v6_staged.py:6671`); a preflight that REQUIRES it per the PI directive (`:7420-7431`, escape hatch `--i-know-this-arm-predates-nav` which records the choice in the run config); `NavConditioner` instantiated in production (`v6.py:4937`) with zero-init and gating; `nav_cond` accepted by `OperativePredictor.forward` **and** carried through the `zh_op_seam` detached path (`v6.py:5648`) so the port's own parameters get gradient without the WM loss training nav backwards through the trunk; `NavEmitter` in `v7_labels.py:435`. ⛔ **ABSENT — the join:** **nothing anywhere produces `nav_token` into a training batch.** MEASURED at four probes: no assignment outside test files; the training batch dict (`v6.py:5825`) is `{frames, actions, v0}`; `tanitad/data/` mentions `nav_token` ONLY in `v7_labels.py` (the label reader), never in a dataset; the trainer reads it via `batch.get(...)` → `None`. ⭐⭐ **BUT IT IS NOT A SILENT LIE — I checked before propagating that, and was wrong to fear it.** `V6Stack.forward` raises **`NavTokenMissing`** by name (`v6.py:5517`, `nav_conditioning.py:200/212`, `v7_labels.py:507-518`) when `nav_cond` is on and the token is absent, and it is pinned by tests in `test_nav_v6stack.py:67`, `test_nav_conditioning.py:67/73`, `test_v7_labels.py:317`. ⇒ an arm with `--nav-cond` and no nav data **hard-fails at step 0 rather than training an advertised-but-inert supervision** — the defect class the same file refuses for S2 at `:7793`. Someone built that deliberately and it works. ⇒ **THE ONLY REMAINING v7f BLOCKER IS THE DATA JOIN:** connect `v7_labels.NavEmitter` → per-window `nav_token`/`nav_args` in the batch, using the v7.2 labels + `clip_index_v7.2_train.json` now on Thor at `/home/nvidia/data/v72/`. The block is enforced MECHANICALLY, not by discipline, so v7f cannot launch early by accident.

⛔⛔⛔ **D-HORIZON-6S — WE TRAIN 1.7 % OF THE BINDING PREDICTION HORIZON (2026-08-31, PI-flagged).** PI: *"we said clearly that the prediction must be up to 6 seconds, this was already included in v1.7"* — **correct, binding, and documented**. §4b (`Reports/2026-08-15-2200-campaign-science-addendum.md` §1.5, *"the clause that reshaped v6"*): *"Every planned trajectory spans up to 6 s… a 60-step control sequence @10 Hz integrated through ONE unicycle rollout 0→6 s"*, and explicitly **"emission heads scale k=20 → k=60"**. Already in code: `v6.py:153` `PLAN_STEPS = 60`, `:155` `HORIZON_S = 6.0`. ⛔ **RETRACTS my D-HORIZON-LADDER recommendation to "restore `--o5-k` to its default 20": k=20 is the SUPERSEDED value and §4b says so in the same sentence.** ⛔ **And the three bands are NOT my discovery — §4b states *"0–2 s is the operative band, 2–6 s the tactical band"*; the labels IMPLEMENT a decided design.** What is new is only the gap. ⭐⭐ **THE MEASUREMENT THAT MATTERS: the h=2 and h=4 predictor heads receive EXACTLY ZERO GRADIENT** — bit-identical across all 8 snapshots, steps 5,000→22,500, Δ `0.000e+00`, while ‖W1‖ climbed 3.73→7.75, **with `w_o1_ctrl 1.0` in force** (so it is wiring, not an objective). ⛔ **SELF-RETRACTED 2026-08-31 — the "0.1 s / 1.7 %" reading was WRONG.** `rollout_transitions` (`metric_dynamics.py:247`) reaches long horizons by applying the 1-step head **autoregressively** — `predictor(ws,wa)[1]`, k times, **full-chain gradient (explicitly NOT truncated BPTT)** — and O5 supervises error at EVERY step. ⇒ the trained horizon is **`o5_k × dt` = 0.8 s = 13 %**, not one tick. ⭐ The zero-gradient fact stands; the consequence I attached to it did not — **I alarmed in the wrong direction**, the exact failure I had been cataloguing all day. The correct, narrower reading: heads 2/4 are **VESTIGIAL** (allocated, computed in `forward`, consumed by no loss) and the damage is **measurement integrity**, not horizon — they produced MM-E10's meaningless h2/h4 ratios, MM-E14's retraction, and then this false alarm. ⇒ **the 6 s fix is `--o5-k 60`, one flag**, and the code was built for it (rollout grad-checkpointing exists *because* "the k=60 roll … killed the run at 37.97 GiB on a 44 GiB A40"). ⛔ MEASURED: **no v7-tiny arm has ever run `o5_k ≥ 20`**. **FIXED 2026-08-31:** the trainer now REFUSES `--horizons` with any entry ≠ 1 at preflight, naming `--o5-k 60` as the 6 s target; `OperativePredictor.trained_horizons = (1,)` is the honest attribute to report; and the constructor stays PERMISSIVE on purpose — **loading is not authoring**, and refusing old checkpoints would have destroyed ~30 banked arms' evidence instead of preventing the defect. `stack/tests/test_horizon_wiring.py` pins it by actual backprop through the rollout (5 tests; 53+86 green). ⚠️ Anticipated a week earlier without a mechanism — `PLAN_TO_THE_GOAL_2026-08-24.md:159`: *"The 0.6 s horizon may be too short for actions to matter at all"* — and it is not 0.6 s, it is 0.1 s. ⭐ Same failure class was CAUGHT once before (2026-08-12: inheriting `plan.max_horizon = 20` *"would have made the 6 s horizon structurally untrainable"*) and missed here. ⚠️ **Scope:** §4b binds the planned trajectory and emission heads; whether `o5_k` must equal 60 is a design question. The sufficient claim is narrower — **a world model trained to imagine 0.1 s cannot support a planner required to roll to 6 s**, and no objective on top supplies reach the trunk never had. ⇒ the prepared `o5k20` arm targets the retired number and must NOT run as specified. Evidence: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-08-30-horizon-ladder/RESULT.md`.

⭐⭐ **D-HORIZON-LADDER — THE ONLY BAND THE PREDICTOR CAN REACH IS THE ONE WITH NO TOKEN SUPERVISION (MM-E15, 2026-08-30).** MEASURED, **train-only** (`s2_labels_v7.2_train.jsonl.gz` md5 `0ff90213…`, 4,572 records / 3,464 manoeuvres): the bands are CONSTANT — `operative_s [0,2]`, `tactical_s [2,6]`, `strategic_s [8,30]` — and are FORWARD horizons, confirmed independently because `a_str.args.by_time_s` lands inside `strategic_s` on **100.0 %** of 1,624 records and **equals a manoeuvre `t_start_s` on 76.7 %**. Median manoeuvre start **12.5 s**. Against the arms (`dt 0.1`, `o5_k 8` = 0.8 s; effective reach ~0.2 s since the predictor collapses past h=1, MM-E10): tactical is **2.5× short of its NEAR EDGE**, strategic **15.6× short at the median** (62× vs effective). ⭐⭐ **The sharpest form:** there is **no operative field of any kind in the schema — 0 of 4,572 carry `a_op`/`g_op`** — so **the only band the rollout partially covers (operative, 40 %) is the one with NO token supervision, and every band that HAS token labels lies beyond reach.** ⚠️ NOT a missing layer: operative is supervised **continuously** by trajectory regression (`ade_dense_m`/`fde_last_m` at T1); `operative_s` states SCOPE, not token coverage. ⛔ **CONSEQUENCE FOR THE THESIS:** selection-by-imagined-consequence needs the imagination to reach the consequence ⇒ the tactical and strategic heads are currently **feed-forward classifiers, not planners**. This retrodicts the T1 S-rate straddling its control on all three arms. ⛔⛔ **AND WE SHORTENED THE LADDER OURSELVES:** Thor's own trainer defaults are `--o5-k 20` (2.0 s — **exactly** the operative band's far edge) and `--o1-k 10`; **every arm ran 8 and 4**. With `--w-o1-ctrl` (default 1.0, every arm ran 0) that is **three flags below their own defaults at once** ⇒ the arms we drew architectural conclusions from were never running the intended recipe. ⭐ Cheap action: restore `--o5-k 20`, one flag, queued BEHIND MM-E11 (never an amendment to a live one-variable prereg). ⚠️ **Recipe direction:** the naive fix (`o5_k 80`) is 10× rollout compute on a predictor that already collapses; the bands argue the hierarchy must abstract **TIME** as well as content — each level rolling out at its own dt, ratios **1 : ~3 : ~15** read off the corpus. Pre-registered as MM-E16. ⚠️ **FOUR counting conventions appeared for "unassigned manoeuvres" (53 / 332 / 440 / the 9 at exactly 30.0) — three right answers to three different questions.** ⛔ **I committed the defect while documenting it:** this write-up quoted **264** (`t >= 30`) and **256** (`t > 30`) over-horizon, both train-only, both mine, differing by exactly the 8 boundary manoeuvres. ⇒ **any count must name its CONVENTION, its CORPUS, and its OPERATOR** — and in every one of the five instances found today across two agents, the thing left implicit was the obvious thing to write, so the fix is **generate + assert, never type**, not more care. Evidence: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-08-30-horizon-ladder/RESULT.md`.

⛔ **D-V72-WIRING — THE TRAINER CANNOT READ OUR LABELS AT ALL, AND THE v7.2 BLOBS ARE NOT WHERE THE TRAINER RUNS (2026-08-30).** PI directive: *"the trainer must use our last v7.2 labels"* — BINDING. Measured from source: the trainer is `stack/scripts/train_v6_staged.py` (⚠️ **not** `stack/tanitad/train/` — my first probe there returned zero and would have read as *no label path exists*); its ONLY label door is `--s2-labels` (`:7315`), whose loader refuses any `schema_version != s2-strategic-v1` (`s2_labels.py:625`); and `tanitad/data/v7_labels.py` is **imported by nothing except its own test**. ⇒ a fully built, fully tested reader for our schema is wired to nothing while the only door opens onto a superseded corpus. ⭐ **The accurate framing is that the trainer CANNOT read ours — a wiring defect on the trainer side — never that it has a claim on different labels**; and pointing `--s2-labels` at v7.2 today fails LOUDLY rather than mis-training, so the guard already works and only the wiring is absent. ✅ **BLOCKER RESOLVED SAME DAY (probe log kept):** the v7.2 label blobs and clip indexes are absent from `git ls-files` on my branch, from the repo working tree, from the DataFlyWheel's worktree dir, from their branch `claude/interesting-tharp-463cf3` (tip `c29c659`), and from `/home/nvidia` on **Thor, where the trainer actually runs**. ⭐ The EVAL6 cache verified immediately on Thor (exactly 6 `.v2ep.pt`), so this is not general doubt about the delivery — it is specifically the labels. **RESOLVED: they are on HF `Sayood/tanitad-v7-training-corpus` (PRIVATE) by design** — multi-MB data artifacts, while the repo carries the code and the manifests; the repo-side files are STAGED not committed, which is why the producing branch's tip predates them. ⇒ **the five probes were the five places a REPO-RESIDENT artifact would be, and it is not repo-resident.** ⭐ The keeper is not *look in more places* — it is that **"pushed and round-tripped" names no address: a remote is not a location, a repo id is.** The deliverable-manifest rule closed it in one message instead of a day. Spec: `Project Steering/SPEC_V7_LABEL_TRAINER_WIRING.md`. ⛔ **THREE MEASURED FINDINGS, TWO OF WHICH CORRECT MY OWN FIRST DRAFT** (301 records of `s2_labels_v7.jsonl.gz`, md5 `e22acf70…`): **(1)** v7 carries **THREE bands, one per abstraction layer** — `operative_s [0,2]`, `tactical_s [2,6]`, `strategic_s [8,30]` — differing on **301/301** records, where the incumbent has ONE applied to every family. ⇒ `_in_band` must be evaluated **PER FAMILY**, or the tactical heads are supervised over the strategic horizon, silently. And the v7 record has **no `valid_window_s` key at all**, so the incumbent silently falls back to the FILE-LEVEL band — not a crash, a plausible wrong answer. ⭐ The three bands are **the hierarchy made mechanical**, not a formatting quirk. **(2)** `args` is a **named dict** (`{within_m, by_time_s}`), not the incumbent's `[8]` vector — the token maps 1:1, the args need an explicit encoder + mask. **(3)** v7 records carry **no `disjointness` stamp (0/201)** while `load_s2_labels` requires one (5 refs) and `v7_labels.py` requires none ⇒ converting would have meant **inventing** a stamp our builder never emits — asserting a property instead of recording one, the exact failure the stamp prevents. A second, independent reason not to convert. ⭐ **Design ruling recorded there:** reuse `S2WindowSupervision` (the JOIN MACHINERY — stable-id lookup, validity band, `n_stack` offset, window census, legacy-id refusal) and **replace only the rows**; *do not convert* forbids emitting an s2-strategic-v1 ARTIFACT, it does not forbid the code path, and reading it that way would force a reimplementation of the collision guard. ⛔ New trap pinned: `s2_labels.py:734` DERIVES the frame offset as `ch // 3 - 1`, valid only while `channels == 3 x n_stack` ⇒ **INVARIANT: nav enters as a TOKEN, never as an image channel**, or the validity band silently shifts. 

⛔ **THE 6 val40 CLIPS HAVE LABELS BUT NO PIXELS (found 2026-08-30).** They were excluded from the epcache by the parity gate, so they carry v7.2 eval LABELS and no `.v2ep.pt`. ⇒ **the eval set silently delivers 141 of its stated 147** — and the 6 the PI specifically asked for are exactly the unusable ones. ⚠️ **The defect is the SILENCE, not the count**: either resolution (build them, or drop them and say 141) is acceptable; a manifest claiming 147 while delivering 141 is not. **RESOLVED: build the 6 into a SEPARATE eval-side cache** (~2 min CPU) so the TRAIN cache stays **provably** val40-clean — two directories is a structural guarantee where one directory plus a filter is only a promise. ⭐ They were correctly NOT marked `excluded`: that flag makes the loader REFUSE those label records, silently undoing the PI's instruction — a release decision wearing a script's clothing. ⛔ **AND THE OVERLAP BECOMES ACTIVE:** until the 6 have pixels the val40 overlap is LATENT (they contribute nothing, so our val curve and the published statistic stay effectively independent). Building them ACTIVATES it, and per D-VAL40-NOLABELS those 6 are val40's ONLY labelled clips — so any label-based comparison then shares its ENTIRE support, not merely 15 %. ⭐ **Also settled: `clip_index_v7.2_{train,eval}.json` ship** (4,572 / 147), ids computed by CALLING `stable_episode_id` rather than reproducing it — vindicated by census: the LEGACY 16-bit ids **collide on 258 of 4,572 clips across 128 ids**, so a reimplementation would have joined those wrong and the loader's cross-check would have refused the WHOLE artifact. *Call the function, do not reproduce it* — here about being wrong immediately, not drifting later. ⚠️ **NAMING RULE EXTENDED:** three label releases are live — v7 `ee44875916`, v7.1 `93b49273`, v7.2 (train `0a586fe8` / eval `bffc9df5`) — and **a v7.2 number must name WHICH SIDE as well as which blob.** |
| D-B1-100H | **The 100 h target is BACKLOGGED, not cancelled (PI 2026-08-30).** The 2026-08-06 design targeted 18,000 clips / 100 h; the run stopped at 4,729 / 26.3 h. PI: *"put the missing tasks in the backlog of the DataFlyWheel and inform it. It is NOT urgent, since we will first use the 26 h as a starting point and at the same time try to understand the impact of the data-related aspects, criteria etc."* | **BACKLOG — DataFlyWheel, not urgent** | Remaining work when it resumes: the **road-class stratum** (the design's own unfilled WORK ITEM, and the axis governing our 88.7 %-longitudinal gap), the **strata cell in the selection manifest**, and the ~13,300 unlabelled clips. Cost anchor from the design: 18,000 clips ≈ **42 A40-days** at ~200 s/clip for the 5-task battery. |
| D-B1-CRITIQUE | **PI ruling 2026-08-30 on the six B1 criticisms: IMPLEMENT #2 (record the STRATA CELL in the selection manifest); BANK the rest for future work.** *"Let's start with B1 and then we will understand the impact of further improvements."* | **RULED — #2 active, 1/3/4/6 banked** | Banked, not dropped: road-class stratum (→ D-B1-100H), the ~2.5× unchosen hour-of-day swing, the country equal-weighting choice, and 1-of-7 cameras. ⭐ **#5 (Alpamayo trained on PhysicalAI-AV ⇒ labels possibly memorised) is CLOSED BY PI DECISION**: *"I don't think it is a problem, because the quality is good there and this is what matters."* ⇒ the `ALPAMAYO_SCREENING` UNRESOLVED flag is resolved as **ACCEPTED RISK, PI-ruled** — record it that way rather than leaving it open, and do not re-raise it without new evidence about label QUALITY specifically. |

|---|---|---|
| slow urban 7–29 km/h | 67.6 % | 37.2 % |
| urban/arterial 29–50 | 32.4 % | 34.5 % |

⛔⛔ **AND 88.7 % OF OUR ORACLE GAP IS LONGITUDINAL.** The parity corpus — the one every cross-arm comparison in the programme runs on — contains **NO high-speed driving at all**, so it **cannot in principle exhibit or fix** the defect that dominates our error budget. The *unselected* corpus (B1, an availability accident) preserves **26.3 %** of that regime. | **MEASURED — the parity corpus cannot address the longitudinal defect; B1 IS a designed corpus with one stratum unfilled** | ⚠️ **SCOPE (PI, 2026-08-30): r0 is HISTORY.** B1 is the relevant corpus and the r0 speed-gate finding is retained as provenance for published parity numbers only, not as a live criticism. | ⛔⛔ **CORRECTED 2026-08-30 — MY "B1 WAS NOT SELECTED" CLAIM WAS WRONG, AND THE PI'S MEMORY WAS RIGHT.** B1 **IS** the product of a designed, PI-ordered stratified selection. `…/2026-08-06-alpamayo-augmentation/DESIGN.md` records the directive verbatim — *"select **100 hours** of well-distributed PhysicalAI-AV data (highway/urban/intersections, day/night, structured/unstructured…)"* — with the plan *"stratified 18,000-clip selection (**equal-weight cells over road-class × day/night × country**, proportional fill, seed 0, manifest committed)"*. ⚠️ **The error's mechanism, worth naming: the DataFlyWheel correctly measured B1 == the Alpamayo record set and I read that as "no selection" — but THE SELECTION HAPPENED ONE LEVEL UP**, in choosing which clips to send for labelling. A set that equals its parent is not unselected; its parent was selected. *(Class: an absence established at the wrong level of the pipeline.)*

**What survived of the design, MEASURED:** ✅ **country stratification WAS applied** — B1 is near-uniform ~5–6 %/country where a random draw would be 50.75 % US, roughly 60 σ from chance, so it cannot be accidental. ⛔ **road-class stratification was NOT** — the design flagged it *"No road-type column found yet (highway/urban/intersection): **WORK ITEM**"* with an egomotion fallback; equal weight over three road classes would give ~33 % highway and B1 has **12.0 %**. ⚠️ **The run was TRUNCATED at ~26 %** — 4,729 clips / 26.3 h against the 18,000 / 100 h target (the design's own estimate was **42 A40-days**, so compute is the likely cause). ⛔ **THE DECISIVE TELL: the design specified a "selection manifest (clip_id + STRATA CELL)" and the shipped manifest has only `clip_id` + `t0_us` — the strata cell was never recorded**, so the corpus cannot state its own composition and the stratification is only inferable from the country distribution. ⚠️ **CORRECTION TO MY OWN BRIEF (mine):** I told the DataFlyWheel the dataset card's tags were *"a ready-made stratification axis nobody has used"*. **They do not exist in the shipped metadata** — two probes with full column dumps: `data_collection.parquet` has FIVE columns (country, month, hour_of_day, platform_class, radar_config) and `feature_presence.parquet` has 36 SENSOR flags; there is **no weather, road_type, traffic_density or surface column anywhere**. Those axes must be DERIVED from pixels or Alpamayo text — a build, not a join. ⇒ what IS free today: **speed regime per clip from egomotion** (the axis that discriminates the two corpora most sharply), plus country and hour-of-day as catalogue joins. Other measured skews: **United States is 50.75 % of the parent corpus and 6.40 % of B1 = 0.13×**, an eight-fold under-representation (B1 is near-uniform ~5–6 % per country, so half the parent is squeezed into a sixteenth of ours); Austria 3.12×, Netherlands 3.00×, Sweden 2.56× over; `hour_of_day` swings **~2.5×** across the day with 19–21h at 1.32–1.46× and 08–10h at 0.56–0.71×, none of it chosen. ⛔ **CONSEQUENCE FOR THE PROGRAMME: any longitudinal claim measured on the parity corpus is measured on a corpus from which the failing regime was removed by design.** Evidence: `…/incoming/2026-08-30-data-distribution-audit/`. Parity untouched — any stratified sample is a NEW NAMED ARM, never a re-selection within `e438721ae894`/`f09e44db`. |

## Standing rules with their origin (never re-litigate silently)

| rule | origin |
|---|---|
| Open-source research-licensed models/data are USABLE; augmented sets go PRIVATE on paid HF; HF Pro GPU/SSH is first-class compute | PI 2026-08-22 |
| Labels may use ego; inference is vision-only | PI 2026-08-03 |
| Goal input admissible; must not carry situation-classifier output | PI 2026-08-03 |
| Four metric families, never pooled; tier stamps on every number | PI 2026-08-02 / EVAL_DOCTRINE |
| ≥5 parallel streams; never idle; a report is not work | PI 2026-08-03 / 07-29 |
| Bank the primary or the deliverable is incomplete | PI 2026-08-18 |
| Thresholds calibrated on REAL references; statistic named, not just quantity | C131/C132, 2026-08-22 |

### D-DATA-ALPA-FULL (2026-08-23) — the augmentation is 26.3 h in FIVE tasks, and four of them were unused

**MEASURED** (`stack/tanitad/data/alpamayo_records.py`, `records.parquet`
sha256 `ecae276db9969de1…`, 23,644 rows):

| task | rows | coverage | status before today |
|---|---|---|---|
| `meta_action` | 4,729 | 100 % | ⚠️ I stated it was "NOT reachable locally" |
| `cot` | 4,729 | 100 % | the only field the pipeline used |
| `auto_labeling` | 4,729 | 100 % | unused |
| `vqa` | 4,722 | 99.9 % | unused |
| `grounding_via_vqa` | 4,411 | 93.3 % | unused — **2D boxes** |

**Corpus = 4,729 clips x 20 s = 26.3 hours.** The PI's recollection of "~20 h
augmented and pushed to HF" is CONFIRMED; the dataset is
`Sayood/tanitad-alpamayo2-augmentation`. My "the amount of data with Alpamayo is
limited" described the 801-clip s2 subset — a restriction I imposed — and was
reported as a property of the dataset. See RETRACTION_LOG C142.

**What each source is good for (MEASURED, not assumed):**

* `meta_action` **lateral** — 69.9 % vs 41.6 % chance, **lift x1.68**, n=1,584.
  The best lateral corroborator. (The "at chance" verdict was n=16.)
* `meta_action` **longitudinal** — monotone and magnitude-bearing:
  Strong Decel −5.88 · Gentle Decel −1.73 · Maintain +0.03 · Gentle Accel +1.93
  · Strong Accel +6.00 m/s. **Cohen's d = 1.59** accel vs decel.
  ⚠️ `Stop` is a STATE (v0 = 1.11 m/s, 66.7 % already below 1 m/s), NOT a
  deceleration — mis-mapping it is most of why a naive 4-way agreement read
  30.7 %.
* `auto_labeling.ego_vehicle_motion_analysis` — **97.1 % parse** into typed,
  time-stamped segments. Value is TIMING and VOCABULARY (EVADE / TAKE_EXIT /
  YIELD / CREEP), NOT axis agreement (55.2 %, lift only x1.16).
* `critical_components_analysis` — names the forcing object, and
  **`type: none` on 853 clips is an explicit clean negative**, the first source
  in the programme that separates "no obstacle" from "no statement".
* `grounding_via_vqa` — **the fusion gate**. 93.3 % of clips carry boxes
  (Car 2,292 · Pedestrian 1,079 · traffic light 889), so a CoT token can be
  checked against image-space perception instead of believed.
* `vqa` — ⛔ **NOT dense enough to label from.** A sampled bank: ~5 questions per
  clip, so any single question covers ~0.3 % of the corpus. Spot-checks only;
  treating it as a dense feature would fabricate coverage.

⚠️ **The anchor differs: Alpamayo `t0_us` = 5.1 s, our s2 anchor = 8.0 s.** Every
cross-source statistic published before today compared moments 2.9 s apart.

## D-REFCV4-NAV-WIRING — nav reaches ONE layer, not three (2026-09-04, Arch+Inference)

**Full design + audit: `Project Steering/DESIGN_REFCV4_NAV_WIRING.md`.** Every
claim below carries `file:line` there, re-pinned by CONTENT against the current
`refc.py` (2,477 lines) / `refc_v3.py` (1,270 lines) — both moved during the audit.

**ASSERTED (MEASURED, source read).**
* The CORE's two decision heads are strictly `f(pooled)` — vision only. `route_head
  = nn.Linear(feat, 3)` on `pooled` (`refc.py:1955`, called `:2278`); `tac_in =
  pooled` because `tactical_speed_input = False` (`refc_v3.py:447`). **Nav reaches
  neither.**
* Nav enters at exactly ONE place: the measurement encoder, `meas_in = ([v, nav] +
  ...)` (`refc.py:2273-2275`), and `m` reaches the DECODER (`:2364`) and, through
  `cond_proj(m)` (`:1503`), the selector's anchor confidence. **Nav is present in
  the operative path and absent from both decision heads** — the inversion of the
  hierarchy's thesis.
* `route_head`'s output reaches NOTHING downstream: `graft_route = False`
  (`refc.py:2341-2342`, `:1643-1644`). It is a dangling aux.
* E13's nav injection into `ctx` is a rebind of the HOOK'S LOCAL parameter
  (`refc_v3.py:861`); the hook returns only `{maneuver_logits, target_latent}`
  (`:968-969`), so the decoder still receives the un-nav'd `ctx` built at
  `refc.py:2190`. **The nav-augmented `ctx` never leaves the hook.**
* The live 40,284-step arm runs with `--nav-from-v7` OFF, `--goal-str`/`--graft-lan`
  OFF and `--ego-state-inject` OFF (verified in TWO files: `refcv3_b1_launch.sh:126-142`,
  `sup_refcv3.sh:62-76`). ⇒ **`str_goal_head` has NO DIRECT LOSS** — it trains only
  by backprop through the tactical FiLM — and the arm is **v3, not v4**.

**REFUTED — the standing claim was VACUOUS.** *"The route head is not a nav echo"*
rests on `paired_true_minus_shuffled_accuracy` = **0.0 [0.0, 0.0]**, n=3,622 win /
128 eps (`taniteval/results/refcv3-40284-openloop.ARM.json`), with the shuffle
changing the token on **49.89 %** of windows. The head is `nn.Linear(feat, 3)(pooled)`
and **cannot echo an input it never receives**. It is a pin on the WIRING, not a
finding about the model (C109 class: a probe that cannot fire proves nothing).
⇒ Re-label wherever quoted.

**ROOT CAUSE of the dead nav channel — a CORPUS interaction, not a wiring bug.**
`nav_command` needs `NAV_MIN_STEPS = 150` future poses (15 s) and reads to 250
(25 s) — `refb_labels.py:72-73` — while `*.v2ep` episodes are **199 frames = 19.9 s**.
Only windows with `t0 <= 4.9 s` (**28.5 %**) can ever emit left/right; on a 74 %-straight
corpus that lands at ~5 % non-follow, i.e. the MEASURED **94.6 % `follow`**
(`refc_v3_train.py:1127-1128`).

**CORRECTED SCOPE — two numbers were quoted outside their scope.**
* `45,466 / 168,910 = 26.92 %` is a **REF-A v1** figure (`refav1_loader.py` grid,
  `dt = 0.2`). The literals are banked NOWHERE (two mechanisms); the nearest artifact
  is a 20-episode slice. **REF-C v3's own in-band coverage is 41/172 = 23.84 %**
  (DERIVED on its grid), cross-checked against the banked **24.0 %** at
  `RESULT-refcv3-40284-stratified.md:361`. At +-4.0 s: **47.09 %**.
* The oft-quoted **72.08 %** reach-clamp kill rate is **REF-C-XL step-30k, 256
  anchors, 881 windows**. refcv3-40284 is 128 anchors / 4,823 windows / 6 s path.
  ⛔ **The refcv3 reach telemetry is NOT BANKED AT ALL** — `reach_frac_*` is computed
  at runtime and the shipped `refcv3_arm.py` never reads `sel_tele` (two probes).

**CORRECTED — "the selection surface is unstructured" is wrong.** MEASURED on the
arm: **50 of 128** anchors ever selected, entropy **2.8425 / 4.8520 nats (0.586)**,
modal anchor #57 at 14.8 %, oracle agreement 0.5652, and `goal_gate` has **OPENED to
0.1744** (score_absmean 4.0686). What is missing is any RANKING TERM expressing the
commanded manoeuvre: `r_terms` is **empty** (`graft_route`/`graft_goal`/`graft_cons`
all False). Oracle selection is worth **-0.0751 m [-0.0884, -0.0618]** = **17.0 %**
of deployed ADE.

**BOUNDING FACT — this work is necessary and provably NOT sufficient.** Oracle
selection (-0.0751) + oracle nav (-0.0239) = **0.099 m**, still short of the
**0.1423 m** by which the trivial hold-action control beats the model.

**CORRECTED — a 30 s strategic label IS constructible.** `s2_geom_emit_v7.py:52,59`
already emit `STRATEGIC_S = (8.0, 30.0)` and `LOOKAHEAD_S = 30.0`, and **1,882 train
manoeuvres start in [8, 30) s** (`band_census.json`). The reach exists because the
emitter reads the RAW clip (`recording_span_s` mean **139.7 s**) while the trainer's
`*.v2ep` cache is a **19.9 s excerpt**. **The horizon is not the constraint; EVENT
DENSITY is:** ≥1 event on **47.75 %** of clips, **≥2 (the PI's over-next) on 18.72 %**
= ~12.4 % of windows; median inter-event gap **8.9 s**. 43.0 % of windows have a
fully-observed 30 s horizon; the rest are right-censored at 38 s − NOW and must be
MODELLED as censored, not as negatives.

**HYPOTHESIS (pre-registered primary).** `t_bin` accuracy, not `p_man`. The v7.2 nav
command is a **per-clip constant** — it can echo *whether* a routing manoeuvre happens
and cannot echo *when*. Timing is the one strategic quantity a nav-conditioned head
cannot fake, and it is what the PI's *"within its time frame"* names. Controls that
must read known values: constant-only, nav-shuffled, `os_navzero`.

**RETIRES on execution: C-NAV-SOURCE-DIVERGENCE** (this file, above) — W0
(`--nav-from-v7` ON) aligns refcv3 to the v7.2 token and closes the contradiction.


## D-REFC-DDAUDIT — REF-C vs DiffusionDrive v1/v2 implementation audit (2026-09-05, Arch+Inference)

Evidence for every row: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refc-vs-diffusiondrive-audit/RESULT.md` (+ `raw/mechanism_check.json`, 201 windows / 3 clips, refcv3 @ 40,284, window-level paired SEs — a MECHANISM probe, not a benchmark number). Sources: arXiv 2411.15139v3 + `hustvl/DiffusionDrive@9b52ed0`; arXiv 2512.07745v1 + `hustvl/DiffusionDriveV2@1cd12a1`.

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFC-DDAUDIT-1 | ⛔ **REF-C's "truncated diffusion" decoder is NOT a diffusion model.** MEASURED, source + run: no noise schedule, no anchored Gaussian, no x0/DDIM update, no sampling at inference (`refc.py:1596-1603`: `x ← x_in + off`, eval noise `zeros_like`); train-only N(0, 0.1 m) jitter measured σ = 0.0994 and, injected at eval, moves the fan by **0.062 m** against **8.41 m** of deterministic classifier-pass offset (ADE −0.005, SE 0.006, n = 201). The timestep table has 3 rows and saturates: trace for steps = 8 is `[0,1,2,2,2,2,2,2,2]`. ⇒ It is an unrolled 3-pass anchor-refinement regressor; the registry wording should say so | **SUPPORTED** | RESULT.md §1.1 #1–#12, §2 Q1–Q4 |
| D-REFC-DDAUDIT-2 | ⭐ **The refinement passes are load-bearing but not convergent.** 0 → 2 passes: ADE 0–6 s 6.415 → 3.927 m (paired −2.488, SE 0.105), oracle-in-fan 3.942 → 1.721 m; beyond the trained 2: −0.20 m at k = 8 on 47 % of windows while ADE 0–2 s WORSENS 0.651 → 0.901 m; per-pass displacement 5.68 / 4.28 / 3.30 / 2.82 / 2.04 m (ratio 0.72–0.85, never → 0); endpoint spread collapses 103.7 → 81.7 (k = 2) → 42.0 m (k = 8) — mode collapse controlled by the pass count. The timestep embedding is live: forcing t_idx = 0 costs +1.48 m (SE 0.06) | **SUPPORTED** | RESULT.md §2 table + Q2/Q5/Q6 |
| D-REFC-DDAUDIT-3 | ⛔ **Selection is decided before refinement — the scored object is not the emitted object.** `sel_idx_base` (t = 0 confidence + priors, reach-masked) is identical to the steps = 0 winner on **201/201** windows for every steps ∈ {0,1,2,3,4,8}; only the v3 goal-scorer graft (reads fan geometry) moves the winner (10.9–14.4 %). DiffusionDrive scores the fan it emits (`argmax(poses_cls[-1])` on `poses_reg[-1]`). The in-source D1/S1/S1b notes were correct and the flags to fix it (`sel_refined`, `sel_score_emitted`, `sel_ce_reach`) are OFF in refcv3/v4 | **SUPPORTED** | RESULT.md §1.4 #28, §2 Q7; `refc.py:1613-1636`, `refc_select.py:11-31` |
| D-REFC-DDAUDIT-4 | ⭐ **The cross-attention is implemented but is not DiffusionDrive's.** Ours: content MHA from 128 anchor queries to 160 PV conv tokens + FiLM(measurement) (`refc.py:1096-1113`, `:1502-1509`). Theirs: trajectory-coordinate-indexed BEV grid-sample attention (samples the BEV map AT the candidate's waypoints) → agent-query MHA (30 detections) → ego-query MHA → FFN → per-layer AdaLN timestep modulation (`transfuser_model_v2.py::CustomTransformerDecoderLayer`, `blocks.py::GridSampleCrossBEVAttention`). We have 0 of the 3 grounded attentions because we have no BEV, no detector, no LiDAR | **SUPPORTED** | RESULT.md §1.3 #19–#26 |
| D-REFC-DDAUDIT-5 | **TransFuser is MISSING by construction (vision-only rule) and V2 changed nothing in the backbone** (`diffusiondrivev2/transfuser_backbone.py` 19,898 B vs 19,896 B; config adds `num_groups = 4` only). Structural loss: no metric frame for the decoder to index, no agents to attend — the LONGITUDINAL (headway/TTC) and TACTICAL grounding | **SUPPORTED** | RESULT.md §1.5 |
| D-REFC-DDAUDIT-6 | **V2's additions (intra-anchor GRPO G = 4, inter-anchor truncated advantage with collision → −1 and `clamp(min=0)·(r ≥ r_GT)`, scale-adaptive multiplicative noise, coarse-to-fine PDM-sub-score selector) are MISSING; the prerequisite — a stochastic denoising policy with a log-probability — is absent from our decoder.** `stack/tanitad/rl/` holds the estimators and the reward/selector-disjointness audit (sibling's RL-readiness deliverable) | **SUPPORTED** | RESULT.md §1.6 #37–#43 |
| H-DDA-1 | **Waypoint-indexed PV sampling (DD's grid-sample attention in perspective view, zero-init gate) beats a random-location control on oracle-in-fan AND the longitudinal family on the v7-tiny ladder.** Pre-registered both ways: separation ⇒ grounding is the lever, schedule base-size; none ⇒ PV tokens already carry the geometry, do not spend on BEV lifting yet | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §4 rank 1 (E-DDA-1) |
| H-DDA-2 | **Scoring the emitted fan (`sel_refined + sel_score_emitted + sel_ce_reach`, 0 params) closes the selection gap on the ladder.** E-SEL-0 showed the UNSUPERVISED refined readout ranks worse; the arm is the supervised one. Closes ⇒ turn on in refcv4c; no change ⇒ selection is not binding at this fan quality, stop re-litigating S1 | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §4 rank 2 (E-DDA-2) |
| H-DDA-3 | **A DD-faithful sampler (diffusers DDIM 1000/scaled_linear, x0 prediction, train t ~ U[0,50), infer from t = 8 in 2 steps, σ ≈ 0.9/0.7 m around the anchors) improves oracle-in-fan at equal N or raises the DD diversity score without ADE loss.** Gain ⇒ sampling adds coverage AND is the GRPO prerequisite; none ⇒ the vocabulary ceiling (D-REFCV4-VOCAB1) binds, keep the deterministic refiner and rename it honestly | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §4 rank 3 (E-DDA-3) |
| H-DDA-4 | **refcv4b's v0-conditioned vocabulary is a real prior: classifier-pass ‖offset‖ mean < 1 m and oracle-in-bank ≈ oracle-in-fan at 40 k** (refcv3 reads 8.41 m). Zero-cost readout from the refcv4b dump; still metres-scale ⇒ the head is doing the vocabulary's job, revisit N / grid density first | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §4 rank 4 (E-DDA-4) |


## D-DDV2 — DiffusionDriveV2: the released code vs the paper, results, and transfer to REF-C (2026-09-05, Research Lab / Arch+Inference)

Evidence for every row: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-diffusiondrive-v2-analysis/RESULT.md` (+ `raw/2512.07745v1_text.txt`, `raw/ddv2_src/` Apache-2.0 copies of every upstream file read with sha256s in `raw/ddv2_fetched_sha256.txt`, `raw/ddv2_noise_magnitudes.{py,json}`). Sources: arXiv 2512.07745v1 (lib `2512.07745`) read 17/17 pp; `hustvl/DiffusionDriveV2@1cd12a1` with `diffusiondrivev2_model_rl.py` and `diffusiondrivev2_model_sel.py` read IN FULL; v1 counterparts at `hustvl/DiffusionDrive@9b52ed0`. Builds on `D-REFC-DDAUDIT-1..6` / `H-DDA-1..4` (above, commit `7c67b3d`) and on `D-RL-REFCV3` / `H-RL-REACH-1` / `H-RL-THRESH-1` — cites them, does not restate them. Zero GPU; our code was NOT re-audited.

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-DDV2-CODE-1 | ⭐⭐ **THE REWARD THE PAPER NEVER DEFINES IS NAVSIM's PDM SIMULATOR SCORE, AND THE `collision` PREDICATE OF Eq. 10 IS `NC ≠ 1 OR DAC ≠ 1`.** `TrajectoryHead.__init__` instantiates `PDMSimulator` + `PDMScorer` from `default_scoring_parameters.yaml` (`_model_rl.py:741-753`; 4 s @ 10 Hz LQR-tracked rollout, replayed agents) in a 16-process pool; `_pairwise_scores` (`:75-117`) rebuilds `NC × DAC × (5·EP + 5·TTC + 2·C)/12` per candidate with **EP re-normalised against the GT's own progress**; the −1 branch fires on `no_collision != 1` **or `drivable_area != 1`** (`:900-902`), the paper says collision only. Upgrades the 2026-08-29 RL-library research's "HYPOTHESIS, NOT ASSERTED — the reward is PDMS" to PUBLISHED-CODE; the paper still does not say it | **SUPPORTED (PUBLISHED-CODE @ `1cd12a1`)** | RESULT.md §1.2 (d), (f) |
| D-DDV2-CODE-2 | ⭐⭐ **THE RELEASED EXPLORATION IS TWO SCALARS PER ANCHOR (ALONG-TRACK SCALE, LATERAL SCALE) — THE ADDITIVE "η = 1 DDPM" NOISE IS MULTIPLIED BY ZERO, AND BOTH σ FLOORS BIND EVERYWHERE.** `std_dev_t_add = 0.0` in both branches (`:640,:643`); `prev_sample = prev_sample_mean · (1 + 0.04·ε_{long,lat})` (`:646-666`). ESTIMATED from the diffusers `scaled_linear` schedule (`raw/ddv2_noise_magnitudes.json`): σ_t(η=1) = 0.0083–0.0120 at every rollout step t ∈ {18,…,2}, below both floors (0.04 exploration, 0.10 likelihood) ⇒ exploration std is a constant **4 % of the coordinate** (0.40 m at x = 10 m, 1.60 m at 40 m; 0.08 m at y = 2 m) and the likelihood std a constant **5.0 m (x) / 2.0 m (y)**; the noise schedule plays no role in the RL stage | **SUPPORTED (PUBLISHED-CODE + ESTIMATED)** | RESULT.md §1.2 (b), §1.3 #2–3 |
| D-DDV2-CODE-3 | ⭐ **THE ESTIMATOR IS PLAIN REINFORCE WITH A MISMATCHED LIKELIHOOD, A GT-ANCHORED BAR AND A GLOBAL IL PULL — NOT GRPO AND NOT A TRUST REGION.** `per_token_loss = −exp(logp − logp.detach())·A` (`:1096`, ratio ≡ 1, one update per rollout; no clip, no KL, no reference policy — confirming the sibling's derivation); the log-likelihood is an isotropic Gaussian over 16 coordinates with σ = 0.1 evaluated on samples from the 2-scalar multiplicative law (`:668-675`) — **not the sampler's density**, so `refcv3_adapter.py`'s "surrogate Gaussian, stated limit" is the same class of object as the published estimator; positive advantage ONLY where `reward > reward_GT − 1e-6` (`:892-893`, absent from Eq. 10); intra-anchor `(r − mean_G)/(std_G + 1e-4)` with G = 4 (`:886-889`, `_rl_config.py:37`), discount `0.8^(T−i−1)` over T = 10 rollout steps (`:801,:926-932`); IL = L1 of **all 80 chains** to the GT at every step/layer, λ = 0.1 or **1.0 when no sample beat the human** (`:1104-1118`); v1's `poses_cls` is computed, untrained and unread; **trunk + agent/BEV heads frozen and in eval mode, only `_trajectory_head` trains** (`_rl_agent.py:61-67`) | **SUPPORTED (PUBLISHED-CODE)** | RESULT.md §1.2 (a), (c), (d), (e), (j); §1.3 #3–7 |
| D-DDV2-CODE-4 | ⚠️ **THE VALIDATED V2 DECODER IS ONE CASCADE LAYER, THE COORDINATE NORMALISATION CHANGED, AND THE INFERENCE FAN IS 800 CANDIDATES.** `CustomTransformerDecoder(diff_decoder_layer, 1)` in both V2 model files (`_rl.py:733`, `_sel.py:844`) vs v1's 2 (`transfuser_model_v2.py:430`), cold start loaded `strict=False` so v1's second layer is dropped — the paper's "matching the diffusion decoder size used in DiffusionDrive" (§5.2) is not what ran; `norm_odo` = `x/50, y/20` (`_rl.py:755-772`) vs v1's `2(x+1.2)/56.9−1, 2(y+20)/46−1`, so the t = 8 start noise is **σ 1.58 / 0.63 m** (ESTIMATED) vs the 0.90 / 0.73 m the cold start was trained with; inference = 10 groups × 20 anchors → 2 DDIM steps → 3 multiplicative augmentations U(0.1, 0.3) → **800 candidates** → coarse scorer (1 layer, d 512, 16 heads, FFN 2048, self-attention among candidates, five PDM-sub-score heads recombined by the PDMS formula `_sel.py:1461`) → **top-32** → fine scorer (3 layers) → argmax (`_sel.py:1390-1523`); the official-eval `return {"trajectory": …}` is commented out (`:1491`) and `run_pdm_score_fast.py` scores the picks against the metric cache. Selector training: 2 groups × 20 → 2 aug U(0.1,0.2) → 120 candidates + ≈ 32 GTRS-vocabulary trajectories (`16384.npy[:, ::5]`, dropout 0.99) with precomputed PDM sub-scores; BCE per sub-score (NC 0.5 → 0, comfort masked) + `MarginRankingLoss(0.05)` on EP pairs × 2; 20 epochs, generator frozen | **SUPPORTED (PUBLISHED-CODE)** — ⚠️ **UNVERIFIED** which of the two eval paths produced Tab. 1's 91.2 | RESULT.md §1.2 (h), (i), (k); §1.3 #1, #8 |
| D-DDV2-CODE-5 | **THE COMMON PARTS ARE v1's CODE — consistency of implementation is high.** The V2 model files import v1's package for the backbone, config, blocks and loss (`_model_rl.py:6-15`); `_rl_config.py` = v1's config + `num_groups: int = 4` (`_sel_config.py` differs only by `num_groups = 8`, diff-verified); same 20 k-means anchors, same `DDIMScheduler(1000, scaled_linear, sample)` (+ `steps_offset=1`), noise at t = 8 everywhere, inference `[10, 0]` in 2 steps, same query/time embeddings, same decoder-layer order (grid-sample BEV → agent → ego → FFN → AdaLN). V2 is a PHASE on v1's released 88.1 checkpoint (`docs/train_eval.md`): stage I 10 epochs / lr 2e-4 / wd 1e-4 / cosine 10 % warmup / batch 512 on 8 × L20; stage II 20 epochs, same optimiser | **SUPPORTED (PUBLISHED-CODE)** — `D-REFC-DDAUDIT-5` stands | RESULT.md §1.1, §1.2 (j) |
| D-DDV2-RES-1 | **THE PUBLISHED RESULTS, WITH THE CAVEAT THAT TRAVELS WITH EACH.** NAVSIM v1 navtest PDMS **91.2** (NC 98.3 · DAC 97.9 · TTC 94.8 · Comf 99.9 · EP 87.5) vs DD 88.1 — **+3.1 = EP +5.3, DAC +1.7, NC/TTC/Comf ≤ 0.1**; leaderboard at publication (R34): DriveSuprim 89.9, WoTE 88.3, DIVER 88.3, DD 88.1, ARTEMIS 87.0, Hydra-MDP++ 86.6, Hydra-MDP 86.5, GoalFlow 85.7 (V2-99: Hydra-MDP 90.3, GoalFlow 90.3 — both below V2's R34). NAVSIM v2 **navtest** EPDMS **85.5** (single-stage; NOT navhard) with EC 91.0 vs the field's 77.0. Raw fan (Tab. 3, pre-selector, 20 trajs): diversity 42.3 → 30.3 (**−28 %**), PDMS@1 93.5 → 94.9, **@10 75.3 → 84.4**. Authors' control (Tab. 9): DD + V2's selector 89.1 (NC 98.2 → 97.2, TTC 94.7 → 92.2, EP 82.2 → 86.8) ⇒ **selector +1.0 in the hackish direction, RL ≈ +2.1**. Ablations on a reduced-epoch 90.1 base, never additive: multiplicative noise +0.4, intra-anchor +0.9, inter-anchor truncation +0.6, coarse-to-fine +0.2, rank loss +0.2. Never ablated: the reward, the ≥ GT mask, the all-modes IL pull, the 1-layer decoder, G, the 800-fan. **No interval on any number; no other benchmark.** ⚠️ NAVSIM's "closed-loop" = 4-s LQR rollout vs NON-REACTIVE replayed agents (NAVSIM's title, V2 ref. [10]) — **self-action open loop (T1 class) under the 2026-09-02 ruling** | **SUPPORTED (PUBLISHED-PRIMARY; competitors PUBLISHED-SECONDARY-via-2512.07745)** | RESULT.md §2 |
| D-DDV2-ORDER-1 | ⭐ **H-DDA-3 (the DD-faithful sampler) IS DEMOTED from "the GRPO prerequisite" to "the prerequisite for the paper's per-step chain objective and for diversity claims".** Because the released mechanism never uses the diffusion chain's randomness (D-DDV2-CODE-2), a per-anchor (a_long, a_lat) scale policy over refcv4's DETERMINISTIC fan is the code-faithful port. Decision ladder: **E-DDA-3b → E-DDA-2b → E-DDA-6 → E-DDA-3**. Two code-only ingredients are missing from `stack/tanitad/rl/` — the ≥ GT bar (`advantage.py`) and a 2-scalar noise mode (`refcv3_adapter.py::sample_offsets`) — escalated as integration request #1 | **DECIDED (ordering) — no arm launched** | RESULT.md §3.1, §3.4, §5 |
| H-DDA-5 | **V2's RL gain is a longitudinal-scale effect, and on our fan the same mechanism moves the ALONG-track error and not the LATERAL error.** Basis: the 2-scalar exploration (D-DDV2-CODE-2), V2's gain profile (EP +5.3, DAC +1.7, NC/TTC/Comf flat), and `D-REFCV3-AXIS1` (92.2 % of `os − ha` along-track). **E-DDA-3b** (pre-registered, P-RC21 rig, 4060, minutes per arm): per-anchor Gaussian over (a_long, a_lat) scales of the emitted offset, σ floor 0.04, likelihood σ 0.1, G = 4, REINFORCE, truncated advantage **with** the ≥ GT bar, IL pull on all modes 0.1/1.0, trunk frozen; reward `NC(replay) × (5·TTC/headway + 2·comfort + 5·progress)/12` after `PREREG_D_SAFE_CAL`; controls = P-RC21's (deliberate-regression, w = 0). Outcomes: along-track oracle gap shrinks with CI excluding 0 AND collision-vs-replay does not rise ⇒ the lever transfers on the predicted axis, schedule E-DDA-3; along shrinks but lateral worsens ⇒ scale-only exploration too coarse, stop; neither ⇒ V2's map half (DAC) was load-bearing, park RL until a lane signal exists (AlpaSim `map.xodr`) | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §3.1, §3.4 |
| H-DDA-6 | **Grouping by the v7.2 factored tactical class (or refcv4b's (a_lon, a_lat) cell) is at least as good as grouping by anchor, and both beat pooled grouping** — V2 Tab. 5's +0.9 for "never compare intents" is the published prior; refcv4b's 13 × 9 grid IS that partition. **E-DDA-6**: three grouping keys on the E-DDA-3b rig, pooled = V2's ✗ arm as control; readouts per family. The class must be the grouping key OR a reward term, never both (echo firewall, RESULT.md §3.3) | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §3.4 |
| H-DDA-7 | **A sub-metric selector trained on rule-based targets selects for the rule, not for ADE: it improves the LONGITUDINAL family and may worsen 0–2 s ADE** — V2 Tab. 9's NC/TTC ↓, EP ↑ signature. **E-DDA-2b** (dev box, ~1 day, no sampler needed): V2-style scorer on refcv4's emitted 128-fan + 2 multiplicative augmentations U(0.1, 0.2) + ~1 % foreign bank (refcv3's synthetic vocabulary); heads {collision/TTC, comfort, progress, tactical-consistency (v7.2)} = the four families instead of NAVSIM's five; BCE + `MarginRanking(0.05)` × 2; 1-layer coarse → top-32 → 3-layer fine; targets = rule-based sub-scores vs the `obstacle.offline` replay join; control = E-DDA-2's 0-param flags; deliberate-regression = progress-only head. Outcomes: selection gap (`D-REFCV3-40284a`'s 0.0751 m) closes with CI excluding 0 AND fan-collision does not rise ⇒ adopt for refcv4c; closes but collision rises ⇒ the Tab. 9 hackish direction, refuse; no change ⇒ selection is not binding, bank. **The ADE-only reading is refused in advance** | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §3.2, §3.4 |

## D-RL-READY — REF-C RL readiness, resumed and measured (2026-09-05, Production & Optimization / Deploy FlyWheel)

| id | claim | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-RL-READY-1 | ⭐ **The refcv3 RL-stage experiment is RUNNABLE-WITH-FIXES — and the fixes are applied, tested and staged; nothing is launched.** The Training FlyWheel's preparation is real and substantial (`stack/tanitad/rl/` 8 modules / 149 tests green; pilot line P-RC21 with EXIT C; the DeployFlyWheel's driver `rl_refcv3_min.py` + chain `launch_refcv3_rl_min.sh` + 8 fit clips + lead block, all found stranded off-Drive). Resumption found and fixed four defects, each MEASURED: **(1)** the harness strict-load REFUSED the published refcv3 @ 40,284 (md5 `b1ed7075…`) because refcv4-b added the persistent buffer `core.decoder.anchor_controls` (`refc.py:1168`), which is read only when `v0_conditioned` — a false refusal that would have blocked EVERY dev-box eval of the shipped weights; now tolerated for that one inert key and recorded (`refcv3_arm.load_model`); **(2)** the driver's `--mode verdict` read a record key (`families_paired_deltas`) that `paired_openloop.py` never writes — the exit was structurally NULL whatever the data (the false-green class in a false-null costume); rewritten against the real `rec["families"][FAM]["metrics"][KEY]` schema; **(3)** the reward held the lead STATIC at its t0+0.2 s sample (a follower reaches that point after one time-gap → "collision"); `rewards._collision` gained a TIME-ALIGNED moving-lead branch + pinning test, `--lead-mode track` is the default; **(4)** a gate that raised without writing its measurement (the preflight now banks a partial report on any failure). Verdict on the three RL surfaces: **(b)** Gaussian perturbation of the offset head — what the predecessor's script assumes — is the only surface both implemented and runnable on the frozen base (trainable **9,206,032 / 107,032,901 = 8.60 %**, 4 selector tensors excluded); **(a)** a policy gradient over the anchor-confidence softmax is 0 new code but trains the SELECTOR (doctrine-forbidden; ceiling = the 0.0751 m selection gap) and, under this reward, would select toward the floor; **(c)** a DD-faithful sampler then true GRPO needs a RETRAINED base (H-DDA-3) — a training run, not a post-train | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** — STAGE 0 of the chain passes on the dev-box 4060; ⛔ STAGES 1–5 gated on `LAUNCH_APPROVED=1` | `TanitAD Research Lab/Deployment & Optimization/Research/2026-09-05-refc-rl-readiness/` — `RESULT.md` §2–§7, `SPEC.md`, `launch_refcv3_rl_min.sh`, `raw/preflight.json`, `raw/humanflag_fit8.json`, `raw/probe_step0_divergence.json`, `raw/patch_{1,2,3,4}*.py` |
| D-RL-REWARD-FLOOR-1 | ⭐⭐ **The DEFAULT RL reward does NOT flag the human driver — but it PREFERS THE TRIVIAL CONSTANT-VELOCITY PATH TO THE HUMAN'S OWN FUTURE on 78.3 % of lead windows.** MEASURED 2026-09-05 (`rl_refcv3_min.py --mode humanflag`, 0 GPU, 318 lead windows / 4 episodes of the fit8 RL-fit clips, NON-PARITY, T0): contact fires on the human **0.000** (static AND track lead), TTC veto **0.047**, headway < 0.5 on 7.5 % — the H-RL-THRESH-1 class is CLEAR on this corpus (no human window follows closer than 2.93 s, median 5.8 s; median v0 2.33 m/s). But the composed reward of the hold-v0 straight path (the `ha0` floor) is ≥ the human's on **78.3 %** (track; 70.8 % static; means 0.899 vs 0.861), frozen ≥ human on 27.7 %. ⇒ The reward's improvement direction from the demonstration is toward `ha0` — which the base's own eval ranks **0.2304 m WORSE** than the model (`os` − `ha0`, separated). This is the pilot's "the reward pays for drift / makes the fan blander" (RESULT_P_RC21_SWEEP §3) measured at the demonstration level, before any arm. ⚠️ Not yet a claim about the outcome; it is the committed PREDICTION of `H-RL-MIN-1` | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** — re-measure on the 120-clip fit set in STAGE 0 (the chain does it) | `…/2026-09-05-refc-rl-readiness/raw/humanflag_fit8.json` (`summary.track.hold_v0_scores_at_least_human`), `RESULT.md` §3.3 |
