| D-RL-REWARD-RANK-1 | ⭐⭐ **THE COMPOSED RL REWARD IS *NOT* DISQUALIFIED AT SOURCE: WITHIN A WINDOW'S OWN FAN IT RANKS ENVELOPE-VIOLATING CANDIDATES *LOWER*, AND THE ONE TERM THAT DOES THE OPPOSITE IS `progress`.** MEASURED 2026-09-05 (`stack/scripts/rl_reward_envelope_rank.py`, one forward on the dev-box 4060, **240 EVAL windows / 121 episodes / 30,720 candidate scores**, per-window Spearman ρ over the 128 emitted candidates, episode-cluster bootstrap n_boot 4,000; T0 instrument probe, NON-PARITY not applicable … the EVAL corpus). A group-relative advantage IS a ranking, so this is the question that decides admissibility. **ρ(DEFAULT reward, envelope) = -0.5367 [-0.5581, -0.5138]** (n = 228 windows / 116 episodes; 12 windows UNDEFINED -- the flag had no variance -- reported, not averaged to zero), `kamm_over` **-0.5790**, `peak_g` **-0.4603**, `ttc_below` **-0.3544**, `contact` **-0.6326**, `off_reach` -0.0756 -- every one SEPARATED and NEGATIVE. ⭐ **Attribution: `progress` is the only component with a POSITIVE correlation to violation** -- ρ(progress, peak_g) **+0.2900** [+0.1811, +0.3966], ρ(progress, ttc_below) **+0.4697**, ρ(progress, contact) **+0.3286**, all separated -- while ρ(comfort, peak_g) is **-0.9695** (an almost perfect inverse ranker of friction load) and ρ(feasibility, envelope) -0.5389. ⛔ **AND IT PRICES THE OBVIOUS FIX AS WORTHLESS:** quadrupling `feasibility` moves ρ(envelope) -0.5367 → **-0.5377** (nothing), whereas REMOVING `progress` moves ρ(peak_g) -0.4603 → **-0.7306** and ρ(ttc_below) -0.3544 → **-0.5632**. Controls all read their known values: self-correlation **+1.0000** exactly; the constant score **UNDEFINED on 240/240**; the random score **-0.0138** [-0.0250, -0.0022], the probe's own residual bias, below which \|ρ\| is not interpretable -- the effects above are ~39× it. Practical: the reward's own argmax picks an envelope-violating candidate on **5.4 %** of windows against the fan's base rate of **88.8 %** and the MODEL SELECTOR's **10.8 %** → the rule reward is a *better* feasibility ranker than refcv3's trained scorer. ⚠ `peak_g` is the FINITE-DIFFERENCE load (the exact control-rolled `flyability.friction_load` needs `controls` and cannot be applied to a free-waypoint fan); it under-reports LEVELS by 1.21-1.85×, but a RANK correlation is invariant to a monotone under-report -- which is why this probe reads ranks | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** -- the `H-RL-VETO-1` disqualification branch does NOT fire | `TanitAD Research Lab/Deployment & Optimization/Research/2026-09-05-veto-only-fan-safety/raw/reward_envelope_rank.json` (`panel`, `practical`, `per_window`), `raw/reward_envelope_rank.log`, `raw/fan_bank_base_240w.npz` (the 240×128 fan + every component + every flag, banked so ANY future reward design is scorable at **0 GPU**) |
| D-RL-VETO-EXPLICIT-1 | ⭐ **THE VETO IS NOW A TYPED, RECORDED FIELD -- A ZERO-WEIGHT CONTROL IS AN ACTUAL NULL AGAIN, AND VETO-ONLY IS A PRODUCT ARM.** `posttrain.rl_objective` keyed the collision veto on `"collision" in spec.weights` (KEY MEMBERSHIP, true at weight 0.0) and the TTC channel on no config at all, while `advantage.py:146` pins vetoed candidates at -1 OUTSIDE the centring → a constant reward gave a VETO-ONLY advantage at full strength (`D-RL-FANSAFE-2`; `ctrl_const` measured `veto_rate_mean` **0.0897**, `final_loss` **-1.863**, 14/57 metrics moved). FIXED 2026-09-05: the mask is composed by **`posttrain.veto_mask()`** from `PostTrainConfig.veto_enabled` / `veto_collision` / `veto_ttc`, written into every run's `config.json` by `to_dict()`, and it returns an all-False tensor (never `None`) when off so a `veto_rate` of 0.0 is still LOGGED -- a channel that reports 0.0 is evidence, one that reports nothing is not. `reg_echo` is pinned to `veto_collision=False`, which is what the old key-membership code gave it, so the banked arm still reproduces. New arms `veto200` / `veto2k` (zero reward, veto ON -- the product) and `ctrl_null` (zero reward, veto OFF -- the null `ctrl_const` was supposed to be). Pinned by **`stack/tests/test_rl_veto_explicit.py` (8 tests)**, which asserts BOTH directions and carries a same-breath control proving the constructed scene really contains the violation being vetoed; full RL suite **172 passed** | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** -- closes escalation (2) of `.../2026-09-05-refc-rl-readiness/RESULT.md` §12.7 | `stack/tanitad/rl/{config,posttrain}.py`, `stack/tests/test_rl_veto_explicit.py`, `stack/scripts/rl_refcv3_min.py` (`ARMS`, `make_cfg`), `.../2026-09-05-veto-only-fan-safety/raw/patch_p2{,b}_*.py`, `raw/patch_p3_veto_arms.py` |
| D-RL-READOUT-CTXRANK-1 | ⛔ **`readout()`'s R1/R2 WERE A BATCH × BATCH OUTER PRODUCT: EVERY WINDOW'S FAN WAS SCORED AGAINST EVERY WINDOW'S LEAD.** MEASURED 2026-09-05, reproduced in three lines. `rl_refcv3_min.reward_ctx()` shapes every scene fact for the TRAINING tensor `[B, N, G, S, 2]` (three leading axes) and `readout()` handed it a `[B, N, S, 2]` FAN (two). Right-alignment then broadcasts `lead_path [B,1,1,S,2]` against `traj [B,N,S,2]` to **`[B, B, N, S, 2]`**, and `v0 [B,1,1]` against `along [B,N]` the same way, so `_collision` / `_headway` / `_progress` returned `[B, B, N]`; the driver indexes `r1[j]` and means over a `[B, N]` slab of the wrong windows' leads. Readout batch = 4. **SCOPED, not blanket-voided: (a)** TRAINING is unaffected -- `make_sample_fn` really does hand a three-leading-axis tensor; **(b)** the PRIMARY fan-safety metrics are unaffected -- `FS.score_paths` is called with correctly-ranked `lead5 [B,1,5,2]` and `v0 [B]`; **(c)** the AFFECTED numbers are exactly `R1` and `R2` in every `readout_*.json` of the 2026-09-05 panel and their paired deltas (incl. *"R1 -0.0058, the stage did not improve its own objective out of sample"*, which is withdrawn as stated). FIXED: `reward_ctx(..., cand_dims=1)` plus a POSITIVE shape assertion in `readout` that raises instead of returning a number. ⭐ **Corroborated by the first arm after the fix**: `R1` 0.5876 → **0.6071** and `R2` 0.03612 → **0.02923** while `R3`, `R_FAN`, `R_REACH`, `R_ORACLE` reproduce the banked base to every printed digit -- exactly the two metrics the defect could touch and nothing else. Root-cause CLASS: the `df` / Thor `free` / `step_s` / units family -- **a correct quantity computed in the wrong SCOPE, returning a number rather than an error** | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** -- drafted for `RETRACTION_LOG.md` | `stack/scripts/rl_refcv3_min.py` (`reward_ctx`, `readout`), `.../2026-09-05-veto-only-fan-safety/raw/patch_p1a_readout_ctx_rank.py`, `SPEC.md` §3 |
| H-VETO-FAN-1 | ⭐ **PRE-REGISTERED, both outcomes committed BEFORE any arm ran: the VETO ALONE (zero reward, DDv2's constraint channel only, `w_anchor` 1.0) makes refcv3's emitted fan measurably MORE feasible, and the gain CLEARS THE RIG'S OWN SEED-REPLICATE NOISE FLOOR.** Five arms on the frozen refcv3 @ 40,284 (md5 `b1ed7075…`), 120 train-split B1 v7.2 fit clips, readout on the SAME fixed 120 EVAL windows (`Random(1234)`), dev-box 4060 only: `ctrl_null` (veto OFF -- **G1: `veto_rate_mean` must read EXACTLY 0.0**), `veto200_s0/_s1`, `veto2k_s0/_s1`. ⛔ **A separated CI is NECESSARY, NOT SUFFICIENT (`H-ESTIM-SEED-1`)**: `floor(m) = \|paired delta(veto_s0, veto_s1)\|` and a lever is quotable only if BOTH seeds separate, in the SAME sign, with `min(\|d_s0\|,\|d_s1\|) > floor(m)`. Per-metric separation floors from each metric's own quantum AND UNITS (1e-4 for rates, **1e-3 g** for `fan_peak_g_mean`, 1e-3 m/s for `fan_v_mean_2s_spread`); any metric whose base sits below its own floor is stamped **UNDETECTABLE-DOWNWARD**, never null. **SUCCESS** = `fan_peak_g_mean` negative and quotable AND at least one of `top32_infeasible` / `sel_infeasible` negative and quotable, AND T1 `ade_m` not regressed beyond the same floor. **FAILURE** = the gain does not clear the replicate floor, or ADE regresses past it. SECONDARY (T1, four families, per family, never pooled): committed prediction **NULL** -- a pure constraint with the trust region intact should not move driving quality | **OPEN -- pre-registered 2026-09-05, arms running; result rows follow in the same package** | `TanitAD Research Lab/Deployment & Optimization/Research/2026-09-05-veto-only-fan-safety/SPEC.md` §4-§7, `raw/run_veto_arms.sh`, `raw/analyze_veto.py` |
| D-RL-VETO-DOSE200-1 | ⭐⭐ **THE VETO ALONE MAKES refcv3's EMITTED FAN MEASURABLY MORE FEASIBLE — `fan_peak_g_mean` **-0.093 / -0.116 g**, REPLICATED ACROSS THREE RUNS, CLEARING BOTH FLOORS — AND IT COSTS A LITTLE FEASIBILITY ON THE SELECTED PATH.** MEASURED 2026-09-05 (`veto200`: reward weights ALL 0.0, `veto_enabled=True`, `w_anchor` 1.0, lr 1e-5, **200 steps**, two_scalar noise, G 4, batch 2; 214.7 s and 150.8 s on the dev-box 4060, 0 pod-hours). Readout: the SAME fixed **120 EVAL windows** (`Random(1234)`), 79 episodes, 36 lead windows, paired episode-cluster bootstrap n_boot 4,000. `veto_rate_mean` **0.0897 (s0) / 0.0980 (s1)** — the constraint channel really fired. **Guards:** G1 `ctrl_null` `veto_rate_mean` **0.0000 exactly**; G2 every BEFORE readout **bitwise identical**, max abs diff **0.000e+00** over 120 w × 65 metrics; G3 both seeds present. ⭐ **`fan_peak_g_mean` clears all three hurdles** — separated at both seeds, same sign, 4-5× the seed-replicate floor (0.0229), and the zero-information arm drifts it **+0.134 the OTHER way**, so the drift cannot explain it; the direct veto-minus-`ctrl_null` contrast is **-0.227 g**, separated. Level: **4.1809 → 4.088 / 4.065 g, -2.2 to -2.8 %**, from a channel carrying NO reward at all. ⛔ **AND THE REPLICATE KILLED FOUR METRICS A SINGLE SEED WOULD HAVE SHIPPED**, including the one the previous package proposed shipping on: `top32_infeasible` reads **-0.0064 (s0) vs -0.0011 (s1)** against a seed floor of 0.0053 — not reproducible across training runs; same for `top8_kamm_over`, `top32_envelope`, `top8_infeasible`. ⛔ **THE COST IS REAL AND IS REPORTED:** `sel_peak_g` **WORSENED** and clears the same floors (**+0.0155 / +0.0080 g** on a base of 0.1947, i.e. **+4 to +8 % on the path the car drives**), with selector agreement **1.000 / 0.992** — the fan moved under a selector that never changed its pick, so the selected candidate itself got more aggressive. A safety claim quoting only `fan_peak_g_mean` would be true and misleading. ⭐ **A THIRD run was already banked:** `ctrl_const` (same seed, same flags, hours earlier, `veto_rate` 0.0896826 vs 0.0896924 — GPU non-determinism, not a config difference) reads `fan_peak_g_mean` **-0.0859**; across the three runs the metric is **-0.0859 / -0.0929 / -0.1158**, every one negative. But `top32_infeasible` spans **-0.01431 to -0.00105 over those same three runs, TWO OF WHICH SHARE A SEED** — so this rig's run-to-run noise is not only a SEED effect, and `H-ESTIM-SEED-1`'s replicate requirement needs to be read as *runs*, however they differ | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** — `fan_peak_g_mean` only; every other feasibility metric is WITHIN-NOISE. SPEC §7's committed SUCCESS text needs `fan_peak_g_mean` **and** one of `top32_infeasible`/`sel_infeasible`, so the formal PRIMARY exit at this dose is **FAIL** | `TanitAD Research Lab/Deployment & Optimization/Research/2026-09-05-veto-only-fan-safety/RESULT.md` §5; `raw/veto_verdict_veto200.json` (all 57 metrics, both floors, the contrast), `raw/run/s{{0,1}}/veto200/arm_summary.json`, `raw/run/s0/ctrl_null/arm_summary.json` |
| D-REFC-OFFSET-FEAS-1 | ⭐⭐ **refcv3's FAN INFEASIBILITY IS NOT IN THE VOCABULARY — THE DIFFUSION DECODE MANUFACTURES IT, AN 8.56× BLOW-UP IN PEAK FRICTION LOAD.** MEASURED 2026-09-05 at **ZERO GPU** (`stack/scripts/bank_vs_fan_feasibility.py`): the FROZEN anchor bank (`core.decoder.anchors`, a fixed `[128, 8, 2]` path set read straight out of the checkpoint) scored by the SAME `fan_safety.score_paths`, on the SAME 240 EVAL windows, with the same `v0` and lead track as the emitted fan. **`envelope` 0.0156 → 0.8877** (+0.8721) · **`kamm_over` 0.1953 → 0.8406** · **`peak_g` 0.4808 → 4.1131 g (+3.6323, 8.56×)** · `contact` 0.0239 → 0.0249 · mean \|emitted - bank\| per waypoint over the 2 s prefix **9.29 m**. ⚠ **Read the `off_reach` row before concluding the offset is gratuitous:** the bank is **77.8 %** off-reach and the emitted fan only **10.8 %**, because the bank is a FIXED path set that mostly does not match the window's own speed — adapting it IS the decode's job. ⛔ CORRECTED SAME TURN (RETRACTION #28): the path bank → fan is THREE stages (`refc.py:1590` bank · `:1640` `x = bank + offset` classifier pass · `:1676` `x = x_in + off` refinement loop), and `out["offset"]` is the CLASSIFIER-PASS offset only — so "the offset head" named one stage of several. The TABLE is unaffected: `roll_bank` returns `anchors[None].expand(...)` byte-identically for a fixed vocabulary and refcv3 carries no `--anchor-v0-cond`, so `core.decoder.anchors` IS the bank the decode started from (verified from source). The finding is that **the adaptation is paid for in the friction envelope, at 8.56×**. ⭐ **Three consequences.** (1) The RL veto moves `fan_peak_g_mean` by **-0.10 g against a +3.63 g blow-up** — it addresses **~2.7 %** of the gap; RL post-training of the offset head is the right SURFACE and the wrong SIZE of instrument. (2) The high-value build is a **FEASIBILITY-AWARE OFFSET** — a reparameterisation or penalty keeping the ROLLED path inside the envelope, applied where the offset is produced rather than after it. (3) It explains `D-RL-FANSAFE-1`'s paradox with no RL panel at all: `oracle_sel` (best-ADE) is the LEAST drivable candidate (0.1105 vs `os` 0.0865) because matching the human closely is exactly what large offsets buy. ⚠ LIMIT: `peak_g` is the finite-difference load on free waypoints and under-reports by 1.21-1.85× — **on BOTH sides**, so the RATIO is the robust quantity and the levels are lower bounds | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** — opens a feasibility-aware-offset work item for Arch+Inference | `…/2026-09-05-veto-only-fan-safety/RESULT.md` §6, `raw/bank_vs_fan_feasibility.json`, `raw/fan_bank_base_240w.npz`, `stack/scripts/bank_vs_fan_feasibility.py` |

## D-REFCV4B-EGODROP2 / H-EGODROP-PRED — the ego-dropout burden MEASURED on the trained model (2026-09-05, Master Mind, T1 early-training diagnostic)

Evidence: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refcv4b-egodrop-burden/RESULT.md` + `raw/EGODROP_{9500,5000}.json` (4,823 windows / 141 EVAL episodes, paired kept/withheld forwards of the SAME checkpoint on the SAME windows, paired episode-cluster bootstrap n_boot 2,000; controls: `ha` 0.29962 / `ha0` 0.67229 reproduce the banked 0.2996 / 0.6723; `ha0` jerk ≡ 0; withheld ≡ zeroed-ego forward at 0.0 m).

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFCV4B-EGODROP2 | ⭐⭐ **THE WITHHELD-ROW BANK IS A REAL, LONGITUDINAL BURDEN ON THE TRAINED MODEL — AND IT IS SHRINKING.** At step 9,500 the selected-anchor offset is **6.18 m [4.80, 7.81]** on withheld rows vs **0.64 m [0.61, 0.67]** on kept (×10; 6.03 of the 6.18 m along-track); the residual after the offset is **2.99 m [2.53, 3.58]** vs **1.07 m [1.01, 1.14]** (the head recovers 61 % of the 7.61 m need, vs 5 % of the 1.125 m kept need). Four families at 2 s, withheld vs kept: speed MAE **1.859 vs 0.500 m/s** (×3.7), along-track **2.155 vs 0.384 m** (×5.6), cross-track 0.215 vs 0.183 (×1.2), curvature 0.023 vs 0.022 — **only the longitudinal family moves**, which is the axis a fixed-speed roll gets wrong. Selected-trajectory jerk **3.65 vs 2.42 m/s³** (+51 %, non-overlapping; lateral jerk identical 2.18 vs 2.11) against GT **0.86** and `ha` 0.03. ⭐ **Trend 5,000 → 9,500:** withheld offset 13.39 → 6.18 m, residual 5.59 → 2.99 m, oracle−selected ADE −11.07 → −3.86 m, own 2 s speed prediction on withheld rows MAE vs GT **4.91 → 1.84 m/s**; straight-ahead share 45.5 → 20.4 % withheld — the model is learning speed from vision. ⚠️ Early read, stated plainly: at step 9,500 the *kept* selected trajectory beats `ha` on **no** family at 2 s (speed 0.50 vs 0.25, along 0.38 vs 0.23, cross 0.18 vs 0.12) — 24 % of training, a diagnostic, and the bar refcv3 never cleared at 40 k | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05, T1, EARLY-TRAINING DIAGNOSTIC, step 9,500 of 40,284)** ⇒ sharpens `D-REFCV4B-EGODROP1` from a MODEL-FREE ceiling to a MODEL-INCLUSIVE measurement; **NOT an abort reason** (the burden is converging on its own; the vision-only rule is doing what it is for); STRATEGIC family absent with reason; TACTICAL and distance-keeping (n = 1,153) present in the raw JSON | `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refcv4b-egodrop-burden/RESULT.md` §1–4; `raw/EGODROP_9500.json` (`offset.levels`, `offset.paired_w_minus_k`, `smoothness.native8`, `four_families_2s`), `raw/EGODROP_5000.json` |
| H-EGODROP-PRED | ⭐ **Rolling the withheld-row bank at the model's OWN predicted 2 s speed (vision-derived, `g_tac`) removes most of the burden without any ego input.** MODEL-FREE ceiling on the anchor prior, withheld rows, step 9,500: fixed 10 m/s **7.61 m** → own prediction **2.00 m** → true-v0 LEAK bound 1.125 m — **86 % of the gap recovered**, and improving with training (5.03 m at step 5,000). Admissible on its face (no label, no future) but a SELF-CONDITIONING loop (the bank depends on a prediction from the same trunk) ⇒ carries a shuffle-the-predicted-speed intervention control at eval (the gain must vanish) and is pre-registered on the v7-tiny ladder before any launch. Alternatives priced: keep 10 m/s (costs the 2.99 m residual and +51 % jerk on half the rows); speed-bucketed reference ⛔ inadmissible if keyed on true v0 (the leak in disguise); lower `ego_dropout` — last resort, it is the lever `H-ECHO-8` measured to make an arm read the scene | **OPEN — pre-registered for refcv5; NOT a live-run change.** Outcomes committed: separated gain on the longitudinal family with the shuffle control reading ~0 ⇒ adopt for refcv5; no separated gain, or a gain that survives the shuffle ⇒ the bank geometry is not the binding constraint / the loop is echoing its own prior, keep the fixed roll | `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refcv4b-egodrop-burden/RESULT.md` §5; `raw/EGODROP_9500.json` (`option2_own_predicted_speed`, `paired_predw_minus_fixed`, `paired_predw_minus_truev0`, `by_v0_band`) |


## D-NAVCOMP — the nav-COMPLIANCE metric, and the refcv4b post-training hierarchy prereg (2026-09-05, Benchmarks & Eval)

**Evidence:** `TanitAD Research Lab/Benchmarks & Evals/Research/2026-09-05-nav-compliance-metric/RESULT.md` (+ `raw/refcv4b-9500-openloop.json`, `raw/navcomp_refcv4b-9500.json`); instrument `taniteval/taniteval/nav_compliance.py` (28 tests) wired into `taniteval/tools/refcv3_arm.py`; registry v2.6.0 (`strat.nav_compliance`, `strat.nav_compliance_ctrl_shuffle`, `strat.nav_compliance_ctrl_zero`); prereg `Project Steering/PREREG_REFCV4B_HIERARCHY_EVAL.md`. PI rulings implemented: *"we are not evaluating the nav command itself, we are evaluating the fact that the model is following the nav command in consistency to the strategic goals"* · *"let it do it after finished training"* · *"Use the 30 s label as the strategic supervision; keep nav as a conditioning input."*

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-NAVCOMP-1 | ⛔ **The old strategic route metric is UNFALSIFIABLE and is now REPLACED as the criterion, not repaired.** It compares a head to a label that is a bijection of the fed nav token (D-REFAV1-ROUTE-LABEL-IS-THE-NAV, 141/141): copying the input scores 1.0000 and no behaviour can lower it. The replacement scores BEHAVIOUR — the emitted 6 s path's terminal heading, the strategic goal bearing `g_str`, and the SELECTED anchor's geometry — against the COMMAND on informative windows (turn inside the plan; for `g_str`, within 160 m), and is DECIDED by the intervention pair: compliance with the TRUE command must DROP under nav-SHUFFLE and nav-ZERO on the same windows (paired episode-cluster bootstrap); the changed subset must follow the FED command. A rate that does not drop is scene coincidence, and the nav-blind controls (`ha0_ext`, `ha`) read that floor on the same windows. **It cannot be satisfied by an echo, because a path is not a token** — pinned by a deliberate-regression test in which a perfect token-echo head reads Δ = 0.0 exactly on the behavioural readout | **SUPPORTED (instrument DELIVERED + STAGED; the token-echo arm and every known-value control read their values)** | `taniteval/taniteval/nav_compliance.py`, `taniteval/tests/test_nav_compliance.py` (28 pass), `products/P7-TanitEval/CRITERIA_REGISTRY.json` v2.6.0 (+7 tests in `tools/tests/test_criteria_check.py`: a rate without its two controls is TWO named violations) |
| D-NAVCOMP-2 | ⚠️ **`refcv3_arm.py` evaluated v4 builds in a regime they never trained in.** `run_dump` passed no `ego_state`, so an `ego_state_inject` model (refcv4b) had its goal path starved while the core still saw `v0`. FIXED additively (v3 builds byte-unchanged; manifest `ego_state_fed`). Any refcv4b number from the unpatched harness is inadmissible | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED, source: `run_dump` had no `ego_state` argument; fixed 2026-09-05)** | `taniteval/tools/refcv3_arm.py` (marker `feed_ego`) |
| D-NAVCOMP-3 | ⭐ **EARLY-TRAINING DIAGNOSTIC (refcv4b @ 9,500 of 40,284 = 23 %, T1\* `os`, 1,860 win / 141 eps, stride 10, grid 6 s; NOT a result about the run):** on the **141 imminent windows / 22 episodes** the emitted PLAN follows nav — compliance with the TRUE command **0.390 [0.303, 0.472]** → **0.291** under shuffle (paired Δ **+0.099 [+0.041, +0.167]**, separated) and **0.298** under zero (Δ **+0.092 [+0.027, +0.169]**, separated); the SELECTED ANCHOR likewise (Δ_shuffle **+0.106 [+0.049, +0.170]**); the fan holds a reach-surviving compliant candidate on **100 %** of those windows. But **`g_str` is BIT-IDENTICAL under all three conditionings** (0.792 / 0.792 / 0.792, Δ exactly 0.0000 on n = 485 / 43 eps) → the zero-init E13 `nav_to_str` edge has not moved. **Seam reading: PATH_FOLLOWS_WITHOUT_GOAL** — nav reaches the operative layer through the measurement encoder, not the strategic decision: the D-REFCV4-NAV-WIRING inversion, measured on refcv4b early in training. ⚠️ And the plan complies LESS often than a constant-(a, κ) extrapolation of the measured t0 state (`ha0_ext` **0.418**, `ha` 0.390 on the same windows): nav-following exists and is weak. Known values: `ha0` = 0.0000, nav-blind deltas = 0.0000, GT = 0.986, label time base *relative* 22/23 vs *absolute* 1/39; τ_plan = 0.2608 rad (Youden J 0.81, GT + label only). Four families @ 6 s: `os` 3.517 m ties `ha` 3.560 (n.s.) and beats `ha0` 4.955 (−1.438, separated) | **MEASURED (ours), EARLY-TRAINING DIAGNOSTIC — decides nothing in the prereg; shows the instrument runs and its controls read** | `…/2026-09-05-nav-compliance-metric/raw/refcv4b-9500-openloop.json` (`refcv3.strategic.nav_compliance`), `raw/navcomp_refcv4b-9500.json`; ckpt md5 `ab4cd79e35b92bf70d6e2b80ac053e51`, step verified by content; dev-box RTX 4060 |
| H-NAVC-1 | The FINAL refcv4b's emitted plan FOLLOWS the nav command: plan-compliance drops under nav-shuffle AND nav-zero, separated, ≥ 0.10 absolute, on imminent windows | **OPEN — pre-registered, both outcomes committed** | `PREREG_REFCV4B_HIERARCHY_EVAL.md` §2–§4 |
| H-NAVC-2 | The FINAL refcv4b's strategic goal `g_str` FOLLOWS the nav command (the zero-init `nav_to_str` edge opens): `g_str`-compliance drops under shuffle AND zero, separated, ≥ 0.10 | **OPEN — pre-registered; the step-9,500 reading is its FAILURE mode (PATH_FOLLOWS_WITHOUT_GOAL) at 23 % of training** | same |
| H-NAVC-3 | The final plan's compliance clears the nav-blind `ha0_ext` floor on the same windows (CI lower bound above the floor) | **OPEN — pre-registered; FALSE at step 9,500 (0.390 vs 0.418)** | same |
| H-SEAM-1 / H-H19-1 / H-CONS-1 / H-SEL-1 | E7-off / E9-off degrade the plan (seam carries the goal); H19-off degrades TACTICAL; deferred windows read hold-and-point; scoring the EMITTED fan changes the pick ≥ 10 % without ADE loss | **OPEN — pre-registered; ⚠️ the eval-time ablation switches are NOT yet CLI flags of `refcv3_arm.py` (ESCALATED, 0 GPU, must precede the post-training run)** | `PREREG_REFCV4B_HIERARCHY_EVAL.md` §3, §7 |

**Honest scope, stated in the prereg:** the core's `route_head` readout is dangling (`graft_route` False) and cannot be tested by any eval-time ablation; eval-time knockouts measure RELIANCE, not what training with/without the edge would give; T1 is open loop and supports no driving claim.

## D-REFAV1-CCOS-ARMS / H-REFAV1-SURFACE-1 — the three cost-metric ARMS landed (2026-09-05, Master Mind; extends the panel row `D-REFAV1-CCOS-EVAL`)

| id | claim | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFAV1-CCOS-ARMS | ⭐⛔ **THE `ccos` REPAIR WORKS MECHANICALLY AND IS REFUTED AS AN IMPROVEMENT.** 282 windows / 141 episode clusters, `refav1-b1-v72-ep3-speed` step 21,109, **T1**, episode-cluster bootstrap n_boot 2000. **Mechanism:** the CEM's own plan supplies the emitted trajectory on **46.1 %** of windows under weight-compensated `ccos` vs **33.7 %** under the shipped `cos` (injected baseline 66.3 % → 53.9 %). ⭐ **The fingerprint:** under `cos` the planner's LATERAL row is **bit-identical to `ha0`'s** (cross **0.2257**, heading **2.462°**, curvature **0.0072** on both) — on baseline-won windows the "plan" IS the constant-velocity rollout; under `ccos` they move to 0.2902 / 3.272 / 0.0099, i.e. it genuinely plans. **Weight compensation is real and was predicted:** naive flip ADE **0.9268** [0.7808, 1.0819] → compensated **0.7116** [0.6291, 0.7973] (the banked 5,792.6× `chord` factor generalises). ⛔ **Every family degrades:** ADE **0.5474** [0.4839, 0.6108] → **0.7116**; speed MAE 0.5412 → 0.7175; along-track 0.4241 → 0.5506 — against `ha` **0.5391**, `ha0` **0.5316**, `ha0_ext` **0.5207** (`ol` 0.4237 is ⛔ **T0**, a WM diagnostic, never driving). Compensated weights `(W_JERK, W_KAPPA, W_VEND) = (12.859430, 32.148575, 64.297150)` | **SUPPORTED (mechanism) + REFUTED (improvement) — MEASURED 2026-09-05, T1** ⇒ ⛔ **`ccos` does NOT become the default**; the shipped `cos` path stays bit-identical and every banked refav1 number stays reproducible. ⚠️ The compensated interval [0.6291, 0.7973] does not overlap `ha0`'s [0.4710, 0.5939] and grazes `ha`'s upper bound — **these are PER-ARM intervals; the paired episode-cluster delta is the decision-grade form and is an OPEN WORK ITEM**, though no reading of the direction makes `ccos` a gain. Corroborates `D-COST-CHORD` from a second direction: *once the goal term is legible the surface itself prefers straight* — now with the term dominant enough to move half the decisions. ⚠️ Scope: one checkpoint; STRATEGIC UNAVAILABLE with reason (no route channel); distance-keeping PRESENT (n = 87–90) | `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refav1-ccos-eval/RESULT.md` §3; `raw/rec_{cos_ext,ccos_naive,ccos_comp}.json`, `raw/ccos_compensated_weights.txt` |
| H-REFAV1-SURFACE-1 | ⭐ **The binding constraint on refav1's planner is the COST SURFACE, not the goal metric's arithmetic.** Legibility is now MEASURED as insufficient: with the goal term audible on ~half the decisions the emitted plans are worse on all four families. Ranked candidates: `W_JERK` = 0.02 dominating a goal term that can now compete; and the seed channel | **OPEN — pre-registered.** Committed outcomes: a weight sweep that moves the surface's minimum toward GT on the four families with the echo gate holding ⇒ the surface is the lever; no weight in the swept range beats `ha0_ext` ⇒ refav1's iCEM planner is not repairable by re-weighting and the line closes | `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refav1-ccos-eval/RESULT.md` §3d |
| D-REFAV1-SURFACE-PAIRED | ⭐⛔ **THE `ccos` REFUTATION SURVIVES THE DECISION-GRADE PAIRED TEST, ON EVERY FAMILY SEPARATELY — AND THE SHIPPED `cos` PLANNER'S LATERAL OUTPUT IS A WINDOW-LEVEL IDENTITY WITH CONSTANT VELOCITY.** MEASURED 2026-09-05 (ArchInf FlyWheel, `Research/2026-09-05-refav1-surface-sweep/`, `tools/paired_delta_refav1.py`), **T1**, 282 windows / 141 episode clusters, **paired episode-cluster bootstrap n_boot 2000** on the three banked THOR dumps. Closes the OPEN WORK ITEM named in `D-REFAV1-CCOS-ARMS`. **(1) `ccos_comp − cos` is separated WORSE on 9 of the 13 available metrics:** ADE **+0.1642 [+0.1075, +0.2282]**, FDE **+0.4467 [+0.2984, +0.6131]**; LON speed **+0.1763 [+0.1155, +0.2425]**, along **+0.1264 [+0.0800, +0.1763]**, accel **+0.1848 [+0.1223, +0.2531]**; LAT cross **+0.0645 [+0.0211, +0.1193]**, heading **+0.7940° [+0.2059, +1.4960]** (n = 272), yaw-rate **+0.0159 [+0.0056, +0.0289]**; TAC lon **−0.1028 [−0.1596, −0.0496]**. TAC lat and dist-keep min-TTC straddle; the only two metrics favouring `ccos` are dist-keep headway (+0.2786, n = 87) and time-gap (+0.0483, n = 81) — ⚠️ **safety SURROGATES on the plan's own path, not errors against GT**, and `ccos_comp` reads more headway largely because it is slower. **(2) ⭐ `cos − ha0` reads exactly 0.0000 with a ZERO-WIDTH interval on ALL THREE lateral metrics and on `TAC_traj_lat_correct`** — the "bit-identical lateral row" that `D-REFAV1-CCOS-ARMS` read off four-decimal means is a **window-level identity on all 282 windows**: under `cos` there is nothing left to attribute to lateral planning. **(3) Weight compensation is real, paired-confirmed:** `ccos_naive − ccos_comp` separated on ADE **+0.2152 [+0.1022, +0.3530]**, FDE, all three LAT metrics and TAC lat — and still not enough to reach any floor. **(4) No arm beats any floor on all four families.** `ccos_comp − ha0_ext` is separated WORSE on ADE (+0.1909), all three core LON metrics (speed +0.4469) and TAC lon (−0.2305), yet separated BETTER on LAT cross (−0.0764) and yaw (−0.0250) — `ha0_ext` holds a drifting κ₀. **(5) `cos` is NOT vindicated:** vs `ha` and `ha0_ext` its ADE straddles (+0.0083 / +0.0267) while LON speed (+0.2706) and TAC lon (−0.1277) are separated WORSE — it ties the trivial controls by BEING one of them. **Controls:** known-value `cos.cl − cos.cl` exactly 0.0000 [0, 0] on all 14 metrics (**PASS**); same windows asserted bit-exact (`ws`/`v0`/`g`/`clip_index`); shared floors bit-identical across dumps (`ha`/`ha0`/`ol` 0.0, `ha0_ext` 7.63e-06 = float32 ulp from post-hoc `add_floor` vs inline). ⚠️ **A searched planner arm is a cross-box REPLICATE, not an identity**: the dev box's independent `ccos_comp` differs from Thor's by up to **0.044** over 570 scalars, so the Thor dumps were used. STRATEGIC **UNAVAILABLE, n = 0**, with reason (no route channel; the planner's strategic input is the tactical head's own imagined goal, so scoring against it scores the model against itself). ⚠️ **Scope: this is a statement about the PLANNER over the world model, never about the world model — which responds correctly to turn actions (separated, banked).** | **REFUTATION UPHELD — MEASURED 2026-09-05, T1, paired episode-cluster bootstrap** ⇒ `D-REFAV1-CCOS-ARMS` needs no status amendment; its open paired work item is CLOSED. ⛔ `ccos` still does not become the default | `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refav1-surface-sweep/RESULT.md` §1; `raw/paired_delta_phase1.json`, `raw/paired_delta_phase1.md`; estimator `taniteval.ci.paired_episode_cluster_bootstrap`, n_boot 2000, 141 clusters |
| D-REFAV1-SURFACE-SCREEN | ⭐⭐ **`W_JERK` IS NOT A REGULARISER ON THIS SURFACE — IT IS A SWITCH THAT TURNS THE SEARCH OFF; AND THE DECODED GOAL IS DIRECTIONALLY RIGHT BUT COMMITS TO A TURN ON ONLY A FIFTH OF THE TURNS.** MEASURED 2026-09-05 (ArchInf FlyWheel, `Research/2026-09-05-refav1-surface-sweep/` §2, `tools/surface_screen2.py`), **ZERO GPU**: 59 weight settings evaluated exactly on the banked box panel, whose own closure `total = goal + W_JERK·jerk_raw + W_KAPPA·kap_raw` reproduces to **1.6e-08**. Pre-registered in `SPEC.md` with the SELECT/SCORE split (`raw/split.json`) banked BEFORE any weight was computed. **(1) ⛔ 24 of the 26 candidates have `jerk_raw` IDENTICALLY 0** — only `seed0` (median 0) and `proposal` (median **2.471**) carry any. So `W_JERK` cannot re-rank 24 of 26 candidates at ANY magnitude: **nine settings spanning 1e13** give an identical turn rate (0.129) and identical sign correctness (0.846). Its only function is to penalise the **iCEM proposal, i.e. the searched plan** — at the compensated 12.86 the proposal's median penalty is **31.8** against a goal term whose whole range is [0, 2] — and the proposal wins **0.0 %** of windows at every setting except the fully unpenalised corner (2.1 %). ⇒ **the `H-REFAV1-SURFACE-1` suspect "`W_JERK` dominates a goal term that can now compete" is REFUTED as stated and REPLACED**: `W_JERK` does not compete with the goal across the candidate set, it deletes the search. ⚠️ The pre-registered deliberate regression (`W_JERK` × 1e4) consequently FAILED to move a single decision; it was re-designed for this instrument (`W_KAPPA` × 1e6) and **PASSES** at `frac_plan_turns` 0.0000 — both recorded, not swapped. **(2) ⭐ GOAL QUALITY, no weights involved** (episode-cluster bootstrap, n_boot 2000): GT turns on **0.4681 [0.4043, 0.5355]** of windows; the decoded goal proposes a turn on only **0.2045 [0.1280, 0.2932]** of those (and 0.0733 [0.0347, 0.1169] of straight ones); when both turn (**n = 27 / 21 clusters**) the goal's DIRECTION is correct **0.7778 [0.6249, 0.9231] — the interval EXCLUDES chance 0.5**. ⇒ **the goal's failure is RECALL, not direction.** **(3) ⛔ `cv`'s argmin share is 0.507 at ALL 59 settings**, from zero penalty to 1e6× — on the HOLD stratum every candidate scores exactly 1.0, so **half the grid is unreachable by any re-weighting, arithmetically.** **(4) The weights trade turn RATE against turn CORRECTNESS and the product barely moves:** the correct-turn rate on GT-turning windows is **0.157 shipped, 0.157 compensated, 0.243 at zero penalty**; `W_KAPPA` is the only live axis and only below ~0.3, where the extra turns bought are mostly the WRONG direction (sign correctness 0.846 → 0.571). ⚠️ **SCREEN ONLY — 26 injected/seeded candidates against a 300 × 30 search; it decides no committed outcome** and hands `(W_JERK, W_KAPPA) = (0, 0)` to Stage B by the pre-registered rule. ⚠️ **Scope: the PLANNER over the world model, never the world model** (which responds correctly to turn actions, separated, banked) | **MEASURED 2026-09-05, zero GPU; H-REFAV1-SURFACE-1 still OPEN pending Stage B** ⇒ the evidence now points at the GOAL SOURCE rather than the weights, which is exactly what Stage B's T0 oracle-goal arm (`--with-oracle-goal-arm`) is pre-registered to discriminate | `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refav1-surface-sweep/RESULT.md` §2, `SPEC.md`; ## D-REFAV1-DRIVE-* — refav1 cannot TURN, and it is TWO structural gates in series (2026-09-05, Arch+Inference FlyWheel)

**Package:** `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refav1-make-it-drive/`
(`GATE_MECHANISM.md` · `GATE_ON_REAL_MODEL.md` · `TWO_GATES.md` · `RESULT.md`).
⭐ Supplies the **mechanism** behind `D-REFAV1-SURFACE-PAIRED` (2) and the **lever** for the
recall failure `D-REFAV1-SURFACE-SCREEN` (2) measured. ⛔ Does **not** reopen
`D-REFAV1-CCOS-ARMS`; no ADE gain from `ccos` is claimed anywhere in it.

MEASURED zero-GPU (`tools/decision_ceiling.py`, 282 windows / 132 GT-turn / 150 GT-straight, step 21,109): **AUC 0.712** vs a **shuffled-label control of 0.5055 ± 0.0336** (200 shuffles) ⇒ **the information IS present**; the trunk needs nothing, and the WM was already measured sound. **But argmax is NOT badly mis-thresholded** — at its own false-turn rate (0.0733) the best possible threshold buys **+0.015** recall (0.205 → 0.220). All real gain comes from **moving along** the frontier, i.e. buying recall with false turns at ≈ **0.49 extra false-turn rate per unit recall**: recall **0.265 @ 0.10**, **0.348 @ 0.15**, **0.470 @ 0.20**, **0.644 @ 0.30**. ⛔ **THE NEAR-MISS FRAMING IS TRUE AND MISLEADING, AND ONLY THE CONTROL SHOWS IT:** of **105** missed turns **100 % lose by ≤ 2 logits** (p50 **1.252**), which reads as "a calibration failure, one scalar fixes it" — but on the **139 correctly-held** windows LANE_KEEP wins by p50 **1.549**, a **median gap of only 0.297 logits** with almost totally overlapping distributions (missed p90 1.652 > held p10 1.117). Cheapest first: **(a)** re-run the oracle arm **unconfounded** (oracle goal **+** a seed derived from it) — until then the prize is unbounded; **(b)** the **`ccos` hold-branch / goal-scale** work becomes the CRITICAL PATH, since the LANE_KEEP degeneracy and this saturation are the same defect; **(c)** the lateral-baseline asymmetry gains value — it reaches turning without routing through the goal term. ⚠️ `cl_oraclegoal` is **T0** (true-future-conditioned) and is used only as a bound; the finding is that it fails even as one | `…/2026-09-05-refav1-make-it-drive/ORACLE_GOAL.md`; `raw/oracle_goal.json`; Thor `rec_B1_puregoal.json`, `dump_B1_puregoal/` |
| D-REFAV1-DRIVE-RECALL-RETRACTED | ⛔ **MY OWN "20.5 % TURN RECALL" FRAMING IS RETRACTED: IT COUNTED TURNS THE ACTION SPACE CANNOT EXPRESS.** The `D-REFAV1-DRIVE-CEILING` measurements stand (head AUC 0.712, margin gap 0.297 logits, trunk + WM adequate), but its **recommendation is superseded**. MEASURED by the Master Mind (M15/M16, `4013231`) on a dense panel: restricted to turns the goal vocabulary can **express** (**R ≤ 25 m**; measured crossover **0.04101**, agreeing with the analytic **0.040**), the **shipped** head already decodes a curvature-carrying token on **74.4 %** of windows at **12.0 %** false-turn, **AUC 0.8806** (shuffled flat 0.498–0.504). ⇒ the 20.5 % figure is a statistic about **R ≈ 1000 m** curves that `GOAL_KAPPA_TURN = 0.08` (R 12.5 m) cannot express at all — the head was not failing to see them, the **action space had no token for them**. ⚠️ **This is also the mechanism behind `D-REFAV1-DRIVE-AB`:** the lever forced κ = 0.08 onto windows whose true curvature was ~**80× gentler**, so its extra turns were wrong in **magnitude**, not merely in direction | **RETRACTION — MEASURED 2026-09-05** ⇒ do not optimise a head against the 0.297-logit margin gap: that criterion is measured against a **mis-specified target**. ⭐ **The general rule: a recall number is a claim about a LABEL SET — before quoting one, ask what the model would have had to EMIT to be scored correct, and whether it CAN.** Same family as the estimator rules in `CLAUDE.md`, object swapped to the label set | `…/2026-09-05-refav1-make-it-drive/MUST_WE_RETRAIN.md` (correction at the head of the file); M15/M16 `4013231` |
| D-REFAV1-DRIVE-PRIOR-ARTIFACT | **THE BANKED SWEEP'S `prior:label τ≥1.0` COLLAPSE IS AN EPS-SMOOTHING ARTIFACT, NOT A PROPERTY OF PRIOR CORRECTION.** The priors are eps-smoothed at **1e-6** and **three classes are NEVER LABELLED** (`LANE_CHANGE_L`, `LANE_CHANGE_R`, `ABORT_LC`), so `−τ·log π` hands them a bias of ~**+10** — the rule most strongly promotes exactly the tokens with no evidence behind them. MEASURED: at τ=1.0 the eps-smoothed rule decodes to never-labelled classes on **282/282** windows (stride-40) and **1974/1974** (stride-5); the MASKED variant does not collapse | **CORRECTION — MEASURED 2026-09-05** ⇒ a masked prior is admissible where the banked sweep's collapse implied it was not | `…/RESULT.md`; `raw/bias_ladder_{s40,s5}.json` |

## D-VLA-EXT / H-VLA-1..8 — the language backbone for the tactical and strategic layers (2026-09-05, Research Lab / Arch+Inference)

**Deliverables:** `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-vla-extension-frontier/RESULT.md`
(survey §1 from 58 banked primaries · design options §2 · grounding/consistency §3 · hypotheses §4 · refusals §5) and
`Project Steering/REFCV5_VLA_EXTENSION_PLAN.md` (the plan). Contract: `Project Steering/REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` §9.

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-VLA-LAT1 | ⛔ **Alpamayo-R1's "99 ms" is a WORKSTATION number and 71 % of it is the CoT.** Table 14 measures on an NVIDIA RTX 6000 Pro Blackwell: vision 3.43 ms · prefill 16.54 ms · **reasoning decode 70 ms for 40 tokens** · trajectory decode 8.75 ms (5 flow steps); totals 29 ms trajectory-only / **99 ms** with CoT / 312 ms with an autoregressive trajectory (127 tokens). ⇒ 1.75 ms/token for a 10 B model on a Blackwell workstation card, and **the paper publishes no embedded breakdown**. Quoting it as an on-vehicle latency is the datacentre-GPU trap | **DECIDED (PUBLISHED-PRIMARY, `2511.00088` Table 14)** | RESULT.md §1 |
| D-VLA-LAT2 | ⭐ **Thor's 7.5× compute advantage does NOT reach autoregressive decode; the transferable ratio is 1.33×.** Decode is weight-bandwidth-bound: Thor 273 GB/s vs Orin 204.8 GB/s (NVIDIA module pages, fetched 2026-09-05). Prefill and the vision trunk are compute-bound and do get the 7.5×. Same family as the `df` / `step_s` scope traps | **DECIDED (PUBLISHED-SECONDARY spec + derivation)** | RESULT.md §2.0 |
| D-VLA-LAT3 | The only published **embedded** driving-VLM latency table: DriveVLM Table 6 on **OrinX** — Qwen1.8B **79.6 tok/s** (12.56 ms/token), MobileLLaMA-1.4B **117.4 tok/s**; the table's own FP16 "model size" column is arithmetically impossible against the measured rate, which **proves the deployed weights are quantised** and implies an achieved efficiency **η ≈ 0.37** of the bandwidth roof (two independent rows give 0.37 / 0.36). DriveVLM-Dual ships at **410 ms on OrinX**, asynchronous, on a second chip — the published existence proof for a VLM at a 300–500 ms tact beside a fast planner | **DECIDED (PUBLISHED-PRIMARY, `2402.12289` Tables 6/9, §6)** | RESULT.md §1, §2.0 |
| D-VLA-DESIGN1 | ⭐ **CHOSEN: frozen small LM + LoRA with REF-C's trunk as the ONLY vision encoder** (`TanitLang`). ≈ 368 M resident / ≈ 6 M trainable with SmolLM2-360M (≈ 806 M with Qwen3.5-0.8B); both ≤ 1 B. Runner-up (a whole small VLM + projector) refused on four grounds: §9.1's contract term, SmolVLM Finding 1 (a large encoder beside a small LM is a net loss), SigLIP's disproportionate Jetson INT8 hit (`2607.08029`), and — decisively — **two encoders destroy the faithfulness intervention**, because explanation and plan would look at different tensors | **DECIDED** | RESULT.md §2.c/§2.d; PLAN §1 |
| D-VLA-DESIGN2 | ⛔ **A prose CoT every tact is arithmetically impossible on Thor.** ~325 ms of decode after REF-C and prefill = **41 tokens at 0.8 B FP8**, against a template CoT of **300–500 tokens** (`2602.02864`). ⇒ two clocks: 2–6 executable tokens + **non-decoded** goal vectors every tact; prose at ≤ 1 Hz or on demand. **A design in which the planner waits on text is refused at the interface** | **DECIDED** | RESULT.md §2.0/§2.c; DESIGN_PLAN §9.6 |
| D-VLA-TEACHER1 | ⛔ **The Alpamayo CoT supplies FORM, not CONTENT.** Measured on our own corpus and our own teacher: 42.5 % reasoning fidelity, 48.3 % reasoning-action consistency, 37.9 % of stop-claimed cases continue (`2605.17268`, 300 inferences over 100 PhysicalAI-AV scenarios); 33.3 % of CoTs unreliable (`2608.29583`); our own spot check 3/5 with a hallucinated cyclist. Content comes from our v7.2 labels and `obstacle.offline` | **DECIDED** | RESULT.md §0.2, §1.4, §5 N8 |
| H-VLA-1 | A **~6 M bridge** (`proj_in` + LoRA r=16) lets a **frozen** SmolLM2-360M read REF-C's 160 PV tokens: v7.2 class *through the LM* beats a **linear probe on `pooled`** (paired episode-cluster CI excluding 0) | **OPEN — pre-registered, both outcomes committed** | FAIL ⇒ staged unfreeze, then option (a) with the ≤1 B ceiling re-checked. | H-VLA-3 | On Thor, **0.36 B FP8** emits ≥ 6 tokens + the non-decoded goal heads in **≤ 150 ms**, and REF-C + module ≤ **500 ms** at **batch 1** (`torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()` only) | **OPEN — pre-registered** | FAIL ⇒ NVFP4 and/or 0.2 B, else the text head moves entirely off-cycle. ⚠️ INT4 can *slow* Jetson generation (`2607.08029`), so the fallback is itself a measurement. ## D-V5A / H-V5A — the vision-only maximum: the camera inventory, the mechanism ladder, and the v5a/v5b boundary (2026-09-05, Arch+Inference)

Trigger: PI 2026-09-05, verbatim — *"i prefer to do the environment extensions in two versions/steps, let start by pure vision and then add lidar. So check, what we can do maximally with vision, bev, und was else?"* Evidence for every row: `TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-vision-only-maximum/RESULT.md`, with `raw/camera_rig_probe.json`, `raw/rig_ground_plane_probe.json`, `code/probe_camera_rig.py`, `code/probe_rig_ground_plane.py`. **Zero GPU** (two HF network probes on the dev box; the 4060 and Thor were not touched). Builds on `D-REFC-DDAUDIT-1..6` / `H-DDA-1..4` and on `REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` §0–§6; ⛔ that plan's §7–§10 were being written concurrently by a sibling and are **not** edited here — reconciliation is the Master Mind's.

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-V5A-CAM1 | ⭐⭐ **PhysicalAI-AV IS A SEVEN-CAMERA, 360°-COVERED CORPUS AND WE READ ONE OF THE SEVEN — 33.36 % OF THE AZIMUTH CIRCLE.** MEASURED from `calibration/sensor_extrinsics` chunk_0000 (1,700 rows = 100 clips × 17 sensors: 7 cameras + LiDAR + 9 `srr_0` radars), per-sensor median pose, boresight = the rotated sensor **+z**: front_wide **−0.58°** / cross_left **+67.33°** / cross_right **−66.47°** / front_tele **−0.23°** / rear_left **+151.80°** / rear_right **−151.25°** / rear_tele **+179.83°**. Union coverage over 3,600 azimuth bins: **front_wide alone 33.36 %**, **the three 120° cameras 70.53 %**, **all seven 100.00 %**. **Convention control read its known value** — the front-wide +z lands at −0.58° az / −0.54° el (straight ahead, level) and its +x at −90.58° (image-right) | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | RESULT.md §1.2; `raw/camera_rig_probe.json` |
| D-V5A-CAM2 | ⭐ **THE SURROUND UPGRADE COSTS 27.2 % OF WHAT LiDAR COSTS, AND ZERO EPISODES.** MEASURED by full HF blob listing (3,146 files per feature): the six unread cameras total **27.85 TB** corpus-wide = **90.98 MB/clip** ⇒ **428.8 GB** for the B1 corpus (4,713 clips); the **cross pair alone is 149 GB**. LiDAR for the same corpus is **1,574 GB** (99.60 TB ÷ 298,326 covered clips × 4,713). All **7 cameras are at 100.00 %** corpus coverage and 500/500 on our own r0 selection, vs LiDAR/`.offline` at **97.44 % / 0.99** ⇒ **adding cameras costs no episodes; adding LiDAR costs 2.56 %.** The **rig transform for all seven is already downloaded** — `physicalai.py:317-324` pulls `sensor_extrinsics` per clip and then filters to the front-wide row ⇒ marginal calibration cost **zero bytes, one changed filter** | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | RESULT.md §1.3; `raw/camera_rig_probe.json` |
| D-V5A-CAM3 | ⚠️ **CORRECTS the 2026-07-26 feature census's CAMERA byte figures.** That probe priced every feature as `chunk_0000 × 3,146`, which is near-exact for LiDAR (**×1.02**: 101.74 extrapolated vs **99.60 TB** measured) because a spin is a near-constant size, but **×1.33–1.58 high for video**, whose bitrate is scene-dependent. ⇒ "**40.7 TB** for the six unread cameras" is really **27.85 TB**; "**21.1 MB**/clip/camera" is really **13.35** (front-wide) / **14.90** (7-camera mean); "301 GB for 2,376 clips × 6 cameras" is **216 GB**. **Root-cause class: a true measurement quoted outside its scope** — a one-draw extrapolation presented as a corpus figure (the `df` / Thor `free` / cgroup / `step_s` family). It did not change that document's recommendation, but it inflates by ~46 % exactly the number the surround-camera decision turns on. ⛔ The old figures must not be re-quoted | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** — ⭐ **ADJUDICATED: it rises to an entry. `RETRACTION_LOG.md` (#28), 2026-09-05.** The deciding reason is not the size of the correction but the shape of its check: the extrapolation WAS validated — against LiDAR, at ×1.02 — and that agreement is why nobody re-derived it for video. ⛔ A validation performed on the artifact where the assumption holds certifies nothing about the artifact where it does not | RESULT.md §1.4 |
| D-V5A-GROUND1 | ⭐ **RIG z = 0 IS THE ROAD PLANE, to within 0.05–0.13 m — so the front-wide camera height is 1.43–1.56 m and monocular depth has a metric anchor with no LiDAR.** MEASURED over **87,481 cuboids** (12 clips, one `obstacle.offline` chunk; `reference_frame == "rig"` on 87,481/87,481): median bottom face `center_z − size_z/2` reads **−0.125 m** (automobile, n = 76,574), **−0.050** (person), **−0.122** (heavy_truck), **−0.093** (bus), **−0.055** (other_vehicle). **Two controls in opposite directions, both reading their known values**: every ground-standing class ≈ 0, and the one class that must *not* touch the road — `protruding_object` — reads **+1.676 m**. Also re-confirms the enum: **10 classes, all dynamic** | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | RESULT.md §1.7; `raw/rig_ground_plane_probe.json` |
| D-V5A-THORSHARE | ⭐ **THE ENCODER IS ~90 % OF THE TICK, WHICH IS WHY GROUNDING IS FREE AND A SECOND CAMERA IS NOT.** Against the MEASURED **60.3 ms p50 / 63.1 ms p95** Thor tick on real step-29999 weights (100 ms budget, `PROGRAM_OVERVIEW.md` §5.0.2) and the ~90 % encoder share (`refc.py:2238`, INHERITED source comment): ⇒ **≈ 54 ms encoder / ≈ 6 ms everything else**. Every decoder-side mechanism (grid-sample attention, `cross_agent`, the score head) costs a few percent of 6 ms; a **second pass through the trunk costs ~54 ms per view** and would put three cameras at **≈ 172 ms — over budget** — *unless* Thor's 20 SMs absorb batch 3. ⛔ **That is a HYPOTHESIS, not a fact**: the batch-8 saturation figure was measured on a *training* step of a *different* trunk, and quoting it here would be the `step_s` scope error | **DECIDED (design consequence)** — the open half is `H-V5A-THOR` | RESULT.md §2.0 |
| D-V5A-ATT2OF3 | ⭐ **v5a RECOVERS TWO OF DIFFUSIONDRIVE'S THREE GROUNDED ATTENTIONS WITH NO NEW SENSOR, AND REFUSES THE THIRD ON EVIDENCE.** `D-REFC-DDAUDIT-4` found we have **0 of 3**. (i) **waypoint-indexed spatial** — recovered in PV today (`H-DDA-1`, 0 new bytes) and later on a camera-lifted BEV; (ii) **agent cross-attention** — recovered from a monocular 3D detector trained on `obstacle.offline` **labels** (97.44 % coverage, 0 new bytes), which is admissible under the binding rule *labels may use privileged signals; inference is vision-only* (PI 2026-08-03); (iii) **ego-query — deliberately refused**, because the FiLM + `ego_dropout 0.5` + X15 design is the one lever MEASURED to make an arm read the scene (`H-ECHO-8`) and DD's ego query is an unguarded echo channel. ⇒ **the audit's structural gap is a VISION-ONLY gap, not a LiDAR gap** | **DECIDED** | RESULT.md §2.7, §3.9 |
| D-V5A-OCC-LIMIT | ⛔ **"OCCUPANCY" IN v5a MEANS AGENT OCCUPANCY PLUS AN EXPLICIT UNOBSERVED MASK — GENERAL/STATIC OCCUPANCY IS NOT SUPPORTED BY THIS CORPUS AT ALL.** SurroundOcc/TPVFormer/Occ3D-style occupancy needs static structure; our only 3D label source is `obstacle.offline`, **10 classes, all dynamic** (re-MEASURED today over 87,481 cuboids), and PhysicalAI-AV ships no map, lane graph, junction annotation or traffic-light feature, with no lat/lon in `egomotion`. Two consequences: (a) `bev_raster.py`'s existing agent raster ([120, 64] over 60 m × ±16 m @ 0.5 m, with `fov_mask` / `fov_census`) **is** the v5a occupancy target and should be reused, not rebuilt; (b) ⛔ **a one-camera BEV lift covers a 33.4 % frustum and every out-of-frustum cell is UNOBSERVED, not free** — an unmasked BEV is the zero-memmap trap in a new costume, so **every v5a BEV carries a `fov_mask` and every loss is masked by it**. ⚠️ Published LSS/BEVDet/BEVFormer/BEVDepth/SimpleBEV numbers are **6-camera surround** numbers and are **inadmissible** for a one-camera arm | **DECIDED** | RESULT.md §2.3, §2.6 |
| D-V5A-CYLINDRICAL | ⚠️ **EVERY PUBLISHED CAMERA→BEV LIFT ASSUMES A PINHOLE UNPROJECTION AND IS SILENTLY WRONG ON OUR FRAME.** Our canonical frame is **256 × 640 CYLINDRICAL**, `f_ref` 305.577, HFOV **120.0°**; the correct unprojection is explicit (`φ = (u − W/2)/f_ref`, ray `(cos φ, sin φ, ·)`, tan-based vertical), not `K⁻¹[u,v,1]` — the pinhole formula reads **92.6°** for a 120° camera (CLAUDE.md FOV trap). ⭐ And cylindrical is *easier*: the column is **linear in azimuth**, so each feature column is one constant-width BEV ray fan ⇒ **a polar-native BEV is the recommended v5a form**, with `bev_raster.readout_column_index(projection="cylindrical")` already implementing the forward map. Resolution ledger: native 1080p wide **0.0625°/px**, native tele **0.0156°/px**, our canonical frame **0.1875°/px**, the trunk's 8 × 20 PV map **6.0°/column** (≈ 3.1 m lateral at 30 m — wider than a lane) | **DECIDED (MEASURED / derived)** | RESULT.md §1.2, §2.1, §2.3 |
| D-V5A-LADDER | **THE v5a LADDER: ten one-lever pre-registered work packages, ≈ 13.4 rig-GPU-hours serial, of which FOUR start at zero pod GPU and zero new bytes** — WP-V5A-0 (`E-THOR-MV`), WP-V5A-1 (`E-AGT-0`), WP-V5A-4 (`E-DDA-1` = `H-DDA-1`), WP-V5A-6 (`E-DEPTH-0`). Then WP-V5A-2/-3 (the agent-accuracy budget and the detector), -5 (which feature stage is sampled), -7/-9 (the surround pilot then the corpus), -8 (the masked polar lift), and **WP-V5A-F**, the full-scale refcv5a run, which ⛔ **waits for refcv4b's final eval to free the A40** and bundles **only** levers that passed their own rung. ⛔ **No arm in this document has been launched** | **DECIDED — no arm launched** | RESULT.md §3 |
| D-V5A-BOUNDARY | ⭐⭐ **THE v5a/v5b DECISION RULE: v5a PRODUCES THE NUMBER THAT PRICES v5b.** Every v5b argument reduces to *"how much range accuracy are we missing?"*, and two v5a rungs measure exactly that at **zero new bytes** — `E-DEPTH-0` (range error vs distance against the `obstacle.offline` box centres) and `E-AGT-1` (the σ at which our own separation dies). ⇒ **v5b is scheduled iff `E-AGT-1`'s σ bar lands below what `E-DEPTH-0`/`E-AGT-2` achieve**; if it lands above, LiDAR is a **safety** investment (the unclassed-object case), to be argued on its own terms, not a performance one. Two honesty items that belong to the PI's decision: ⛔ **even WITH LiDAR we cannot compute a NAVSIM-style DAC** (no map, no lane graph, no lat/lon), and ⛔ **occlusion is closed by neither release** — LiDAR does not see through a van, and crediting it with occlusion reasoning is where the LiDAR gain is routinely over-estimated. v5b's real, irreducible adds: metric range without a learned prior · the **unclassed object** · **night** (MEASURED **15.41 %** of the corpus, 14.4 % of our r0 selection) · static volume (not semantics) | **DECIDED (decision rule) — needs PI sign-off; interacts with the unresolved `D-REFCV5-PLAN-7` LiDAR-at-inference doctrine call** | RESULT.md §4 |
| H-V5A-THOR | **A second and third camera batched through the ONE vision trunk cost sub-linearly on Thor: p50 trunk latency at 3 views ≤ 1.30× the 1-view p50** (Thor's 20 SMs saturate well above batch 1). **WP-V5A-0**, ≈ 20 min, inference only, no training GPU. Control that must read a known value: the 1-view **end-to-end** tick must reproduce **60.3 ms p50 / 63.1 ms p95**, else the rig is wrong and the sweep is inadmissible; deliberate regression: 6 views must be visibly slower than 3 (a flat 1–6 curve means the timer is not measuring the trunk). ⛔ Only in-process `torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()` is admissible for memory on Thor; never quote an A40 latency. Outcomes: **≤ 1.30× ⇒ surround cameras are an affordable INFERENCE INPUT**; **≥ 2.0× ⇒ surround is a TRAINING-TIME TEACHER only and WP-V5A-7 inverts into a distillation arm** | **OPEN — pre-registered, both outcomes committed** | RESULT.md §3.1 |
| H-V5A-AGT-1 | ⭐ **THE ORACLE-TOKEN CEILING. A `cross_agent` attention fed K = 30 GROUND-TRUTH `obstacle.offline` boxes at inference beats the no-token control on the LONGITUDINAL family (headway / time-gap / TTC vs the replay) AND on TACTICAL**, paired episode-cluster bootstrap over the 141 eval clips, CI excluding 0. **WP-V5A-1**, 4 arms ≈ 2.0 h on the rig, **+≈ 1.2 M params, zero-init gated (bit-identical at step 0)**. ⛔ The oracle arm is **deliberately inadmissible as a capability claim** (it feeds a label at inference, violating I3) and is run **once**, purely as a ceiling, behind an `AGENT_ORACLE=1` flag a deployable config can never set (asserted OFF by a test). Controls: **constant token set** must read the no-information value; **no-token** = today's arm; **deliberate regression = temporally shuffled boxes**, which must LOSE. Outcomes: **separation ⇒ the ceiling IS the budget, proceed to `H-V5A-AGT-2`**; **no separation ⇒ NO detector accuracy can help and the entire agent-token mechanism is REFUSED for zero further GPU-days** | **OPEN — pre-registered, both outcomes committed** | RESULT.md §2.5, §3.2 |
| H-V5A-AGT-2 | **THE DETECTOR ACCURACY BAR IS A DERIVED QUANTITY, NOT A LITERATURE NUMBER: degrading the oracle boxes with calibrated range noise (σ ∈ {0, 0.5, 1, 2, 4} m) and, as a separate one-variable panel, a miss-rate sweep {0, 10, 25, 50 %}, locates the σ at which `H-V5A-AGT-1`'s separation dies.** **WP-V5A-2**, 6 arms ≈ 2.9 h, zero new bytes; σ = 0 must reproduce `H-V5A-AGT-1` exactly. Outcomes: **survives to σ ≥ 1 m ⇒ a monocular detector in the published band suffices, build WP-V5A-3**; **dies below σ = 0.5 m ⇒ the mechanism needs LiDAR-grade range and is a v5b item — a clean, quantitative v5a/v5b boundary** | **OPEN — pre-registered, both outcomes committed** | RESULT.md §3.3 |
| H-V5A-AGT-3 | **A DETR-style monocular 3D head (K = 30 queries, Hungarian, `obstacle.offline`'s 10 dynamic classes) trained as an auxiliary head on the shared trunk produces tokens inside the `H-V5A-AGT-2` σ budget, and its tokens recover a stated fraction of the oracle margin.** **WP-V5A-3**, 3 arms ≈ 3.0 h, **+2–4 M params**, vision-only at inference. Deliberate regression: the head trained on **shuffled** labels — its tokens must not help. A shortfall against the budget is reported **as the v5b case**, ⛔ never as a failure to be re-tuned | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §3.4 |
| H-V5A-DEPTH-1 | **A self-supervised monocular depth head reaches a metric range accuracy, at typical lead distances, sufficient for the LONGITUDINAL family — and its TWO independent scale anchors agree.** Anchor A: **known camera height 1.43–1.56 m** (`D-V5A-GROUND1`). Anchor B: **measured ego speed** (PackNet-SfM velocity supervision — exact here, because `v0` at cycle time is a measured, PI-sanctioned input). **The agreement of A and B is the control**: a disagreement beyond a few percent means the depth is wrong and says so. **WP-V5A-6**, 2 arms ≈ 1.0 h, zero new bytes. Readout is **range error vs distance at the `obstacle.offline` box centres**, plus a mandatory **raw-input floor** (predict the corpus-median range for that image row) the head must beat. ⛔ v5a uses depth as a **supervision signal for the lift**, never as "pseudo-LiDAR" points — an inferred cloud is a learned prior rendered as geometry and inherits every failure of the depth net while *looking* like a sensor | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §2.4, §3.6 |
| H-V5A-CAM-1 | **The cross pair as trunk input (shared trunk + view embedding) separates on the LATERAL and TACTICAL families against the front-only control**, on a **100-clip pilot (≈ 3 GB)** fetched by **HTTP range reads of zip members**, not whole chunks. **WP-V5A-7**, 2 arms ≈ 1.0 h. ⚠️ n = 100 is small: the failure branch reports **UNDERPOWERED with its n**, never "no effect". ⭐ Whichever way it goes, this rung **validates the range-read fetcher on 3 GB of camera** — a mechanism that is still a HYPOTHESIS on **v5b's** critical path (`REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` §3.1) and strictly cheaper to prove here than to discover failing on 1.57 TB of LiDAR. If `H-V5A-THOR` returns the linear answer, this arm **inverts into a distillation arm** (the cross pair supervises a 360° agent-presence target the front-only trunk must predict; tick unchanged) — **both variants are pre-registered and `H-V5A-THOR` selects which runs** | **OPEN — pre-registered, both variants committed** | RESULT.md §2.2, §3.7 |
| H-V5A-BEV-1 | **An LSS-style POLAR-native camera→BEV lift from the stride-8 stage, indexed directly by the fan (both are rig-frame), improves the LONGITUDINAL and TACTICAL families over `H-DDA-1`'s PV grounding.** **WP-V5A-8**, 3 arms ≈ 1.5 h; ⛔ **gated on `H-DDA-1` separating first** — if grounding in PV does not separate, geometry is not what is missing and a lift is premature. First arm's target is **the agent raster alone** (zero new data), so it measures whether a *metric frame* helps before anything is spent on *depth*. ⛔ **Deliberate regression: the same lift with `fov_mask` removed** (out-of-frustum = free) — it must be CAUGHT; if masked and unmasked arms are indistinguishable, the occupancy readout is not measuring occupancy | **OPEN — pre-registered** | RESULT.md §2.3, §3.8 |
| H-V5A-LEAD | **v5b's marginal value concentrates on FAR, LATERAL, OCCLUDED and NIGHT agents, not on the headway signal we most need** — because the LONGITUDINAL family is dominated by the **lead vehicle**, which is close, large and centred (the regime where monocular range is most accurate), while **92.2 % of our measured deficit is along-track** (`D-REFCV3-AXIS1`). ⇒ a maximised v5a captures most of the lead-vehicle benefit. **Refuted or supported by `H-V5A-DEPTH-1`'s range-error-vs-distance curve for the price of two tiny arms**, and by a **night-stratified** four-family table on any v5a arm (free: night is 15.41 % of the corpus) | **OPEN — pre-registered, 0 GPU beyond the rungs already listed** | RESULT.md §4.2 |

## D-REFCV5-PLAN — the refcv5 design plan: closing the DiffusionDrive gaps without giving up the hierarchy (2026-09-05, Arch+Inference)

Full argument, with the file-level plans and every pre-registration: `Project Steering/REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md`
(§0–§10 + manifest). **Zero GPU** — this is a design synthesis over banked packages
(`D-REFC-DDAUDIT-1..6`, `D-DDV2-*`, `D-REFCV4-VOCAB1`, `D-REFCV4-GATE1`, `D-REFCV4B-FLYLOW1`,
`D-REFCV4B-TURNCOV1`, `D-REFCV4B-EGODROP2`, `D-NAVCOMP-1..3`, `D-RL-READY-1`, `D-RL-REWARD-FLOOR-1`,
`D-REFCV3-40284`, `D-REFCV3-40284b`, `D-REFCV3-AXIS1`), and **it launches nothing**. ⛔ Nothing in it
is a result: it is a design with pre-registrations, and the first refcv5 number does not exist.

| id | claim / decision | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-1 | **The DiffusionDrive gap table is closed out**: 43 audited components → **19 IMPLEMENT · 9 ADAPT · 9 KEEP OURS · 5 SKIP**, each with its reason, carried by **10 pre-registered one-lever arms**. Nothing is left "to be decided" | **DECIDED** | `REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` §1 over `…/2026-09-05-refc-vs-diffusiondrive-audit/RESULT.md` §1.1–1.6 and `…/2026-09-05-diffusiondrive-v2-analysis/RESULT.md` |
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-2 | **The six USP invariants I1–I6 are refusal conditions, not trade-offs** — the hierarchy supervised at the top (I1), compliance-not-reproduction plus the post-training tests (I2), vision-only inference with ego-dropout + X15 (I3), the v0-conditioned flyable control vocabulary (I4), four families / tiers / paired estimator (I5), corpus and parity (I6). Every adopted DD mechanism is checked against them individually and the check is written down | **DECIDED** | §2; pinned by `DESIGN_REFCV4_NAV_WIRING.md`, `PREREG_REFCV4B_HIERARCHY_EVAL.md`, `H-ECHO-8`, `EVAL_DOCTRINE.md`, `parity_manifest.json` |
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-3 | ⭐ **PhysicalAI-AV CARRIES LiDAR — the PI was right.** `lidar/lidar_top_360fov`, 128-row spinning (`lidar_intrinsics.offline`: `row-offset-spinning`, 128 × 3600), 10 Hz, ~200 spins per 20 s clip, Draco-compressed, on **298,326 of 306,152 clips = 97.44 %**, 3,146 chunks × 31.7 GB = **99.6 TB, 68 % of the dataset by bytes**; extrinsics in `sensor_extrinsics.offline` (8 sensors = 7 cameras + LiDAR), the same rig frame `obstacle.offline` uses ⇒ **a LiDAR BEV is directly indexable by the anchor fan, with no camera projection involved**. The artifact is a DD-faithful **256 × 256 @ 0.25 m/px, ±32 m, 2-height-bin histogram** as a **PNG sidecar `<clip>.v2bev.pt`** beside the existing `*.v2ep.pt` — priced against the CONSUMER's loader (`v2_dataset.py::V2CompressedCache._payload`), ≈ **3–8 MB/episode** against 34.0 MB of camera, so the 161 GB cache is not rewritten and frames stay byte-identical (I6) | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED — three independent probes: HF blob listing 2026-09-05; the DataFlyWheel's 36-feature probe; the dataset card)** ⇒ work package **WP-DE-BEV-1**, owner DataFlyWheel, consumer Arch+Inference | §3.1–§3.3; `…/Data Engineering/Implementation/incoming/2026-07-26-physicalai-feature-probe/PHYSICALAI_FEATURE_PROBE.md` §1; ⚠️ adding it is a **declared change** to `stack/tests/test_physicalai_feature_readset.py`, a new named layer, never a silent edit |
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-4 | ⭐ **THE DIFFUSION NOISE LIVES IN CONTROL SPACE, NOT METRE SPACE.** `ε` on `(a_lon, a_lat)` normalised by the grid's own ranges `(4.0, 3.0)` and re-rolled through `rollout_unicycle_varstep`, so every sample is **flyable by construction** and the vocabulary stays the prior. Arithmetic: at t = 8 the `scaled_linear` schedule gives √(1−ᾱ₈) = 0.0316 ⇒ DD's metre normalisation is σ 0.90 m along / 0.73 m lateral **per waypoint independently** = ≈ 5.8 m/s² (0.6 g) implied lateral acceleration over a 0.5 s slot; in control units the same schedule is **0.126 / 0.095 m/s²**, i.e. ≈ 2.3 m along and ≈ 1.7 m lateral at the 6 s endpoint — comparable coverage, every sample integrable. The 8-slot control sequence has the **same 16 degrees of freedom** as the waypoint offset it replaces, so nothing expressible becomes inexpressible | **DECIDED — both outcomes committed.** Deliberate-regression arm = the DD-literal metre-space (`norm_odo`) sampler, which **must FAIL the flyability gate or the panel is VOID**; control = `σ ≡ 0, t = 0` must reproduce the deterministic decoder **bit-for-bit** | §4.2, §4.3 (file-level plan, 0 GPU to implement); `kinematic.py::rollout_unicycle_varstep/unicycle_controls_from_path_varstep/control_smoothness_losses`; `H-DDA-3` |
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-5 | **The ranked object becomes the emitted object, and then the selector scores the four families.** Step 1 = `sel_refined + sel_score_emitted + sel_ce_reach` (**0 new params**, flags already in `refc.py:1703-1710`) against the MEASURED defect that the ranking reads the t = 0 confidence while the fan leaves two passes later (`sel_idx_base` unchanged on **201/201** windows). Step 2 = V2's coarse-to-fine sub-metric selector with **our** heads — NC / TTC-headway / comfort-feasibility / progress / tactical-consistency / compliance against the `obstacle.offline` **replay** — **no PDM, no map**; ⛔ `DAC` is refused as unconstructible on PhysicalAI. Candidate self-attention lives **only** in the selector; the generator keeps candidates independent | **DECIDED — pre-registered `H-DDA-2`, `H-DDA-7`; first free read is `H-SEL-1` in refcv4b's post-training ablation** | §5.1, §5.2; new `stack/tanitad/refs/refc_selector.py` + `stack/scripts/refc_selector_train.py`; ⚠️ distinguished from the REFUSED `refc_rescorer.py` v1.2 (winner's curse) because the targets are environment predicates the GT does not enter |
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-6 | ⭐ **THE RL STAGE'S JOB IS THE FAN'S FLOOR, NOT ADE.** V2's published shape is PDMS@10 **75.3 → 84.4 (+9.1)** with @1 93.5 → 94.9 and diversity **−28 %** — the fan got *safer*, not *closer*. Vehicle: a **head-only scale policy** in control space (trainable **9,206,032 / 107,032,901 = 8.60 %**), G = 4 intra-anchor GRPO, the **≥ GT bar**, collision → −1, **no drivable-area term**. ⛔ **`G-REWARD` is a PRECONDITION, not a milestone**: hold-v0 must beat the human on **≤ 30 %** of lead windows on the 120-clip fit set (MEASURED today: **78.3 %**, `D-RL-REWARD-FLOOR-1`), fixed by moving feasibility and comfort out of the reward into calibrated **vetoes**. **A reward that fails G-REWARD may not train anything** | **DECIDED — pre-registered `H-DDA-5`, `H-DDA-6`; vehicle `H-RL-MIN-1` re-scoped as E-DDA-3b with base = the refcv4b FINAL.** ⛔ Launch is the Master Mind's / PI's call | §6.1–§6.4; `stack/tanitad/rl/` (8 modules, 149 tests green); the echo table refusing every GT-future reward term is §6.3 |
| ⛔ D-REFCV5-PLAN-7 | **PI DECISION REQUIRED — is LiDAR an INFERENCE INPUT or a TRAINING-TIME TEACHER?** The constitution's literal wording is *vision-only*; `Mission Plan.md:181` and ROADMAP L4 foresee LiDAR/radar fusion. **Default = teacher** (camera-only inference; **E-BEV-1** lifts a camera BEV taught by the LiDAR histogram + agent raster). The input arm (**E-LIDAR-1**, late fusion into the decoder KV) is designed and pre-registered but **not run** without the ruling. Either way the data is built once and the two arms are **paired on the same windows**, so *what the sensor buys beyond what the camera can be taught* is the measured quantity | **OPEN — PI.** ⚠️ It gates **E-LIDAR-1 only**: not the data build, not E-BEV-1, not the ladder, not the launch | §2 I3, §3.4 |

Five refutations of the **programme's** thesis (R1–R5) are stated too, and they are worth more than C1 | **DECIDED — pre-registered, both outcomes committed; margins fixed in advance (0.10 absolute on compliance, 2 % relative on ADE@6 s) and not renegotiable afterwards** | §8.1–§8.4 |
| D-REFCV5-PLAN-12 | ⭐ **THE VLA MOUNTS ON `hierarchy_hook`, WHICH ALREADY EXISTS AND IS UNFILLED.** `RefCModel.forward(..., hierarchy_hook=...)` is called as `hook(pooled_seq [B,W,F], ctx [B,d_ctx]) -> dict` and fills the model's ports **only where the caller passed `None`** — *"an explicitly supplied port always wins, so the hook can never silently override an experiment's input"*; `maneuver_logits [B, 5]` reaches `refc.py:1645-1647` and reweights the H19 anchor prior with **ZERO new wiring** (*"an outside tactical brain speaking the 5-way surface"*, never filled today). Where the two disagree about a **port**, §9 wins and the disagreement is escalated | §9.1–§9.8; vocabularies quoted verbatim from `stack/tanitad/models/vocab_v7.py` and `stack/scripts/s2_geom_emit_v7.py` |

## D-REFCV5-LADDER — the reconciled v5a/v5b release ladder: one ordered ladder instead of two (2026-09-05, Arch+Inference; integration task delegated by the Master Mind)

Trigger: PI 2026-09-05, verbatim — *"i prefer to do the environment extensions in two versions/steps,
let start by pure vision and then add lidar. So check, what we can do maximally with vision, bev, und
was else?"* (`Project Steering/Decisions/2026-09-05-mm-decisions.md` §M7). Two **COMPLETE** documents
described **overlapping work under two incompatible naming schemes** — `REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` §7
(the 14-package ladder, written *before* the split and therefore single-track) and
`…/2026-09-05-vision-only-maximum/RESULT.md` (the ten-rung `WP-V5A-*` camera-only ladder). ⛔ **No new
research, no arm launched, 0 GPU.** Adjudication rule applied throughout (the Master Mind's): the
vision study's **evidence** wins on the camera-only questions; the design plan's **structure** — SPEC
blocks, gates, claims, refutations — wins on everything else. Where the two conflict on a **fact**,
both readings are recorded and the conflict is **escalated, not adjudicated**.

| id | claim / hypothesis | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFCV5-LADDER-1 | ⭐ **ONE ORDERED LADDER NOW EXISTS INSTEAD OF TWO, AND EVERY NAME RESOLVES.** `REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` **§7A** is the ordering of record; **§7 is preserved unrewritten** (every per-WP detail, SPEC block, param delta and cost stands) and carries a one-line pointer saying it is superseded **for ordering and names only** — the record of what was planned before the split is kept deliberately. The reconciliation decides six things. **(1) Names.** ⛔ **`E-AGT-1` is RETIRED — it named two different arms**: in the plan (WP-6) the *whole* agent-token mechanism in one arm (DETR head + `cross_agent`, ≈ 6 M params); in the study (WP-V5A-2) *only* the range-noise σ sweep on oracle boxes. Canonical, and unconfusable: **`E-AGT-ORACLE`** (the ceiling, fed GT boxes, run once, refusable for 0 further GPU-days) → **`E-AGT-BUDGET`** (σ ∈ {0, 0.5, 1, 2, 4} m + a separate miss-rate panel; **its σ IS the detector specification**) → **`E-AGT-HEAD`** (the learned monocular 3D head + `cross_agent`). ⚠️ **`E-DDA-1` appears in BOTH under the same name for the same arm** (plan WP-3 ≡ study WP-V5A-4) — no rename. **(2) A second overlap the collision hid:** the camera→BEV lift straddled the boundary. The plan's §3.4 already names an agent-raster-taught variant needing no LiDAR, yet its §7.1 schedules `E-BEV-1` **after WP-11** (the LiDAR build). Split: **`E-BEVA-1` = v5a** (polar lift, **agent raster alone**, `fov_mask` mandatory, unblocked), **`E-BEV-1` = v5b** (LiDAR-taught), **`E-LIDAR-1` = v5b** (LiDAR as *inference input*, additionally gated on `D-REFCV5-PLAN-7`). **(3) Hypothesis ids:** the plan's WP-6 SPEC carries `H-DDA-1`, an id already held by `E-DDA-1`, so one id covered two mechanisms ⇒ canonical **`H-V5A-AGT-1/2/3`** for the agent rungs, **`H-DDA-1` reserved for `E-DDA-1`**. **(4) v5a is ordered in five phases** — A instruments (0 GPU, they unblock *reads*), B eleven rungs starting **today at zero pod GPU and zero new bytes**, C camera rungs gated on a Phase-B *result*, D four rungs that genuinely wait for a refcv4b checkpoint, E the launch — each with objective, gate, GPU-hours, box, dependencies and its start condition. It respects the study's capability statement verbatim: **v5a recovers two of DiffusionDrive's three grounded attentions and refuses the third on evidence** (`D-V5A-ATT2OF3`). **(5) v5b stays OFF the model critical path**, preserving the plan's own §7.4 statement and `D-REFCV5-PLAN-8`; its L1–L3 (pilot, rasteriser, sidecar) are 0-GPU pull-items independent of everything in v5a, and ⛔ **no v5a rung may ever be written as "after WP-11"** again. **(6) The boundary is a decision rule, not a date:** **v5b is scheduled iff the range accuracy the mechanism REQUIRES lands BELOW what vision ACHIEVES** — requirement = **σ\***, the σ at which `E-AGT-BUDGET`'s separation dies; achievement = the monocular range error vs distance that `E-DEPTH-0` measures at the `obstacle.offline` box centres (two scale anchors — camera height 1.43–1.56 m and PackNet-SfM velocity supervision on the measured `v0` — that must agree, as its control). **Both inputs cost 0 new bytes, ≈ 4 rig-GPU-h, and neither waits for a checkpoint ⇒ the boundary decision is reachable before refcv4b finishes.** If the requirement lands above what vision achieves, LiDAR is a **safety** investment (the unclassed object), **not a performance one** | **DECIDED — a reconciliation, not a result. ⛔ Nothing here is a T0 or T1 number and no arm was launched** | `Project Steering/REFCV5_DESIGN_PLAN.md` **§7A.1** (crosswalk: every WP/E name in both documents, its counterpart, its canonical name), **§7A.2** (v5a, five phases), **§7A.3** (v5b), **§7A.4** (the boundary rule), **§7A.5** (the two blockers as rungs A1/A2), **§7A.6** (the arithmetic), **§7A.7** (11 conflicts recorded, none adjudicated). ### 2026-09-05 — MASTER MIND ADJUDICATION of the two conflicts Rung A1 escalated

⭐ Rung A1 did the right thing twice: it found that following a written instruction literally
would have **broken the comparison the instruction exists to protect**, and it **escalated
instead of choosing silently**. Both are now decided. Decision record:
`Project Steering/Decisions/2026-09-05-mm-decisions.md` §M11, §M12.

| id | claim / decision | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-MM-ADJ-1 | ⛔ **`D-REFCV5-LADDER-2`'s wording *"it must call the same `echo_gate.ha0_ext`"* IS AMENDED — the canonical `ha0_ext` for EVERY ARM is the INTEGRATOR, `taniteval/tools/refav1_arm.py::hold_ext_controls`.** The instruction's INTENT was right (*a control re-implemented beside the thing it controls drifts, and then the gate measures the drift*) and its NAMED TARGET was wrong. `D-RUNGA1-2` MEASURED the two derivations disagreeing by **0.540642 m at 2 s** and **1.862923 m at 15 s** against a total refcv3-to-`ha` gap of **0.1423 m** — so the choice decides the verdict, not its precision. The integrator wins on two grounds, the second binding: (1) every banked refav1 number is already published under it, so switching would silently move a published result; (2) ⭐ **it is the same kinematic the candidate fan itself is rolled through** (`refc.py::roll_bank` → `rollout_unicycle`), so a closed-form control would compare the model's INTEGRATED candidates against a DIFFERENTLY-DERIVED baseline and score the difference between two kinematics as model skill. The closed form (`stack/tanitad/eval/echo_gate.py:171`) stays for `echo_gate`'s internal use. ⛔ The two may never be mixed inside one comparison, and no arm may quote `ha0_ext` without naming which derivation produced it | **DECIDED (Master Mind, 2026-09-05) — supersedes `D-REFCV5-LADDER-2`'s named target, not its reasoning** | `Decisions/2026-09-05-mm-decisions.md` §M11; `D-RUNGA1-1`, `D-RUNGA1-2`; pin `repo:stack/tests/test_refcv3_ha0_ext_shared.py` |
| D-MM-ADJ-2 | ⭐ **`PREREG_REFCV4B_HIERARCHY_EVAL.ERRATUM-1.md` IS ISSUED** — a SEPARATELY STAGED document, not an edit, because the prereg's falsifiable object is its staged blob id. `D-RUNGA1-5` MEASURED that §3's registered `H19-OFF` mechanism (`decoder.maneuver_to_anchor = None`) is **already the state of every factored v3/v4 build**, `refcv4b` included. ⛔ **The registered arm would have run, changed nothing, and been read as "the H19 seam is inert" — which §5 lists as a condition REFUTING the hierarchy thesis.** An ablation that cannot fail, inside a panel whose entire purpose is to be able to fail; fourth instance of that class this week. The corrected mechanism removes whichever anchor-prior heads the build actually carries and REFUSES when there are none. ⛔ **`H-H19-1` and its pre-committed outcomes are UNCHANGED, and NO result is retracted — the panel has not run** | **DECIDED (Master Mind, 2026-09-05)** | `Project Steering/PREREG_REFCV4B_HIERARCHY_EVAL.ERRATUM-1.md`; `Decisions/…-mm-decisions.md` §M12; `D-RUNGA1-5`; `repo:stack/tanitad/refs/refc.py:1214-1221,1572-1581` |

⚠️ **Neither adjudication produces a model number, and neither moves an acceptance bar.** They fix
*which control the bar is read against* and *which ablation the panel runs*. `D-REFCV5-PLAN-9`'s
and `D-REFCV4-*`'s claims remain exactly as open as before.
