### 2026-09-05 — A BINDING METRIC FAMILY WAS ABSENT FROM EVERY refcv3 EVAL, AND NOTHING FAILED

| id | claim | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-NAVCOMP-SHAPE-1 | ⛔⛔ **THE STRATEGIC nav-COMPLIANCE FAMILY HAS BEEN REFUSED ON EVERY refcv3 ARM SINCE THE SIDECAR LANDED — because a manifest key holds a DICT where the reader wanted a PATH.** `refcv3_arm.py:1898` built `"corpus": {"episodes": …, "labels": a.labels, **join}` while `join` (`:1266`) carries its **own** `"labels"` key holding the *provenance block*; **the splat comes last, so the dict overwrites the path**. `nav_compliance.py` then called `os.path.exists(<dict>)` → `TypeError: _path_exists: path should be string, bytes, os.PathLike or integer, not dict`. ⚠️ **The caller wraps that in `except Exception` and converts it to a REFUSAL**, so the family read UNAVAILABLE with a reason nobody parsed while LONGITUDINAL / LATERAL / TACTICAL reported normally — *the eval never failed*. MEASURED on the RL panel: **3/3 strategic nav-compliance rows lost over 4,823 windows / 141 episodes** of compute already paid for; re-MEASURED on a banked `manifest.json`, where `corpus.labels` reads `dict`, the pre-fix expression reproduces the exact `TypeError`, and the fixed resolver returns the real path string | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05, source + a banked manifest) — FIXED in the same turn** | `repo:taniteval/taniteval/nav_compliance.py::resolve_labels_path`; `repo:taniteval/tools/refcv3_arm.py:1898`; pin `repo:stack/tests/test_navcomp_labels_shape.py` (**8 passed**, incl. a deliberate-regression control that asserts the OLD expression really raises); log `…/2026-09-05-refc-rl-readiness/raw/run/t1_analyze_rl.log:43,45,47` |
| D-NAVCOMP-SHAPE-2 | ⭐ **THE FIX RECOVERS THE FAMILY ON DUMPS ALREADY PAID FOR — no re-roll.** `resolve_labels_path` accepts all three manifest generations: the explicit `labels_path` key (written from today), the provenance dict's `.path`, and a bare string; an explicit `--labels` always wins. The writer now publishes the path under `labels_path` and leaves `labels` as the richer provenance block, so the shape is no longer self-contradictory. ⚠️ **This does NOT retract any published strategic number — there were none to retract**; it makes the family readable for the first time, including on the banked refcv4b/refcv3 dumps when the hierarchy panel rolls | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | same pins |

⚠️ **The class, which is the part worth keeping.** A `try/except Exception` that converts a crash into a
polite REFUSAL is the right shape for a *missing input* and the wrong shape for a *type error in our own
code*: it turned a loud failure into a silent absence, inside the one rule that says an eval reporting
fewer than four families is INCOMPLETE. Same family as the trainer log that went stale while the run was
healthy, and the memmap of zeros that exited 0 — **an error path that reports success in the shape the
reader expects.** ⇒ A refusal reason that names a `TypeError` from our own module is a BUG, never a
refusal, and should be re-raised.

### 2026-09-05 — the collision term could not see a car it drove through

| id | claim | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-SWEPT-1 | ⛔ **`rl/rewards.py::_collision` TESTED THE SAMPLED WAYPOINTS, NOT THE PATH BETWEEN THEM** — a candidate whose consecutive waypoints straddle an obstacle was scored **safe**. MEASURED on the banked base fan (240 windows × 128 candidates × 5 steps, `r = 2.0 m`): waypoint spacing **median 6.628 m / p95 13.381 m / max 46.160 m**, so the undetected corridor (`spacing − 2r`) at p95 is **+9.381 m — longer than the vehicle.** On the 8,320 pairs with a lead the point test fires on **9.1827 %** and the swept test on **12.6562 %**: **289 newly-caught straddling candidates (+37.8 % relative)**, and **18 of 65 lead windows (27.7 %)** contained at least one candidate that drove through the lead undetected | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05, instrument tier — ⛔ NOT T0/T1, no arm ranked)** | `…/Deployment & Optimization/Research/2026-09-05-swept-collision/RESULT.md`; `raw/swept_vs_point.json`; `repo:stack/tanitad/rl/rewards.py::segment_point_distance`; pin `repo:stack/tests/test_collision_swept_segment.py` (**11 passed**, incl. a deliberate-regression control asserting the PRE-FIX expression passes the straddling fixture) |
| D-SWEPT-2 | ⚠️ **THE SWEPT FORM IS A STRICT SUPERSET, SO EVERY CONTACT NUMBER BANKED UNDER THE POINT TEST IS A LOWER BOUND — NOT A NUMBER TO BE REGRESSED AGAINST.** `lost = 0` on the real bank and over 5 random seeds: every waypoint is an endpoint of some segment and the clamp includes `t ∈ {0,1}`. ⇒ the RL panel's human contact **0.0000**, refcv3's **0.0007**, and the identically-zero `sel_contact`/`top8_contact` columns are all lower bounds under a weaker predicate. ⛔ **A swept re-read is a DIFFERENT measurement and must be labelled with which predicate produced it; it is not a regression.** ⭐ Same direction for the veto arm: a veto keyed on this predicate was letting straddling candidates through, so its measured feasibility gain is a lower bound too — the fix can only reveal more to veto, never less | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | same pins |
| D-SWEPT-3 | ⭐ **THE TIME-ALIGNED LEAD GUARD SURVIVES THE FIX** — the moving branch is swept in the **RELATIVE frame** (`lead_s − traj_s` against the origin), so the lead's own motion over the step is accounted for. Sweeping the ego path against a **static** lead would have re-introduced `H-RL-THRESH-1` (*a safety term that fires on the demonstration*: holding the lead at its t0 sample flags every competent follower). Pinned by a test that a 2 s-gap follower is NOT flagged, with a same-breath **positive control** — a stopped lead IS flagged — so the branch cannot pass by being inert | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | same pins |

⚠️ **Open, and owed:** the **static**-obstacle branch's fire-rate change is unmeasured — the banked fan
carries `lead5` but no static obstacle set. It becomes readable once the agent-track join is wired
into the batch, and the same before/after belongs in that package.

That is correct for a MISSING INPUT (no labels blob, a pre-sidecar dump) — the four-families rule explicitly permits dropping a family *with its reason* — and wrong for a bug in our own code. ⇒ `DEFECT_EXCEPTIONS = (TypeError, AttributeError, NameError, IndexError, UnboundLocalError, ZeroDivisionError)` now classifies the two: a defect stamps `defect: True` + `defect_type` **on the block** and appends to a top-level **`_defects`** list, so a driver can exit non-zero instead of publishing a family-shaped hole; the reason text itself begins **"⛔ DEFECT (not a refusal)"**. ⚠️ `ValueError` and `KeyError` are deliberately EXCLUDED — they are the honest way a loader says *"this file is not what you said it was"*, and classifying them as defects would destroy the distinction in the other direction | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05, harness-level)** | `repo:taniteval/tools/refcv3_arm.py::DEFECT_EXCEPTIONS`; pin `repo:stack/tests/test_navcomp_labels_shape.py` (**12 passed**) |

⚠️ **Why this belongs beside `D-NAVCOMP-SHAPE-1` rather than under it.** The path bug was one instance;
the *mechanism* was an error path that reports failure **in the shape the reader expects for success**.
Same family as the trainer log that went stale while the run was healthy, the memmap of zeros that
exited 0, and the search tool that reported "no matches" for files it could not open. ⛔ **A `TypeError`
raised inside our own module is never a refusal, and a record that cannot say so will hide the next one.**

## D-REFCV5-PROV — the two silent flags, the run record, the query budget and the digest scope (2026-09-05, Architecture & Inference FlyWheel)

*Implements `Project Steering/Decisions/2026-09-05-mm-decisions.md` **§M17** and **§M18**;
closes `D-AGTJOIN-5` and `D-AGTJOIN-6`. Package:
`TanitAD Research Lab/Architecture & Inference/Research/2026-09-05-refcv5-flag-provenance/`.*

| id | claim | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFCV5-PROV-1 | ⛔⛔ **THE TWO MONOCULAR WEIGHTS NO LONGER NO-OP — THEY REFUSE, AT CONFIG-PIN TIME, BEFORE A MODEL AND BEFORE THE GPU.** `refc_v3_train.py` set `model._rig_camera = None` and never assigned it while `agent_losses` guards both terms on `cam is not None`, so `--agent-w-project 0.2` could be **stamped into `config.json` and compute nothing** — a run record stating a configuration that did not happen, which makes every later comparison between arms unfalsifiable. `_build_rig_camera(cfg, args)` now refuses **four** ways: a non-zero weight with `--agent-rig-camera off`; a camera under `--agents off` (the mirror-image dead flag); `extrinsics` with no file or no quaternion; and a geometry with no **declared** `CanonicalFrame`. ⛔ **There is no third state**: every argv either computes the terms or is refused. ⚠️ Zero weights with no camera are deliberately untouched, so every arm banked so far is bit-unchanged | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** ⇒ **CLOSES `D-AGTJOIN-5`'s first clause** | `…/2026-09-05-refcv5-flag-provenance/RESULT.md` §P1; `repo:stack/scripts/refc_v3_train.py::_build_rig_camera`; `repo:stack/tests/test_refc_v3_agent_provenance.py` (the config that used to run silently must now raise — parametrised over both weights; the converse control must NOT raise) |
| D-REFCV5-PROV-2 | ⭐ **THE ANSWER TO "IS `_rig_camera` SUPPOSED TO BE ASSIGNED?" IS YES — IT IS WIRED, NOT REMOVED.** `--agent-rig-camera {off,nominal,extrinsics}` (+ `--agent-rig-extrinsics`, `--agent-cam-height`) builds a real `tanitad.data.rig_projection.RigCamera`, and with it `loss_project` and `loss_ground` are present, finite, computed over **n > 0** and **add to `total`** — asserted end to end through the trainer's own builder, against a blind run that emits neither term. ⛔ The known-value control beside it: a PERFECT prediction reads the image-plane term **exactly 0.0 over n > 0**, so the non-zero is an error signal and not an instrument offset. ⚠️ **The first draft of that test used a random untrained head and read 0.0 over n = 0** — a legitimate no-information value that would have "passed" as evidence the term ran; `n` is now asserted before the value. ⚠️ The mount pose carries its PROVENANCE (`anchor_meta` pattern): `nominal` stamps `mount_pose: "NOMINAL-no-pitch"` and warns that `ground_range_prior` back-projects through the road plane so a real pitch biases its range DIRECTLY, while `monocular_projection_loss` is a difference of two projections and cancels a common mount error to first order | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** — ⚠️ **wiring tier, not a detection result**: no arm has been trained with either weight non-zero | RESULT.md §P1; `repo:stack/tests/test_refc_v3_agent_provenance.py::test_P1_the_camera_the_TRAINER_builds_makes_the_terms_COMPUTE` |
| D-REFCV5-PROV-3 | ⚠⚠ **`w_agent` AND `w_u0` WERE **NOT** ABSENT FROM `config.json` — M18's third bullet and `D-AGTJOIN-5`'s sibling clause are PARTLY REFUTED, on the banked artifact of the run that raised them.** `…/2026-09-05-agent-join-into-batch/raw/smoke_agt_head_600.config.json` reads `seams.w_agent = 1.0` and `seams.w_u0 = 0.5`; `_seam_stamp` has carried both since commit `3fd2291`. They are absent from the **top level**, and were looked for there. ⭐ **But the escalation was pointing at something real and structural**: the stamp was a HAND-WRITTEN dict, so "every knob reaches the record" held only while someone remembered to add each line — the same defect deferred. ⇒ `agent_knob_dests(parser)` now reads every `--agent-*` / `--w-*` `dest` **off argparse** (17 today), `agent_knob_stamp` writes them all into `config.json["seams"]["agent_knobs"]`, and `assert_knobs_stamped` runs in `train()` **immediately before the write**, so a run whose record cannot state its own weights **refuses to start**. The test asserts **BY VALUE, not by key name**, over the argparse-derived set, with a same-breath control that ≥ 12 knobs were actually probed | **PARTLY REFUTED (the absence claim) + SUPPORTED (the structural fix), MEASURED 2026-09-05** ⇒ **CLOSES `D-AGTJOIN-5`'s sibling clause and CORRECTS mm-decisions M18 bullet 3** | RESULT.md §P2; `…/2026-09-05-agent-join-into-batch/raw/smoke_agt_head_600.config.json`; `repo:stack/scripts/refc_v3_train.py::agent_knob_dests` |
| D-REFCV5-PROV-4 | ⭐ **M18 IMPLEMENTED: A RECORDED HASH NOW CARRIES THE ARTIFACT IT COVERS, AND A CONSUMER THAT FINDS NO DECLARED SCOPE REFUSES.** Re-MEASURED here rather than inherited, on the 136,689,648-byte train join: `md5(compressed .xz) = 24cbdca8c3b23aafc2fb17e6bf99cf76` (= what the sidecar records) and `md5(decompressed .jsonl) = b7c6b5a7da2d942e8be9dc9fc316e9a9` — so a val40-style checker hashes the decompressed stream, gets `b7c6b5a7…`, compares against `24cbdca8…` and **REFUSES a good file**, reproduced exactly. ⛔ **And the obvious repair is MEASURED wrong**: val40's `.xz`-side sidecar names the **`.jsonl`** in `summary.out` and its `xz_of_md5` holds the SAME digest as `summary.md5`, so the extension heuristic fails in the other direction — the module therefore offers **no** inference (pinned by a test asserting no `infer`/`guess` symbol exists). `tanitad/data/join_meta.py` follows `anchor_meta`'s shape rather than inventing a second: builders `declare`/`attach`, consumers `read_digest_scope` **refuse** an undeclared sidecar, `verify` also refuses a digest taken over a DIFFERENT filename, and `backfill` **MEASURES** a legacy digest's scope by hashing both artifacts — refusing when neither matches **and** when both do (an uncompressed `.jsonl` hashes identically under both scopes). Wired as `--agent-join-verify {auto,off}`, stamped as `config.json["agent_join_digest"]` | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05, end to end on the real 137 MB join)** ⇒ **CLOSES `D-AGTJOIN-6`** | RESULT.md §P4; `raw/join_digest_scope_incident.json`, `raw/backfill_train2400.json`; `repo:stack/tanitad/data/join_meta.py` |
| D-REFCV5-PROV-5 | ⭐ **M17 IMPLEMENTED AND ITS COST MEASURED: `--agent-queries` = 100, and THE COST DOES NOT BIND.** Through the shipped path (`build_parser` → `_pin_trainer_cfg` → `RefCV3Model` → `compute_losses_v3`) at the **corpus geometry 256×640** (grid 8×20 = 160 memory tokens), seam `d_model 256 · depth 3 · heads 8`, `n_pad = 94` (the train max, so Hungarian matching is priced at its worst case): step time **3.346 → 3.622 s = +8.2 %**, parameters **22,740,396 → 22,757,804 = +17,408 = 68 queries × 256 d_model exactly = +0.077 %**, peak memory **NO DETECTABLE DIFFERENCE** (RSS delta {492.7, 533.9} MB at N=32 vs {516.1, 487.3} MB at N=100 — ranges fully overlap). ⭐ **Each arm was run TWICE**, because a single timing pair cannot tell a lever from the rig's own run-to-run noise: replicate spread **3.8 %** (N=32) / **1.9 %** (N=100), and the arms' medians **do not overlap** (max 3.4096 < min 3.5878). The null on memory is explained rather than merely reported — the activation tensors that scale with N price analytically at **2.63 → 10.69 MiB** over 3 layers, i.e. **Δ ≈ 8.1 MiB against a ~500 MB step (1.7 %)**, below the probe's noise floor. ⚠️ **SCOPE: dev-box CPU.** The RTX 4060 was busy with another stream throughout (5.0–5.5 GB of 8.2 GB, 100 % util) so no GPU load was added, and `tanitad-refcv3` and Thor were not touched. The **ratio** and the parameter/activation accounting transfer; the **absolute step time does not**. That a GPU step (trunk-dominated) would show ≤ this relative cost is a **PREDICTION, not a measurement** | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05, dev-box CPU; pod-side re-measure ESCALATED)** ⇒ **implements M17; supersedes `D-AGTJOIN-2`'s open "needs a ruling"** | RESULT.md §P3; `raw/query_cost_b4_replicate_cpu.json`, `raw/query_cost_b2_cpu.json`; instrument `…/measure_query_cost.py` |
| D-REFCV5-PROV-6 | ⚠ **TWO LIMITS SHIPPED WITH THE FIX RATHER THAN HIDDEN INSIDE IT.** (a) `AgentSeamConfig.queries` in `tanitad/refs/refc_agents.py` still **defaults to 32** and its docstring still carries the val40 claim — that file belongs to another stream and was not touched, so any consumer constructing `AgentSeamConfig()` directly still gets 32. (b) `agent_losses(cam=…)` takes **ONE camera per call**, so a run declares one mount pose for the whole batch while the corpus has **two rigs** (cy ≈ 543 / cy ≈ 755) and per-clip extrinsics; per-clip cameras need a signature change in the same unowned file. ⇒ `--agent-rig-camera extrinsics` is honest but **coarse**, and that is stated in the stamp rather than assumed away | **OPEN — escalated with named owners, not fixed here** | RESULT.md ESCALATIONS 1–2 |
| D-REFCV5-PROV-7 | ⚠ **A GEOMETRY WITH NO DECLARED `CanonicalFrame` IS REFUSED, AND THE 64×64 TINY RIG IS ONE.** A frame is **not its pixel count** — it carries `f_ref` **and the projection** — and this corpus is **cylindrical** (column linear in azimuth), where the pinhole formula reads **92.6°** for a **120°** camera and looks entirely plausible. Declared: `(256,640)`, `(176,624)`, `(128,576)`, all `f_ref = 305.5774907364391`, `projection = "cylindrical"`. ⚠️ **Consequence stated rather than hidden: the monocular terms cannot run on the tiny rig**, which is the correct outcome for a 64 px grey crop with no honest `f_ref`, and is why the "the terms compute" test drives the loss at 256×640 directly | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | RESULT.md §P1; `repo:stack/scripts/refc_v3_train.py::_agent_cam_frames` |

⭐ **The through-line across all four fixes is one rule with the object swapped each time** — the
family `CLAUDE.md` already catalogues under `df` / Thor `free` / `step_s` / anchor-units: **a value is
inadmissible without the scope it is true in.** A WEIGHT without the term it weights (P1), a run
RECORD without the knob it trained at (P2), a query BUDGET without the corpus its density was
measured on (P3), a DIGEST without the artifact it covers (P4). In every one the failure wore the
costume of a working configuration.

## refav1 goal margin — the vocabulary is the gate (Architecture & Inference FlyWheel, 2026-09-05)

The corpus curves at **R 100–1000 m** (abs `gt_kappa` p50 **0.00085** = R 1176 m; p90 0.01445 = R 69 m). MEASURED by scoring every token's canonical curvature profile against the window's constant GT curvature (`tools/vocab_fit.py`, 282 windows / 141 episodes, ckpt 21,109): on **90.15 %** of GT-turn windows (abs κ > 1e-3) **`LANE_KEEP` IS THE VOCABULARY-OPTIMAL TOKEN** — no available token is closer to the road. ### 2026-09-05 — MASTER MIND DECISION on refav1's lateral goal vocabulary (M15 / M16)

| id | decision | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-MM-VOCAB-1 | ⭐ **APPROVED: the lateral goal vocabulary gets THREE sustained curvature magnitudes at the measured quantiles.** With an ORACLE token chooser (so each row bounds what ANY head could reach on that vocabulary): shipped `L=1, κ=0.08` → RMSE **0.01015**, medAE-on-turns **0.01551**, expressible **0.3866**; `L=3` → RMSE **0.01032**, medAE **0.00421**, expressible **1.0000**. ⇒ **100 % of real turns expressible instead of 38.7 %, median curvature error on a turn 3.7× lower, at the same RMSE (+1.7 %).** Approved only because BOTH known-value controls read exactly right: the CONTINUOUS ceiling **exactly 0.0** and the `LANE_KEEP`-only floor **exactly the road RMS curvature to 1e-12** | **DECIDED (Master Mind, 2026-09-05)** | `Decisions/2026-09-05-mm-decisions.md` §M15 |
| D-MM-VOCAB-2 | ⭐⭐ **BINDING — FOR VOCABULARY DESIGN THE DECISION METRIC IS `medAE-on-turns`, NOT RMSE.** MEASURED: the two **rank the designs differently**, and RMSE picks the vocabulary we already know is broken — `κ=0.08` has the **best** single-magnitude RMSE and nearly the **worst** medAE on turns, because RMSE is dominated by the **rare sharp** turn (which 0.08 serves) while the **median** turn is gentle (which it serves barely better than doing nothing). RMSE may be reported; it may not decide. ⚠️ **Same family as `CLAUDE.md`'s two estimator rules**, object swapped again: the statistic is computed CORRECTLY and **answers a narrower question than the claim hung on it**. A summary statistic dominated by the tail cannot adjudicate a median failure mode | **DECIDED (Master Mind, 2026-09-05)** | §M15 |
| D-MM-VOCAB-3 | ⛔ **THE refav1 DECISION-RULE PROGRAMME IS CLOSED — MEASURED WORSE.** A/B at **T1** (14 episodes / 28 windows, `ccos` + Stage-B weights, step 21,109): `ADE` **1.6098 → 2.5025**, `FDE` +1.8712, cross-track +0.8232, heading **+9.07°**; **every family degrades EXCEPT longitudinal speed at −0.005** — and that unchanged channel **is the control**, because a lateral-only bias should leave exactly it alone, so the effect is located where the intervention is. The lever worked as designed (constant-velocity share 0.214 → 0.000): **it turned more, and turning more made it worse**, which is what `D-MM-VOCAB-1`'s table predicts. ⛔ `lat_logit_bias` remains a parameterised INSTRUMENT (zero-bias bit-identical to no-flag, pinned) and must not be shipped as a fix | **DECIDED (Master Mind, 2026-09-05)** | §M16 |
| D-MM-VOCAB-4 | ⭐ **GATE 1 IS LARGELY OPEN WHERE IT MATTERS — the head is not the binding constraint.** Dense stride-2 panel, **4,786 windows (17×)**, forward pass verified (banked `lat_logits` reproduced at max abs diff **0.0**, argmax 282/282): at |gt_κ| > 4e-2 — **R 25 m, the MEASURED vocabulary crossover, not a threshold picked for a good number** — the **shipped** head already decodes a curvature-carrying token on **74.4 %** of windows at **12.0 %** false-turn, **AUC 0.8806**, shuffled-label AUC flat at **0.498–0.504**. The measured crossover **0.04101** matches the analytic `GOAL_KAPPA_TURN/2 = 0.040`, and ⭐ **the agreement TIGHTENS with more data — which is what a real quantity does and an artefact does not.** ⇒ the "20.5 % turn recall" that launched the decision-rule programme is a statistic about **R 1000 m** curves the vocabulary cannot express anyway | **SUPPORTED (MEASURED 2026-09-05)** | §M16 |

⚠️ **Two things these do NOT settle, and they travel with every refav1 claim from here.**
**(1) PARITY** — changing `canonical_controls` changes the **ACTION SPACE**, so every banked refav1
number is under the OLD vocabulary; new arms must carry the vocabulary version explicitly and a
cross-vocabulary comparison is inadmissible without saying so.
**(2)** ⛔ **Both A/B arms sit 3.6–5.9× BELOW the trivial floors on turning windows.** The
vocabulary fix is **necessary and not shown to be sufficient**; the floor gap is a separate open
item that no vocabulary change is entitled to claim.

| id | claim | status | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-REFAV1-VOCAB-DESIGN | ⭐⭐ **THE FIX, PRICED AT ZERO GPU: THREE SUSTAINED CURVATURE LEVELS MAKE 100 % OF REAL TURNS EXPRESSIBLE INSTEAD OF 38.7 %, AT THE SAME RMSE.** Each candidate vocabulary is given an ORACLE token chooser and scored by the residual between its best available sustained curvature (× the `TURN` profile's 0.667 duty cycle) and the road's constant curvature (`tools/vocab_design.py`, 4520 windows / 141 episodes after excluding 266 near-stationary, 644 real turns at R ≤ 100 m). **`LANE_KEEP` only (FLOOR)** RMSE 0.01785 / medAE-on-turns 0.01942 / expressible 0.0000 · **κ=0.02 (R 50 m)** 0.01460 / **0.00609** / **1.0000** · ⛔ **κ=0.08 SHIPPED** 0.01015 / **0.01551** / **0.3866** · ⭐ **L=3 at corpus quantiles** 0.01032 / **0.00421** / **1.0000** · **CONTINUOUS (CEILING)** 0 / 0 / 1.0. ⚠️ **THE TRAP INSIDE THE TABLE: RMSE AND medAE RANK THE DESIGNS DIFFERENTLY** — κ=0.08 has the BEST single-magnitude RMSE and nearly the WORST medAE on turns, because RMSE is dominated by the rare sharp turn while the median turn is gentle and 0.08 serves it barely better than doing nothing. Quoting either statistic alone gets the design decision wrong; that is the argument for more levels. **The shipped vocabulary recovers only ~20 % of the available curvature error on a typical turn.** ⛔ **Controls:** the CONTINUOUS ceiling reads **exactly 0.0**; the `L=0` floor reads **exactly** the road's own RMS curvature to 1e-12; the SHIPPED row is asserted present, not eyeballed | **SUPPORTED — MEASURED 2026-09-05, zero GPU.** ⚠️ It is a VOCABULARY-ADEQUACY BOUND under an ORACLE chooser: it bounds the system from above and does **not** promise the head selects well (head selection: AUC 0.8806, recall 0.744 @ 0.120 at the crossover) and predicts **no** ADE. ⛔ **And it does not touch gate 2** — under the shipped `cos` a decoded turn is refused on 38/38 windows, so a finer vocabulary without `ccos` changes nothing that reaches the wheels; the two must land together | `…/2026-09-05-refav1-goal-margin/ESCALATION.md`; `raw/vocab_design.json` |
| D-REFAV1-KAPPA-KNOB | ⭐ **THE VOCABULARY KNOB IS IMPLEMENTED, FLAG-GATED AND PARITY-PINNED.** `--goal-kappa-turn` / `plan(goal_kappa_turn=...)` threads a chosen SUSTAINED curvature through `canonical_controls(kappa_turn=)` → `_imagine_tactical_goal` → `imagined_goal` → `plan`, stamped as `res.goal_kappa_turn` and banked in `manifest["goal_rule"]`, with the same stale-stack verify-gate `--lat-logit-bias` uses (a stack predating the parameter would ignore the flag and bank a shipped-vocabulary arm under a finer-vocabulary name) and a range check against `GOAL_KAPPA_MAX`. ⛔ **Parity PINNED, not promised** (`stack/tests/test_refa_v1_goal_kappa.py`, 7 tests): absent ≡ `None` ≡ explicit `GOAL_KAPPA_TURN`, bit-identical for **all 8** lateral tokens; **only** `TURN_*` is affected (S-curve tokens size from `v_ref` and must be untouched, else this would silently re-scale lane changes); the LONGITUDINAL column is identical for every (lat, lon) pair; bit-identical through the real `plan()`; and `test_f` **forces** a TURN decode with `lat_logit_bias` and requires the goal controls to carry the asked-for curvature — **without it every parity assertion would hold vacuously if no seed ever decoded a turn**. Suite **285 passed, 1 skipped** (278 before) | **DELIVERED (instrument) — plan-level effect OPEN.** ⛔ No ADE or four-family number is claimed; the lookup shortcut does not hold on turn windows so it must be re-planned. Arms were on the dev-box 4060 at hand-off | code `stack/tanitad/refs/refa_v1.py`, `taniteval/tools/refav1_arm.py`, `stack/tests/test_refa_v1_goal_kappa.py`; `…/2026-09-05-refav1-goal-margin/RESULT.md` |
| D-GTKAPPA-LOWSPEED | ⚠️ **`gt_kappa` IS NOT A CURVATURE BELOW ~1 m/s, AND 13 WINDOWS CARRIED THE ENTIRE RMS.** It is `yaw_rate.mean / speed.mean.clamp_min(0.5)`, so at low speed it divides by the FLOOR. MEASURED on 4786 windows: **13 (0.27 %)** read `|κ| > 0.2`, **92.3 % of them at v0 < 1 m/s** (median v0 **0.366 m/s** vs **10.26** elsewhere), max **3.565 1/m = R 0.28 m** — narrower than the car. `RMS|κ|` **0.15827** raw against **0.02192** clipped to `GOAL_KAPPA_MAX`. My first vocabulary-pricing table was computed on the raw figure and was meaningless. ⇒ **any `yaw_rate/speed` curvature metric in the programme needs a speed floor on the WINDOW, not just inside the ratio.** ⛔ **Blast radius CHECKED, not assumed:** every other result in the package is rank-based (`vocab_fit` fractions, `threshold_sweep` AUC) or already clipped (`kappa_head` ±0.2, `soft_kappa` ±0.08), so only that one table was affected | **SUPPORTED — MEASURED 2026-09-05** ⇒ instrument fixed (`--min-v0 1.0`, target clipped, both counts printed) | `…/2026-09-05-refav1-goal-margin/ESCALATION.md` §0; `raw/vocab_design.json` |
| D-ARGPARSE-PCT | ⛔ **A BARE `%` IN AN argparse `help=` STRING KILLS `--help`, AND THE RUN PATH STAYS FINE — WHICH IS WHY IT SHIPS.** MEASURED 2026-09-05: `--goal-kappa-turn`'s help quoted *"… on 90.6 % of GT-turn windows …"*; `HelpFormatter._expand_help` `%`-formats every help string, `"% o"` is read as the `%o` conversion, and `--help` died with `TypeError: %o format: an integer is required, not dict`. ⭐ **WHAT CAUGHT IT WAS A SAME-BREATH CONTROL, NOT THE CHECK:** grepping the help output for the new flag returned **0**, which reads exactly like *"my grep is wrong"* — until the control grep for the SIBLING flag (`--lat-logit-bias`, always present) **also** returned 0. Two zeros, one of which had to be non-zero, is what turned "my grep is wrong" into "the whole help output is missing". Pinned at the CLASS level by `stack/tests/test_argparse_help_percent.py`: a static `ast` scan (never imports the file, so no missing dependency can defeat it) that REFUSES any file it parsed 0 help strings from, plus a control asserting the detector fires on the bad form and stays silent on `%%` and `%(default)s` | **SUPPORTED — MEASURED and FIXED 2026-09-05** | code `taniteval/tools/refav1_arm.py`, `stack/tests/test_argparse_help_percent.py` |
